### [1] [Physics-Informed Functional Tucker Method with RKHS Factors for Sparse Spatiotemporal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03717)
*Zhengyang Liu,Jianguo Huang,Junxi Hu,Yucheng Mi,Haojie Cai,Wenxuan Shen,Zhikun Zhang,Renfeng Peng,Guangtao Zhang,Zhiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysFTM 提出一种物理信息函数 Tucker 模型，利用 RKHS 表示低秩张量因子并以 PDE 残差约束非观测区域，实现稀疏传感器与缺失帧条件下的连续时空场重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定稀疏传感器和帧缺失会使物理场重建成为不适定逆问题：模型可能拟合已观测点，却在非传感器区域和缺失时间帧上产生不可靠结构或预测，因此需要融合低秩先验与物理方程信息来补充观测不足。

Method: PhysFTM 将 Tucker 分解中的模式因子置于再生核希尔伯特空间中，得到连续的函数型张量表示；同时通过有限差分在离传感器配置点计算 PDE 残差，把物理约束引入无直接观测区域。对于支持帧，采用交替最小化优化核心张量与 RKHS 因子；对于缺失帧，则在 Tucker 核心空间构建连续时间轨迹，并由学习到的空间 RKHS-FTM 表示解码完整时空场。

Result: 在 Allen-Cahn、Navier-Stokes、三维热方程以及三维 Copernicus 海洋再分析数据上的实验表明，PhysFTM 能在两类缺失帧协议下实现较准确的二维和三维物理场重建。

Conclusion: PhysFTM 将函数型低秩张量表示与物理方程约束结合，提高了稀疏观测和缺失帧场景下时空重建的准确性与物理一致性。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical dynamics from fixed sparse sensors and missing frames is a fundamental challenge in scientific research. Sparsity leaves large regions of the field unconstrained, making the inverse problem ill-posed: a model may fit the observed sensors while producing inaccurate off-sensor structures or unreliable predictions between support frames. To this end, we propose PhysFTM---a physics-informed functional Tucker model that parameterizes the Tucker mode factors in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs) and incorporates PDE constraints through finite-difference residuals evaluated at off-sensor collocation points. These residuals provide additional physical information in regions without direct observational supervision. PhysFTM first reconstructs continuous spatial fields at support frames via an alternating minimization scheme, optimizing the Tucker core tensor and the RKHS-based factors separately. To enable missing-frame reconstruction, PhysFTM constructs a continuous trajectory in Tucker-core space, which is decoded by the learned spatial RKHS-FTM representation to recover the full spatiotemporal field. Experiments on Allen--Cahn, Navier--Stokes, three-dimensional Heat equations, and a three-dimensional gridded Copernicus ocean reanalysis demonstrate accurate 2D/3D reconstruction under two missing-frame protocols. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Proposals: Region-Consistent Open-Set Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03741)
*Jiehui Tang,Yunlong Yu,Zhao Wang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OCSI-Net，将开放集目标检测（OSOD）从“逐个 proposal 独立判断”重构为“以对象为中心的区域级语义推断”，通过聚合相关 proposal 并建模几何感知的不确定性，缓解稠密 proposal 下同一物体出现矛盾 known/unknown 预测的问题，取得多基准 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稠密 proposal 机制下，OSOD 面临基本局限：同一真实物体往往对应多个高度重叠的 proposal，而现有方法对每个 proposal 独立进行开放集推理，忽略相关 proposal 之间的共享语义，导致同一物体区域出现互相矛盾的已知/未知预测，检测结果碎片化、开放集决策不稳定。

Method: 作者提出 OCSI-Net 统一框架，核心思想是把 OSOD 看作 object-centric semantic inference。具体包括：1）将相关 proposal 聚合为结构化 region clusters；2）从聚合后的语义证据推断区域级语义，使同一物体区域内的 open-set 预测保持一致；3）引入 geometry-aware uncertainty 机制，在开放集判定边界附近稳定优化过程，降低边界处决策波动。

Result: 在多个开放集目标检测基准上进行了充分实验。结果显示 OCSI-Net 持续达到 state-of-the-art，相比次优方法，在已知与未知检测性能的调和平均上平均提升 6.83%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，开放集目标检测的关键瓶颈之一在于 proposal 层面的语义不一致。通过以对象区域为单位聚合语义证据并进行区域级推断，同时结合几何感知不确定性稳定边界决策，可以显著提升 open-set 检测的一致性与稳定性。

Abstract: Open-set object detection (OSOD) suffers from a fundamental limitation under dense proposal mechanisms: highly overlapping proposals associated with the same object are often assigned contradictory known/unknown predictions. Existing methods perform open-set reasoning independently for each proposal, while ignoring the shared semantics among correlated proposals, resulting in fragmented detections and unstable open-set decisions. In this paper, we reformulate OSOD as an object-centric semantic inference problem, where correlated proposals jointly support a coherent region-level semantic decision. Based on this perspective, we propose OCSI-Net, a unified framework that combines object-centric semantic consistency with geometry-aware uncertainty stabilization. Specifically, OCSI-Net aggregates correlated proposals into structured region clusters and infers region-level semantics from aggregated semantic evidence, thereby enforcing coherent open-set predictions within the same object region. Furthermore, a geometry-aware uncertainty mechanism is introduced to stabilize optimization near open-set transition boundaries. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that OCSI-Net consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the harmonic mean of known and unknown detection by an average of 6.83\% over the second-best method.

</details>


### [3] [Geometric Refinement and Distributional Alignment for Sim2Real Roadside LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03936)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍其在 UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge LUMPI 赛道中的方案：不提出新架构，而是基于 PointPillars，通过几何精修、后处理纠错和真实感指标解析获得提升，并提出利用可分解的 per-class AP 在提交前预测总分的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该赛道要求仅用合成数据训练 3D 点云检测器，并在未见过的真实路侧 LiDAR 上评估，同时合成点云还需被评分为分布真实感。作者关注如何在无新网络架构的情况下提升检测与真实感分数，并建立可复现、可预测的实验方法。

Method: 以 PointPillars 为基线：1）诊断出小目标失效主要是定位问题而非检测问题，设计无参数几何精修；2）审计现有后处理规则，发现并修复破坏有效预测的规则；3）将真实感指标从黑箱转为解析分析，发现其中一项可由九个汇总统计量闭式计算；4）利用 mean AP 可分解为独立类别项的性质，在同一次提交中开展互相对照实验，并基于已测组件预先预测提交分数。

Result: 最终组合得分为 0.4793，其中检测 mAP 为 0.1333，真实感分数为 0.9126。几何精修使 pedestrian AP 相对提升 86%；后处理修复使 Van 类检测提升最大，相对提升 335%。作者称在提交前已可将最终分数预测到小数点后四位。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献并非模型结构创新，而是诊断式改进、后处理纠错、指标解析以及可迁移的实验方法论。其强调数据质量、评测指标分解和可预测实验设计在 Sim2Real 点云检测挑战中的价值。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.4793 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.1333 and a realism score of 0.9126. Our contribution is not a new architecture. Working from a PointPillars baseline, we obtained most of our gains from three sources. First, a diagnosis that small object failure in roadside LiDAR is a localisation problem rather than a detection problem, which led to a parameter free geometric refinement worth 86 percent relative on pedestrian AP. Second, an audit of our own post processing which revealed that a shipped rule was destroying valid predictions, the repair of which produced our single largest detection gain at 335 percent relative on the Van class. Third, treating the realism metrics analytically rather than as a black box, which showed that one of the four components is a closed form function of nine summary statistics. We also report an experimental methodology that we believe is the more transferable result. Because mean AP decomposes into independent per class terms, a single submission can carry several mutually controlled experiments, and a submission assembled entirely from previously measured components has a score that is known before it is sent. We predicted our final score to four decimal places prior to submission. We report negative results at equal length, and give a full account of data provenance and compliance in Section 8, including a full account of every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [4] [FlowBench: Counterfactual Imagination Training for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03937)
*Yifan Liu,Baihan Zhang,Khai Loong Aw,Klemen Kotar,Daniel L.K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出“反事实空间想象”（Counterfactual Spatial Imagination, CSI）任务与 FlowBench 数据集，用于训练和评估视觉语言模型（VLM）与世界模型在假设运动条件下想象场景变化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够基于假设动作想象场景如何变化，这一能力对空间推理和机器人规划至关重要；但现有视觉语言模型与世界模型对此类“未观察但可推断”的场景变化缺乏系统研究，模型是否能从已观察结果迁移到反事实想象尚不清楚。

Method: 作者将这一能力形式化为 CSI 任务，并构建 FlowBench，包含 42.8 万个训练样本，覆盖仿真与真实世界场景、家庭环境与桌面操作，以及多种问题类型。该基准依赖可扩展的完全自动化数据生成流程，为每个假设运动配对空间问题；在仿真环境中还提供精确的结果帧和光流标签。随后，作者用该数据评测 VLM 与世界模型，并进行微调与训练分析。

Result: 实验表明，专有 VLM 存在明显的“想象鸿沟”：在需要推断假设运动结果时性能显著下降，而开源模型接近随机水平。对 Qwen3-VL-8B 在 FlowBench-Sim-Train 上微调后，其在仿真场景中的准确率提升 29.5 个百分点，在真实图像上提升 16.9 个百分点，体现出较强的 sim-to-real 迁移能力。对于世界模型，在 FlowBench-Sim 上训练可提升四类模型家族的文本到光流预测性能，其中 Psi-0.6 的准确率从 52.1% 提升到 61.7%。

Conclusion: FlowBench 为训练和评估模型的反事实空间想象能力提供了统一资源，实验揭示了现有 VLM 与世界模型在该能力上的不足，同时说明大规模仿真训练和光流监督能够有效提升模型对假设运动下场景变化的预测能力。

Abstract: Humans imagine scene changes under hypothetical motions, an ability central to spatial reasoning and robotic planning. However, this capability remains underexplored in vision-language models and world models. We formalize this capability as Counterfactual Spatial Imagination (CSI) and introduce FlowBench, a resource for training and evaluating both, with 428K training examples spanning simulated and real-world scenes, household environments and tabletop manipulation, and diverse question types. A scalable, fully automatic pipeline pairs each hypothetical motion with spatial questions and, in simulation, exact outcome frames and optical flow. For VLMs, proprietary models exhibit an imagination gap, performing worse when motion outcomes must be inferred than when observed, and open-source models perform near chance; fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B on FlowBench-Sim-Train raises its accuracy by 29.5 percentage points in simulation and 16.9 on real images, a strong sim-to-real transfer. For world models, training on FlowBench-Sim improves text-to-flow prediction for all four model families we test, raising the accuracy of Psi-0.6 from 52.1% to 61.7%. FlowBench thus serves as a training and evaluation resource for both. Code and datasets are released at this https URL.

</details>


### [5] [Coordinate Frame Recovery, Per Class NMS and Null Return Modelling for Sim2Real V2X LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03940)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇摘要介绍了一个面向 UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge 中 V2X-Real 赛道的方法：检测器仅用合成数据训练，却要在未见过的真实路侧 Ouster OS1-64 LiDAR 数据上评估，同时合成点云还需接受分布真实性评分。作者发现，最终成绩提升主要来自三个“诊断性”修正，而非复杂架构改动：坐标帧错配、按类别设置非极大值抑制阈值，以及真实参考点云中大量 (0,0,0) 空返回未被建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Sim2Real 路侧 LiDAR 检测任务中，模型只能用合成数据训练，却要在真实 LiDAR 数据上评估，并同时对合成点云的真实感进行评分。作者试图分析并解决该赛道中影响检测性能与真实性评分的关键问题，尤其是为什么看似合理的模型仍会出现检测指标和分布评分异常，从而为真实部署中的数据格式差异、评估协议和坐标系问题提供经验。

Method: 方法上，作者首先基于合成 LiDAR 数据训练 3D 目标检测器，并在未参与训练的真实路侧 Ouster OS1-64 LiDAR 数据上评估检测性能；同时，对合成点云与真实参考点云之间的分布一致性进行真实性评分。随后，作者没有优先追求复杂模型结构，而是对评分偏低的原因进行系统性诊断，重点检查三类因素：第一，预测框与评估真值之间的坐标帧是否一致；第二，非极大值抑制是否对不同类别尤其行人产生了过度抑制；第三，真实参考点云中空返回被编码为字面量 (0, 0, 0)，而合成点云中缺少该特征，是否影响 Earth Mover's Distance 的真实性计算。

Result: 最终提交得分为 0.7457，其中检测 mAP 为 0.4382，真实性评分为 0.9251。作者报告，仅修复坐标帧错配就将成绩从 0.3520 提升到 0.5147；将统一 NMS 阈值改为按类别处理，尤其是避免对行人群体产生过度抑制，使总体 mAP 从 0.0751 提升到 0.4295；此外，真实参考点云约 31% 是空返回并编码为 (0, 0, 0)，未被建模时会使 EMD 真实性指标接近下限，加入该格式特征后评分显著改善。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心结论是，在 Sim2Real LiDAR 检测挑战中，性能瓶颈往往不来自模型结构本身，而来自训练、预测和评估链路中的细节问题，包括坐标系转换、后处理策略以及数据格式差异。对坐标帧、类别化 NMS 和空返回编码进行诊断与修正，能够显著提升检测指标和真实性评分。摘要同时强调报告负结果、数据来源与合规性，并追溯所有影响最终分数的机制。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the V2X-Real track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR from an Ouster OS1-64 unit, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.7457 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.4382 and a realism score of 0.9251. Three findings account for most of our result, and all three were diagnostic rather than architectural. First, a coordinate frame mismatch between our predictions and the evaluation ground truth was suppressing detection AP entirely; recovering it took our score from 0.3520 to 0.5147 in a single submission. Second, a single global non maximum suppression threshold was destroying genuine pedestrian detections, because pedestrians occur in groups and a 0.3 IoU threshold applied to a 0.7 m box suppresses distinct neighbouring people; correcting this per class raised overall mAP from 0.0751 to 0.4295. Third, approximately 31 percent of the real reference point cloud consists of null returns encoded as the literal point (0, 0, 0), a format feature entirely absent from our synthetic clouds, which pinned Earth Mover's Distance near its floor until we modelled it. Negative results are reported at equal length, and Section 9 gives a full account of data provenance and compliance. That section also accounts for every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [6] [Camera Radar LiDAR Fusion via Point-Cloud Projection for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03942)
*Kavin Chandrasekaran,Sorin Grigorescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自动驾驶的早期传感器融合架构，将相机图像与HD雷达和LiDAR的透视点云进行融合，在RADIal数据集上实现车辆检测与可行驶区域分割，并优于现有单模态及融合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一传感器难以兼顾全天候鲁棒性、高精度测距与丰富视觉语义：相机在低光照和恶劣天气下性能下降，HD雷达可靠但视觉语义不足，LiDAR精度高但成本较高。因此需要融合互补信息，同时降低计算复杂度并保证精度。

Method: 采用早期融合策略，将相机图像、HD雷达和LiDAR产生的透视视图点云数据在前视图层面进行组合，构建一个同时完成车辆检测和自由可行驶空间分割的多任务感知模型，并在RADIal数据集上进行训练与验证。

Result: 实验表明，将点云数据与相机图像在前视图中融合后，模型性能超过当前最新的单传感器模型和融合模型，验证了多传感器互补融合对自动驾驶感知任务的有效性。

Conclusion: 将相机与HD雷达、LiDAR的透视点云进行早期融合，可在复杂环境下提升自动驾驶感知的鲁棒性和准确性，是一种兼顾检测与分割性能的有效多传感器融合方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles require robust perception systems to operate safely under diverse environmental conditions. While cameras provide high-resolution visual data, their performance significantly degrades in low-light and adverse weather. High Definition (HD) radar offers superior reliability and angular resolution under such conditions but lacks rich visual feature recognition. LiDAR, though expensive, delivers highly precise distance measurements and dense point clouds. Each sensor exhibits unique trade-offs, necessitating the development of advanced sensor fusion algorithms to harness complementary strengths while minimizing computational complexity and maintaining accuracy. In this study, the proposed fusion architecture performs early fusion by combining camera images with perspective-view point cloud data from HD radar and LiDAR sensors. The proposed model is trained to detect vehicles and segment free drivable space simultaneously. Experiments are performed on the RADIal dataset where we demonstrate that fusing point cloud data with camera images in front-view surpasses the performances of the most recent standalone and fusion models.

</details>


### [7] [Do We Still Need Rotation-Invariant Architectures? A Statistical Robustness Benchmark for Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03952)
*Abdulla Nasir Chowdhury,Gulpi Qorik Oktagalu Pratamasunu,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过基准测试发现，在点云上显式内建旋转不变性的模型对姿态扰动鲁棒性最强，但往往对噪声更脆弱；随后提出“不变性损失”训练作为替代/补充方案，发现其可靠提升目标扰动鲁棒性，并在匹配训练预算下使普通骨干与旋转不变架构在未见腐败上接近，而RIConv++在种子间更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大量构建旋转不变的点云架构，但其鲁棒性优势是否内在、是否以噪声鲁棒性为代价尚不明确。论文试图在统一基准和确认性统计检验下系统比较这些架构，并检验“如何获得不变性”是否比“是否不变性”更关键。

Method: 作者在5个领域基准测试10种点云架构（包括PointNet/PointNet++、DGCNN、RIConv系列、PointMLP、PointTransformer及等变VN模型），用确认性假设检验分析姿态、噪声、遮挡鲁棒性；进一步在不改变模型结构的前提下加入嵌入层不变性损失，使用3个训练种子重训所有模型，并在独立设计的ModelNet40-C上评估；同时通过扩大旋转增广范围比较普通与RI模型。

Result: 三种旋转不变架构在姿态鲁棒性上最优，但在4/5领域噪声鲁棒性排名最低；加入不变性损失可针对其扰动目标提升对应腐败族鲁棒性，尤其在遮挡和lidar上有明显增益。匹配训练预算下，最佳普通骨干与最佳RI模型在未见腐败上差距在2.5个百分点内；不变性训练的RIConv++为种子最稳定的顶级模型。拓宽旋转增广后，普通模型只出现与角度无关的近似恒定精度损失。

Conclusion: 旋转不变性的鲁棒性收益并非完全由“内建不变性”本身独占；通过简单的不变性损失训练可获得窄而可靠的迁移性增强，并能在公平预算下与专门RI架构竞争。研究同时指出普通模型与RI模型各有鲁棒性权衡，并发布了可复现的基准与分析工具包。

Abstract: A substantial line of work builds rotation invariance directly into point cloud architectures. We benchmark 10 architectures (PointNet, PointNet++, DGCNN, RIConv, RIConv++, RISurConv, PointMLP, PointTransformer, and the equivariant VN-PointNet and VN-DGCNN) across 5 domains under pose, noise, and occlusion, and analyze the results with confirmatory hypothesis tests rather than point estimates. The three rotation-invariant (RI) architectures achieve the best pose robustness but rank among the lowest on noise robustness in 4 of 5 domains. We ask whether this cost is intrinsic to invariance or specific to how invariance is obtained. We retrain all 10 architectures unchanged, with a simple invariance loss on the embedding and three training seeds each, and evaluate all models on the independently designed ModelNet40-C. Invariance training transfers narrowly but reliably. It improves exactly the corruption families its perturbations target wherever the baseline has headroom (mean occlusion +10.5, lidar +13.4 percentage points of accuracy). At matched training budget the best standard backbone and the best RI architecture end on par on unseen corruptions (within 2.5 percentage points in every seed), and invariance-trained RIConv++ is the most seed-stable top model. Once rotation augmentation is widened, standard backbones concede only a flat, angle-independent accuracy offset under full-sphere rotation. We release the benchmark along with a package for analyzing point cloud robustness and reproducing our results.

</details>


### [8] [Compression Forcing: Hierarchical History Tokenization Enables Flexible World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04028)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“Compression Forcing（CF）”框架，将世界模型中的历史压缩与未来预测目标对齐，通过动态感知tokenization和分层历史引导提升预测保真度、物理真实感和下游决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多沿用视频生成的tokenization和conditioning机制，其目标偏向感知真实而非预测有用性。因此在视觉干扰强或背景信息与任务无关的环境中，模型难以高效压缩和利用历史中的关键动态信息，限制世界模型效果。

Method: Compression Forcing 是一个通用框架，核心包括：Dynamics-Aware Tokenization（DAT），将历史观测压缩为按动态显著性递减排序的token序列；以及 Hierarchical History Guidance（HG-H），基于diffusion backbone，在推理时选择性强调动态显著信息，使历史表征更服务于未来预测。

Result: 在多种环境中的实验表明，该方法能够提升预测保真度、物理真实性和下游决策性能，说明其在视觉噪声和无关背景占主导的场景中仍能有效建模世界动态。

Conclusion: Compression Forcing 通过将历史压缩机制与预测目标显式对齐，为世界建模提供了一种灵活且有效的方案，相比以感知真实为中心的视频生成范式更适合需要准确预测和决策支持的世界模型。

Abstract: Effective world modeling requires compressing salient history information that facilitates future prediction. However, most existing approaches inherit tokenization and conditioning mechanisms from video generation, where objectives are centered around perceptual realism rather than predictive utility. This discrepancy limits the world model's effectiveness, especially in environments dominated by visual nuisances or task-irrelevant background details. To this end, we introduce Compression Forcing (CF), a general framework that aligns history compression with the predictive objective of world modeling. Compression Forcing features Dynamics-Aware Tokenization (DAT), a novel tokenization method that compresses history observations into a sequence ordered by decreasing dynamics saliency. Built on a diffusion backbone, this structure enables Hierarchical History Guidance (HG-H), allowing selective amplification of dynamics-salient information during inference. Extensive experiments across various environments demonstrate improved prediction fidelity, physical realism, and downstream decision-making performance, establishing Compression Forcing as a highly flexible and effective approach for world modeling.

</details>


### [9] [Do Pretrained Backbones Transfer to Physical Sensors? A Systematic Study of Multimodal Initialization Across the Sensor Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04034)
*Fabio Montello,Ronja Güldenring,Lucas Mogensen Sandby,Christoffer Koo Øhrstrøm,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察多传感器自动驾驶视觉中“哪种预训练权重迁移效果最好”以及“域内预训练能否优于现成预训练主干”两个问题，并发现 MultiMAE 本身就是很强的开箱即用基线，针对稀疏模态和稠密重建的改进可带来稳定但较小的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多传感器计算机视觉中，从预训练主干迁移学习已成为常规做法，但仍有两个关键问题尚未明确：第一，不同预训练权重在多传感器设置下的迁移能力如何比较；第二，基于目标领域数据进行的域内预训练是否能显著优于 ViT、MAE、DINO、MultiMAE 等现成预训练方案。现有 MultiMAE 等方法主要依赖稠密且由模型生成的模态，而真实世界自动驾驶数据通常来自多种物理传感器，并在聚合公开数据集时天然存在模态稀疏问题，因此这些方法的实际适用性和鲁棒性仍不清楚。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含 2.82M 张图像的多传感器自动驾驶数据集合，覆盖 RGB、LWIR、NIR、Event 等物理硬件模态，以及 Stereo Depth 和 Segmentation 等模型生成模态。在此基础上，作者采用 MultiMAE 思路进行域内预训练，并针对真实多模态数据中的模态稀疏问题进行改造：调整 MultiMAE 的损失函数以适配稀疏样本；研究通过强制 encoder bottleneck 进行模态对齐的效果；引入面向稠密重建的额外损失，包括 SSIM 和 FFT 相关损失。随后，作者在多个下游传感器融合任务上，系统比较 ViT、MAE、DINO 和 MultiMAE 等常见预训练主干的迁移性能。

Result: 实验表明，MultiMAE 是一个出人意料但非常强的开箱即用基线，即使面对预训练中未见过的新模态，也能表现出良好的迁移能力。作者针对模态稀疏和稠密重建提出的修改，包括稀疏样本损失适配、encoder bottleneck 对齐以及 SSIM/FFT 辅助损失，能够进一步提升多传感器迁移性能，并改善整体鲁棒性；但这些提升属于渐进式、一致性收益，而非大幅超越所有现成预训练主干。

Conclusion: 对于多传感器自动驾驶视觉任务，现成的 MultiMAE 预训练主干已经具有很强的迁移有效性，不一定需要昂贵的域内预训练才能获得竞争力；但在真实物理传感器数据和模态稀疏场景下，通过适配稀疏损失、加强模态对齐并引入稠密重建辅助损失，可以在多传感器迁移和鲁棒性方面获得稳定增益。该工作为选择多传感器预训练初始化权重以及设计域内多模态预训练方案提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Transfer learning from pretrained backbones has become standard practice in multi-sensor computer vision---yet two questions remain open: which pretrained weights transfer best to multi-sensor setups, and can in-domain pretraining outperform off-the-shelf solutions? Frameworks like MultiMAE demonstrate the utility of masked autoencoding in a multi-modal context, but rely entirely on dense, model-derived modalities. It is unclear how robustly these methods translate to real-world applications that use physical sensors, where the aggregation of diverse public datasets for large-scale pretraining inherently introduces modality sparsity. To investigate this systematically, we curate a multi-sensor autonomous driving collection of $2.82M$ images from 16 publicly available datasets, incorporating both physical hardware modalities (RGB, LWIR, NIR, Event) and model-derived modalities (Stereo Depth, Segmentation). We use this collection for in-domain pretraining based on the MultiMAE approach. We adapt MultiMAE's loss formulation to accommodate sparse samples, and study the effect of an enforced encoder bottleneck for modality alignment and of additional losses specific to dense reconstruction (SSIM and FFT). We then systematically compare against common pretrained backbones (ViT, MAE, DINO and MultiMAE) across downstream sensor fusion tasks. We find that MultiMAE is a surprisingly strong out-of-the-box baseline, transferring effectively even to modalities it has never seen, while our targeted modifications yield incremental but consistent improvements on multi-sensor transfer and overall robustness.

</details>


### [10] [From Visual Matching to Textual Conditioning: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation via Reference Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04084)
*Jiahao Nie,Yun Xing,Wenbin An,Qixin Zhang,Yap-Peng Tan,Alex C. Kot,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RI-SAM3，通过“反向提示学习”为每个类别优化可复用的文本条件，使冻结的SAM3在跨域小样本分割中摆脱逐样本视觉匹配，实现更稳定、高效的分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAM的跨域小样本分割方法依赖参考图与目标图的视觉匹配，对目标外观变化敏感；而直接使用SAM3的类别文本提示在严重域偏移下仍不稳定，因此需要学习更适合目标类别的文本条件。

Method: RI-SAM3在冻结SAM3参数的前提下学习类别专属文本token，包含三个核心设计：（1）双层参考反演：在冻结文本编码器之前和之后分别学习互补token，前者保持SAM3文本空间一致性，后者提供类别灵活表示；（2）层自适应文本条件：在解码器不同层中组合这些token，以适配不断演化的object query；（3）存在感知置信度监督：联合监督查询置信度与目标存在性，提升预测可靠性。

Result: 在多个广泛使用的CD-FSS基准上，RI-SAM3取得一致领先的分割性能，同时展现出较强鲁棒性、效率和泛化能力；每个类别仅优化约12K参数，并可在单次前向传播中完成分割，无需逐样本参考-目标视觉匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作证明：将视觉参考信息转化为可复用文本条件，并结合SAM3的视觉-语言预训练知识，可以有效缓解跨域小样本分割中的外观变化和域偏移问题，实现高效稳定的类别感知分割。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment objects from unseen domains using only a few annotated references. Recent Segment Anything Model (SAM)-based methods have advanced CD-FSS, yet their reference-target visual matching is highly sensitive to target appearance variations. SAM3 introduces text-conditioned segmentation, enabling natural-language descriptions to guide segmentation across target images, but directly using the category name as the condition still yields unstable performance under severe domain shifts. We propose Reference Inversion for SAM3 (RI-SAM3) that leverages SAM3's large-scale vision-language pre-training to learn suitable textual tokens for each target category. RI-SAM3 comprises three core designs: (i) Bi-level Reference Inversion that learns complementary tokens before and after the frozen text encoder: the former remain anchored in SAM3's text space while the latter represent the category more flexibly; (ii) Layer-adaptive Textual Conditioning combines these tokens differently as object queries evolve across decoder layers; and (iii) Presence-aware Confidence Supervision that jointly supervises query confidence and target presence for reliable predictions. With SAM3 frozen, RI-SAM3 optimizes around 12K parameters only per category and it efficiently segments each target in a single forward pass with reusable textual conditioning without per-sample reference-target visual matching. Extensive experiments over multiple widely adopted benchmarks show that RI-SAM3 achieves superior performance consistently in CD-FSS tasks, together with strong robustness, efficiency, and generalization.

</details>


### [11] [CHROMA-MILL: Joint Low-Light Enhancement and White Balance under Colored Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04092)
*Pol Riubrogent-Comas,David Serrano-Lozano,Maria Pilligua,Michael S. Brown,Ramon Baldrich,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个带精确色度真值的低光RAW多光源数据集CHROMA-MILL，并发现低光图像增强与自动白平衡联合处理优于传统级联流程；进一步提出CTI-Retinexformer，通过监督光照图、色度预测和光照不变场景嵌入，在质量指标上超过最佳级联基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光环境下的图像会同时受到亮度下降和噪声放大的影响，因此既要进行低光图像增强（LLIE），也要进行自动白平衡（AWB）。现有流程通常先LLIE后AWB，但这种策略很少被系统检验，原因是现有低光数据集光源范围较窄且缺乏可用于评估AWB的色度或光源真值。

Method: 论文构建CHROMA-MILL数据集：采集50个场景，使用单反相机和智能手机，在4种校准光源、11个强度等级下拍摄RAW图像。利用光的加性特性，在线性RAW域合成物理上有效的混合光源样本，并得到精确色度真值。随后在联合LLIE与AWB任务上进行基准测试，提出CTI-Retinexformer模型，其核心是同时监督光照图、色度预测以及光照不变的场景嵌入，从而联合校正亮度衰减和白平衡偏差。

Result: 实验表明联合校正优于传统LLIE后接AWB的级联方案。CTI-Retinexformer相较最佳级联基线提升0.7 dB PSNR_L和1.1 ΔE_00，即在峰值信噪比和白平衡颜色误差方面均更优。

Conclusion: 低光图像增强与自动白平衡不应被视为彼此独立或简单串联的两个阶段，而应在同一联合框架中处理。CHROMA-MILL提供了可评估AWB的低光RAW基准，CTI-Retinexformer展示了通过显式光照与色度监督提升低光色彩恢复质量的有效性。

Abstract: Capturing an image in a low-light environment produces a weak, noisy sensor signal. Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to restore it. Auto white balance (AWB) is also required for every image, and the same noisy signal degrades its ability to infer the scene illuminant. LLIE is typically performed first, followed by AWB, but this strategy remains largely unexamined because existing low-light datasets span a narrow range of illuminants and lack illuminant ground truth for evaluating AWB. We address this with CHROMA-MILL, a RAW dataset of 50 scenes captured with a DSLR and smartphone under four calibrated illuminants at eleven intensity levels. Because light is additive, we blend these captures in the linear RAW domain to synthesize physically valid illuminant mixtures with exact chromaticity ground truth. Benchmarking on this data, we find joint correction is best, and introduce CTI-Retinexformer, which supervises an illumination map, a chromaticity prediction, and an illumination-invariant scene embedding, outperforming the best cascade by 0.7 dB PSNR$_L$ and 1.1 $\Delta E_{00}$.

</details>


### [12] [Is This 3D Asset Usable? Beyond the Beauty Render for Asset Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04102)
*Rushikesh Zawar,Vladimir Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Diagnostic Rendered Evidence（DRE），利用渲染得到的美观点、线框图及UV接缝加线框图，并结合冻结ViT分类器与晚融合，用于自动检测可复用3D资产中的隐藏缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型3D资产仓库便于收集素材，但难以判断这些资产能否在场景装配、编辑、渲染、游戏开发和训练数据构建中可靠复用。资产在普通RGB视角下可能看起来合理，却存在几何不连通、UV组织有问题、导入后材质无法正确解析等缺陷。

Method: 作者提出DRE：为每个3D资产生成对齐的三类诊断渲染证据——beauty视图、wireframe视图、以及UV seam加上wireframe的组合视图。每类视图由面向该任务冻结训练的ViT分类器独立评分，最后通过晚融合形成整体判定。推理时仅依赖导入的资产和渲染得到的证据，不使用资产身份、创作者、热度、文本或源数据等仓库元信息。

Result: 在1,234个经人工标注的TexVerse资产上，采用嵌套分组评估时，DRE达到86.36% balanced accuracy和88.32%坏资产召回率。在另一个平衡的764资产外部参考测试中，DRE达到88.9%的balanced reference-label accuracy。结果表明该方法具有跨语料库的迁移潜力。

Conclusion: DRE表明，仅基于渲染证据的专用诊断视图能够暴露普通beauty视图容易遗漏的3D资产缺陷。该方法不依赖来源元数据，具备跨语料复用与实际生产场景筛选坏资产的可行性。

Abstract: Large 3D repositories make it easy to collect assets, but difficult to determine whether they can be reused reliably across scene assembly, editing, rendering, game production, and training-data construction. An asset can look plausible in RGB while hiding disconnected geometry, problematic UV organization, or materials that fail to resolve correctly after import. We introduce Diagnostic Rendered Evidence (DRE): aligned beauty, wireframe, and UV-seam-plus-wireframe views scored by pass-specific frozen ViT classifiers and combined through late fusion. On 1,234 human labeled TexVerse assets, nested grouped evaluation achieves 86.36% balanced accuracy and 88.32% bad-asset recall. On a separate, balanced 764 external reference test, DRE achieved 88.9% balanced reference-label accuracy. Our method requires only an imported asset and renderer-derived evidence, without repository identity, creator, popularity, text, or source metadata at inference. Our results support cross-corpus portability and show that purpose built diagnostic views expose defects that just the beauty views can miss. Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [13] [Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection with Embedding-Space Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04111)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FOX框架，先构建并锁定基于DINOv3的AI生成图像检测器，再利用其视觉表征引导轻量级多模态模型生成可解释取证说明，从而在不改变检测判定的前提下兼顾跨生成器泛化与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像和指令驱动编辑使得局部篡改难以通过拼接边界定位；现有检测器多为黑箱且泛化较差，因为为检测适配骨干网络会记忆易过时的生成器指纹。同时，将分类和语言生成联合训练会使纯视觉表示受语言目标污染，损害专家检测器优势。因此需要一种解释训练不影响检测结论的可解释AI图像取证方法。

Method: FOX首先训练一个专用的DINOv3检测器并将其锁定，保证检测表征和判定不被后续解释训练修改；随后利用锁定检测器的视觉证据指导轻量VLM进行取证解释。其关键模块是联合嵌入预测器，将锁定视觉表征映射到冻结文本编码器的语义空间，生成可由VLM按需表述的解释引导信号。

Result: FOX在14个未见生成器上达到97.4的平均AUC，表明跨生成器检测泛化能力强；在XPlainVerse上的BERTScore为69.8，在LOKI上为56.1，说明其生成的取证解释质量也较具竞争力。

Conclusion: FOX通过“先锁定专家检测器、再用其表征指导解释”的解耦设计，避免了语言生成目标破坏纯视觉检测表征，实现了高泛化检测与可解释AI生成图像取证之间的兼顾。

Abstract: AI-generated images have reached unprecedented realism, while instruction-driven AI editors re-synthesize images for local changes, leaving no splice boundary to localize. Most detectors are black boxes with unverifiable verdicts and generalize poorly because adapting a backbone on the detection distribution memorizes generator-specific fingerprints that quickly become obsolete. Specialist detectors built on pure-vision representations, particularly DINOv3, generalize well across generators, but explainable methods forgo this advantage: jointly training one multimodal model for classification and language generation subjects its visual representation to the language objective. We therefore require that explanation training cannot alter the verdict. We introduce FOX, which first builds and locks a specialist DINOv3 detector, then uses its representation to guide a lightweight VLM toward forensic explanation. A joint-embedding predictor maps locked visual evidence into a frozen text encoder's semantic space, producing guidance that the VLM verbalizes on demand. FOX achieves 97.4 mean AUC across 14 unseen generators, 69.8 BERTScore on XPlainVerse, and 56.1 on LOKI.

</details>


### [14] [Co-Occurrence Priors Override Visual Spatial Evidence: Understanding False Alarms in Vision-Language Models for Construction Struck-By Hazard Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04112)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现：视觉语言模型在建筑工地“被击打/被撞”危险监测中的误报，主要不是源于物体识别或空间关系编码不足，而是源于危险判定被“工人-挖掘机共现”先验主导的决策失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型正被用于施工危险监测，但其频繁误报的成因尚不清楚。该研究试图区分误报是来自对象识别错误、空间关系表征缺陷，还是来自模型在 hazard decision 阶段依赖学习到的场景共现先验，从而为减少误报提供方向。

Method: 研究评估了 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 InternVL3.5-8B，使用 355 组反事实的“工人-挖掘机”场景对，其中受控改变空间接近程度，并设置移除工人的变体。通过行为分析、内部表征探测、因果干预和决策校正，考察模型是否能识别对象、编码空间关系以及如何在危险判定中使用这些表征。

Result: 两个模型均能较好识别工人和挖掘机，并编码空间关系，空间关系探测的 out-of-fold AUROC 分别为 0.82 和 0.91；在 79% 和 73% 的配对场景中，近距场景的危险评分高于远距场景。然而，远距场景中仍有 74% 和 100% 被错误判为危险。移除工人可使 Qwen 的危险误报率降至 11%，且工人存在对危险评分的影响是空间接近的 2.4 倍和 3.2 倍。针对注意力头、神经元和激活的干预未能消除共现偏置。将危险决策显式条件化到模型自身的空间关系表征上，可在保持匹配敏感度的情况下将 Qwen 的特异性从 0.36 提升至 0.58，优于简单阈值重校准。

Conclusion: VLM 在 construction hazard monitoring 中的 struck-by 误报主要源于决策层面的失败，而非视觉识别或空间关系表征的缺陷。未来改进应重点放在让危险决策显式依赖已编码的空间证据，而不是单纯提升视觉识别能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly support construction hazard monitoring, yet their frequent false alarms remain poorly understood. This study investigates whether false struck-by hazard warnings arise from failures in object recognition, spatial relation encoding, or hazard decisions dominated by learned co-occurrence priors. We evaluated Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3.5-8B using 355 counterfactual worker-excavator scene pairs with controlled spatial proximity and worker-removal variants, combining behavioral analysis, internal representation probing, causal interventions, and decision correction. Both models recognized the entities and encoded their spatial relations (out-of-fold AUROC: 0.82 and 0.91), assigning higher hazard scores to near than far scenes in 79% and 73% of pairs. Nevertheless, 74% and 100% of far scenes were falsely classified as hazardous. Removing the worker reduced Qwen's hazard rate to 11%, while worker presence exerted 2.4- and 3.2-times greater influence on hazard scores than spatial proximity in the respective models. Attention-head interventions, neuron-level interventions, and activation steering failed to eliminate this co-occurrence bias. However, conditioning hazard decisions on the model's own spatial-relation representation improved Qwen's specificity from 0.36 to 0.58 at matched sensitivity, outperforming threshold recalibration. These findings reveal that VLM struck-by false alarms stem primarily from decision-making failures rather than deficiencies in object recognition or spatial encoding, suggesting that future efforts should prioritize conditioning hazard decisions on encoded spatial evidence rather than further improving visual recognition alone.

</details>


### [15] [What Remains in Sight? Autoregressive Video Decoding as Representation-Guided Context Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04164)
*Lixuan He,Jihyeon Je,Yang You,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReCR，一种免训练的长程视频自回归生成推理方法，将“让解码器看到哪些历史片段”作为测试时可显式决策的问题，通过解码器内部表征从生成历史中重选并压缩可见上下文，提升长视频生成的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程视频生成越来越多地采用自回归 rollout：新生成的片段会成为后续预测可见历史的一部分。已有因果解码器、长上下文训练和 KV cache 可延长可生成时长，但推理时可见过去通常仍被近期窗口、压缩缓存或外部记忆隐式决定。作者提出：能否将“选择哪些过去帧/片段作为可见上下文”变成测试阶段可显式优化的决策？

Method: ReCR 将自回归视频解码建模为“表征引导的上下文改写”。在每个解码步中，方法基于解码器内部表征从已生成历史中构造新的可见上下文，优选与全局 rollout 状态一致、与近期延续内容冗余低、覆盖时间边界、并能扩展空间布局范围的历史单元；随后将这些被选中的单元映射到紧凑因果轴上，再解码下一个视频块。

Result: 在多个长程文生视频基准上，ReCR 在相同可见 token 预算下持续提升多个自回归骨干模型的表现。表征空间诊断和 transition-count prompt switching 等分析进一步表明，优化“哪些历史内容保持可见”是实现更稳定长视频解码的有效路径。

Conclusion: ReCR 表明长程视频生成中的历史上下文选择不应只由滑动窗口或缓存机制隐式决定，而可作为测试时显式优化的控制变量。该方法免训练、与现有自回归解码器兼容，并能通过表征引导的上下文重写显著改善长视频生成的时序一致性与稳定性。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is increasingly moving toward autoregressive rollout, where each newly generated segment becomes part of the evidence used for future prediction. Recent causal decoders, long-context training, and KV-cache mechanisms have extended feasible duration, but most inference pipelines still decide the visible past implicitly through recent windows, compressed caches, or external memory. This raises a direct question: \textbf{can the choice of which past frames or segments to show to the decoder be treated as an explicit test-time decision?} We answer this question with \textsc{ReCR}, a training-free method that formulates autoregressive video decoding as representation-guided context rewriting. At each step, \textsc{ReCR} builds a selected visible context from the generated history by using internal decoder representations to favor context units that match the global rollout state, avoid redundancy with the recent continuation, preserve temporal boundary coverage, and widen the range of spatial layouts in view. The selected units are then mapped onto a compact causal axis before decoding the next block. Across long-horizon text-to-video benchmarks, \textsc{ReCR} consistently improves multiple autoregressive backbones under matched visible-token budgets. Empirical analyses such as representation-space diagnostics and transition-count prompt switching further show that improving what remains visible is an effective path toward more stable long-video decoding.

</details>


### [16] [ReconDreamer#: Self-Evolving Closed-Loop Synergy of Reconstruction and Generation for Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04173)
*Songling Liu,Guosheng Zhao,Xiaofeng Wang,Chaojun Ni,Guan Huang,Jiwen Lu,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReconDreamer#，一种面向动态驾驶场景的“重建—生成”自演化闭环框架，通过生成模型与重建模型相互迭代增强，缩小合成长轨迹数据与真实传感器观测之间的差异，并显著改善新轨迹渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态驾驶场景重建虽借助生成先验提升新轨迹渲染质量，但生成数据与真实传感器观测仍存在明显分布差距，限制重建保真度进一步提高；需要让重建与生成过程协同优化以弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出自演化闭环框架，使重建模型与生成模型通过迭代精化相互增强，提升 3D 表示能力；同时引入基于几何先验的自适应地面模型，改善重建模型的渲染质量。

Result: 在 Waymo、nuScenes、PandaSet 和 EUVS 基准上进行综合评估；在 Waymo 的 6 米横向偏移设定下，相较 ReconDreamer++，NTA-IoU 提升 8.8%，NTL-IoU 提升 3.0%，FID 降低 15.1%。

Conclusion: ReconDreamer# 通过重建与生成的相互强化有效缩小生成数据与真实观测之间的差距，显著提升动态驾驶场景的新轨迹渲染保真度与 3D 表示能力。

Abstract: Dynamic driving scene reconstruction has made remarkable strides in recent years by leveraging generative priors, particularly in the quality of novel trajectory rendering. Nevertheless, a significant gap remains between the generated data and real-world sensor observations, severely hindering further improvements in reconstruction fidelity. In this paper, we present ReconDreamer#, a self-evolving framework that synergizes reconstruction and generation for dynamic driving scenes, which substantially bridges the gap between synthesized novel trajectory data and recorded real-world observations. Within this self-evolving closed-loop framework, the generative and reconstruction models mutually reinforce each other through iterative refinement, ultimately yielding a significant enhancement in the 3D representation capability of the reconstruction model. Additionally, we introduce a geometry-prior-based adaptive ground model to further enhance the rendering quality of the reconstruction model. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, we conduct comprehensive evaluations on Waymo, nuScenes, PandaSet, and EUVS benchmarks. Specifically on Waymo, at a 6-m lateral shift, ReconDreamer# improves NTA-IoU and NTL-IoU by 8.8% and 3.0%, respectively, and reduces FID by 15.1% relative to ReconDreamer++.

</details>


### [17] [Statistical Machine Learning for Medicinal Plant Leaves Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04193)
*Jayani P. Gamage,Thiyanga S. Talagala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MEDIPI，一种基于图像和统计机器学习的药用植物叶片识别方法，并开源 MedLEA 数据集；该方法在单一数据集上取得很高准确率，但跨数据集泛化能力明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药用植物传统上依赖从业者经验识别，可重复性较差；因此需要基于图像、可复现且可解释的自动识别方法来补充专家知识。

Method: 研究者从叶片图像中提取 52 个可解释特征，包括形状、纹理、颜色及新的 scagnostics 类别，并采用三级层次化分类流程：先识别叶片形状，再识别边缘类型，最后识别物种。作者发布开放 R 包 MedLEA，包含 31 个斯里兰卡药用植物物种的 1099 张叶片图像，并比较层次化与非层次化模型以及随机森林、XGBoost、梯度提升、线性判别分析、KNN、SVM、概率神经网络和人工神经网络等八种算法。

Result: 在 MedLEA 数据集上，层次化随机森林取得最高总体准确率 100%。在仅使用形状特征达到 90% 形状级别准确率的基础上，加入纹理、颜色和 scagnostics 特征后提升至 97%。线性判别分析图显示模型能够较好地区分叶片形状，但当训练和测试数据来自不同数据集时，准确率显著下降至 23%—50%。

Conclusion: MEDIPI 在受控、单一来源的图像数据集上表现优异，并提供了可解释特征和开源数据资源；然而跨数据集性能大幅下降表明其在实际多样环境中的泛化能力仍是主要挑战，需要更多样化数据、域适应和更稳健的评估。

Abstract: Medicinal plants are usually identified using practitioners' experience, but reproducible, image based methods can complement this knowledge. We present MEDIPI (MEDIcinal Plant Identification), a statistical machine learning approach that classifies medicinal plant leaves from photographs. From each leaf image we extract 52 interpretable features covering shape, texture, color, and a new scagnostics category. We then classify leaves using a three level hierarchy: shape, then edge type, then species. We also release MedLEA, an open R package with 1,099 leaf images of 31 Sri Lankan medicinal plant species. On this dataset, a hierarchical Random Forest achieved the best overall accuracy (100%) in our comparison of hierarchical and non-hierarchical approaches and eight algorithms: Random Forest, XGBoost, Gradient Boosting, linear discriminant analysis, k-nearest neighbor, support vector machine, probabilistic neural network, and artificial neural network. Adding texture, color, and scagnostic features to shape features raised shape level accuracy from 90% to 97%. Linear discriminant analysis plots show how the trained model separates leaf shapes. Accuracy dropped sharply (23-50%) when training and test data came from different datasets, so generalization across datasets remains a challenge.

</details>


### [18] [SettleSplat: Monocular Gaussian Mapping under In-View Scene Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04195)
*Anh Thuan Tran,Xiyun Song,Yubin Zhou,Heather Yu,Jana Kosecka,Liang Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SettleSplat，一种面向在线单目 3D Gaussian 建图的系统，用于处理相机视野内持续发生的场景变化。它通过高斯动态性场剔除运动中的暂态干扰，并利用无参数渲染深度不确定性加权的几何矛盾识别持久性真实变化，从而在动态交互后维持最新且一致的 3D Gaussian 地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线 3D Gaussian 建图方法大多假设静态场景，或将动态区域作为离群点过滤，主要关注背景清洁或视角外变化。实际应用中，物体常在相机前方被移动、放置或交互，导致暂态动态与持久性场景变化混合：移动中的物体是干扰，稳定后的新布局又必须保留。因此，需要在不破坏静态背景的前提下，识别并融合视野内的持久性变化。

Method: SettleSplat 首先将粗粒度像素级不确定性提升为高斯级动态性场，对暂态、运动中的高斯进行剪枝，避免其污染地图。针对持久性变化，方法进一步构造几何矛盾度量，并用参数无关的渲染深度不确定性进行加权，以区分真实场景改变与优化不足区域，从而在线更新 3D Gaussian 地图。

Result: 在 Bonn 和 Co-Fusion 数据集上的实验表明，SettleSplat 能够在场景变化后生成一致且最新的 3D Gaussian 地图，并在整幅图像和变化区域的渲染/建图质量上优于当前基线，同时保持与现有在线方法相当的速度。

Conclusion: SettleSplat 证明了在线单目高斯建图可以有效处理相机视野内的暂态与持久性动态混合变化。通过动态性场剪枝与深度不确定性加权的几何矛盾分析，该方法在保持实时性的同时显著提升了动态交互场景中的地图一致性和渲染质量，适用于 AR、机器人和自主系统等需要持续环境感知的任务。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian mapping enables fast and high-fidelity novel view synthesis, with broad applications in augmented reality, robotics, and autonomous systems. However, many current methods assume static scenes or filter out dynamics as outliers. In this paper, we study in-view scene changes where objects are manipulated in front of the camera within a single online sequence, unlike prior works that detect changes outside the camera view or between separate captures. This setting entangles transient and persistent dynamics, as objects can be distractors while moving and persistent once they settle. While recent studies start to address distractors by removing them for clean static background, in-view scene changes remain underexplored. To resolve this, we present SettleSplat, a monocular mapping system that maintains up-to-date 3D Gaussian maps under such changes. Specifically, we lift coarse per-pixel uncertainty into Gaussian dynamicity field, then prune transient ones. For persistent changes, we derive geometric contradictions weighted by parameter-free rendered depth uncertainty to separate true changes from under-optimized regions. On Bonn and Co-Fusion, SettleSplat achieves consistent 3D Gaussian maps, outperforming current baselines on both full images and change regions while maintaining comparable speed.

</details>


### [19] [LiSA: Linear Stochastic Attention for Long-Range Visual Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04216)
*Sahar Dastani,Fereshteh Shakeri,Samuel Barbeau,Kunal Mahatha,Ismail Ben Ayed,Herve Lombaert,Christian Desrosiers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出线性随机注意力模块 LiSA，通过截断随机游走构建的随机扩散过程实现高阶 token 交互，以缓解线性注意力表达力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力计算复杂度随序列长度平方增长，是 Transformer 建模长程依赖的瓶颈；现有线性注意力虽降低复杂度，但一阶聚合限制了表达能力，难以捕获高阶结构。

Method: 提出 Linear Stochastic Attention（LiSA）作为线性注意力插件模块，利用基于截断随机游走的随机扩散过程建模高阶 token 交互，并引入自适应局部性，优先关注语义相似邻居，以保留细粒度结构并保持训练稳定高效。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测任务上进行大量实验，结果显示 LiSA 相比当前最优方法取得性能提升。

Conclusion: LiSA 在保持线性注意力效率的同时增强了高阶交互与结构建模能力，为高效 Transformer 或视觉序列建模提供了一种实用的线性注意力改进方案。

Abstract: Self-attention has become the standard for modern sequence modeling, enabling Transformers to model long-range dependencies across a wide range of modalities. Despite these advances, its quadratic cost with respect to sequence length remains a significant computational bottleneck. Recent work addresses this limitation by introducing linear variants of self-attention, namely with kernel approximations of the softmax operator. However, their first-order aggregation restricts expressivity and struggles to capture higher-order structure. We propose Linear Stochastic Attention (LiSA), a linear attention plug-in module that enables higher-order token interactions via a stochastic diffusion process based on truncated random walks. LiSA incorporates adaptive locality, favoring semantically similar neighbors to preserve fine-grained structures, while maintaining stable and efficient training. We conduct extensive experiments on classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection tasks and show improved results over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [20] [Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning for Semi-Supervised Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04219)
*Zhongrui Xie,Wenfeng Zhang,Wei Hu,Ruitao Xie,Wenjie Wang,Zhouyun Xie,Yi Zhang,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R2CL，通过建模双网络置信度关系为无标注区域分配监督角色，并结合可靠性-角色混合交叉学习、可靠性转移原型对比学习和高置信度上下文细化，提升半监督心脏图像分割中的伪标签可靠性与边界判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督心脏图像分割中，无标注图像提供的伪标签监督在不同空间区域可靠性差异显著：内部区域较稳定，而边界、低置信度和双网络分歧区域容易产生噪声监督。现有方法通常对所有无标注像素施加均匀监督，忽视区域可靠性差异，限制了在困难区域上的判别学习。

Method: R2CL采用双网络框架，先建模两个网络之间的置信度关系，为无标注区域分配不同监督角色：互相不确定区域用于熵正则化，置信度不一致区域用于单向知识迁移，互相置信区域作为可靠监督锚点。随后提出Reliability-Role Mixed Cross Learning（RR-MCL）通过区域混合训练、方向性跨网络监督和互相不确定区域熵正则化实现角色化监督；Reliability-Transition Prototype Contrastive Learning（RT-PCL）利用前景邻域中的可靠性过渡区域构建查询特征，并从互相置信区域学习网络类别原型，以增强模糊边界附近特征判别性；High-confidence Context Refinement（HCR）利用可靠内部像素和互相置信区域中可信密集模块补充稳定上下文监督。

Result: R2CL在多个数据集上对区域重叠指标和边界相关指标均取得稳定提升，尤其在前景稀疏的MVAA-CT数据集上优势更明显。

Conclusion: 将无标注区域按可靠性赋予不同监督角色，比统一伪标签监督更适合半监督心脏图像分割。R2CL通过角色化跨网络学习、原型对比学习和高置信上下文细化，有效缓解边界和低置信区域的噪声监督，提高分割稳定性和边界判别性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation aims to exploit abundant unlabeled images together with limited annotations, but the reliability of pseudo-label supervision is highly uneven across spatial regions. In a dual-network setting, interior regions are usually more stable, whereas boundary regions, low-confidence areas, and disagreements between networks can introduce noisy supervision. However, existing methods typically impose uniform supervision on all unlabeled pixels, overlooking reliability differences and limiting discriminative learning in challenging areas. To address this issue, we propose R2CL (Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning), a framework for semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation. R2CL models confidence relationships between two networks to assign roles to unlabeled regions: mutually uncertain regions are used for entropy regularization, confidence-discrepant regions for directional knowledge transfer, and mutually confident regions as reliable supervision anchors. Reliability-Role Mixed Cross Learning (RR-MCL) implements these roles through region-mixed training, directional cross-network supervision, and entropy regularization over mutually uncertain regions. Reliability-Transition Prototype Contrastive Learning (RT-PCL) constructs query features from foreground-adjacent reliability-transition regions and learns network-specific class prototypes from mutually confident regions, improving feature discrimination around ambiguous boundaries. High-confidence Context Refinement (HCR) exploits reliable interior pixels and trusted dense blocks within mutually confident regions to complement stable contextual supervision. R2CL achieves stable improvements in region-overlap and boundary-related metrics across multiple datasets, with more evident advantages on the foreground-sparse MVAA-CT dataset.

</details>


### [21] [Mamba3R: Fixed-Capacity Selective State Memory for Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04221)
*Zhenxiang Xiong,Tianchen Deng,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Guole Shen,Weidong Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mamba3R，一种面向流式三维重建的联合设计状态-记忆适配器。它在冻结预训练递归主干和任务头的前提下，仅训练 128 万参数，通过选择性状态空间混合器将原生状态特征与固定容量辅助记忆耦合，并利用显式读写路径和分解反馈路径增强跨帧信息传递。该方法在公开与机器人采集数据上降低轨迹误差并提升累积表面/深度精度，同时几乎不增加显存并保持较高吞吐；在 RTX 4060 上以同一检查点离线回放可达 11.7 FPS、5.1 GiB，满足 10 Hz 传感器速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式三维重建需要在长输入序列和固定 GPU 预算下持续维持几何质量。预训练递归模型虽然具有有界状态，但其骨干网络被冻结后，已学到的状态-观测交互也无法更新，限制了长程记忆、跨帧信息校正和实际部署中的精度-效率平衡。因此，作者希望在不更新主干和任务头的情况下，引入可训练适配路径，改善递归重建中的状态演化与记忆使用。

Method: 提出 Mamba3R，一个联合设计的状态-记忆适配器。核心机制包括：使用选择性状态空间混合器，将递归模型的天然状态特征与固定容量辅助记忆联合变换；通过显式读取和写入路径实现跨帧信息传播；通过分解反馈路径调节辅助记忆反馈给后续推理的内容；仅训练 128 万适配器参数，不更新冻结的主干网络和任务头。实验采用共享周期性重置协议，在公开室内外数据集和机器人记录的遍历数据上评估轨迹误差、累积表面精度、深度精度、显存占用和吞吐量。

Result: 与递归基线相比，Mamba3R 在多个公开和真实机器人采集场景中降低轨迹误差，并提升累积表面和深度精度，某项指标提升最高约 40%。对照实验支持所选择混合操作符的有效性以及跨帧携带辅助记忆的必要性。适配后显存占用保持不变，序列长度从 1000 帧增至 2000 帧时吞吐量变化不超过 1.4%。在平台 RTX 4060 上离线回放记录遍历，使用同一检查点平均达到 11.7 FPS，占用 5.1 GiB，平均吞吐覆盖 10 Hz 传感器采集频率。

Conclusion: Mamba3R 证明：在冻结预训练递归主干的情况下，轻量级、可训练的状态-记忆适配路径仍可显著改善流式三维重建的长程几何一致性和精度，同时保持近似恒定的显存与较高吞吐。该结果为在资源受限设备上部署高精度、实时流式重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction must maintain geometric quality over long inputs within a fixed GPU budget. A pretrained recurrent model provides bounded state, but freezing its backbone also fixes its learned state-observation interactions. We introduce Mamba3R, a jointly designed state-memory adapter that provides a trainable adaptation path without updating the backbone or task heads. A selective state-space mixer jointly transforms native state features and a fixed-capacity auxiliary memory, explicit read and write paths carry information across frames, and a decomposed feedback path regulates what the added memory feeds back. Only 1.28 million adapter parameters are trained. Under a shared periodic-reset protocol, it lowers trajectory error and improves accumulated surface and depth accuracy over recurrent baselines on public indoor and outdoor datasets and robot-recorded traversals, gaining up to 40 percent on one metric. Matched comparisons support both the choice of the mixing operator and the utility of carrying the auxiliary memory across frames. Allocated memory remains unchanged and throughput moves by at most 1.4 percent from one to two thousand frames. Replaying its recorded traversals offline, the platform's RTX 4060 averages 11.7 frames per second within 5.1 GiB with the same checkpoint, a mean throughput covering the 10 Hz sensor rate.

</details>


### [22] [LINDA: Low Resource Foot Ulcer Segmentation using Local Similarity and Iterative Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04233)
*Trishaani Acharjee,Sudip Chakrabarty,Rajdeep Chatterjee,Tathagata Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LINDA：一种用于足部伤口分割的轻量、可解释模型，通过局部自相似先验替代原始灰度依赖，在 FUSeg 2021 上取得最优 Dice/IoU/边界精度，仅 25.70M 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足部溃疡是下肢截肢主要原因，在低中收入地区专科医生和计算硬件稀缺。伤口监测工具需高精度但又要能在普通设备上运行，而高精度模型通常较重，因此需要轻量且可靠的分割方法。

Method: 将直接由图像计算、对局部光照变化稳定的局部自相似（LSS）结构先验融合进 ResNet-34 编码器，并用小型 Liquid Time-Constant 模块在四步迭代中细化伤口边界，实现结构引导分割。

Result: 在 FUSeg 2021 统一训练协议下，LINDA 获得最佳 Dice（86.68%）、IoU（79.67%）和边界精度（HD95 6.37 像素），参数量仅 25.70M，少于 U-Net 和 Transformer 基线。

Conclusion: LINDA 兼顾高精度、轻量化和可解释性，适合资源受限场景下的足部伤口分割与评估。

Abstract: Foot ulcers are a leading cause of lower limb amputation, and the highest burden often falls on low and middle income regions where specialists and computing hardware are both scarce. Wound monitoring tools are most useful there when they are accurate yet small enough to run on ordinary devices, but accuracy usually comes from heavy networks. We present LINDA, a compact model that grounds segmentation in an explicit structural prior instead of raw intensity. A Local Self-Similarity (LSS) map, computed directly from the image and stable under local lighting changes, is fused into a ResNet-34 encoder, and a small Liquid Time-Constant module refines the boundary over four iterative steps. With every model trained under one protocol on the FUSeg 2021 dataset, LINDA obtains the best Dice (86.68\%), IoU (79.67\%), and boundary accuracy (HD95 6.37 pixels) in our comparison while using only 25.70M parameters, fewer than the U-Net and transformer baselines. As the LSS map is a fixed function of the input, it exposes the structural cues the model follows in a readable form. Together this makes LINDA an accurate, inspectable, and lightweight option for wound assessment in resource-constrained care.

</details>


### [23] [TCINet: Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network for Nuclei Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04235)
*Zhenghang Qian,Yang Liu,Yanzhi Yu,Peiwu Qin,Yibing Shen,Joongwon Chae,Lihui Luo,Zhongde Zhang,Jianwen Guo,Lianshun Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TCINet 是一种面向病理图像中粘连细胞核分割与细胞类型分类的单阶段网络，通过任务特定特征适配器和组织条件感知的跨分支交互机制，在 PanNuke 上取得了较好的多类与二值全景分割质量，并具备 MoNuSeg 零样本检测迁移能力。</tldr>
 <motivation>细胞核分割尤其需要保留局部边界细节以区分相互接触的细胞核，而细胞类型分类更依赖较宽范围的形态学上下文；同时，这两类任务的需求会随周围组织环境不同而变化，因此需要一种能够根据组织背景自适应协调分割与分类分支交互的模型。</motivation>
 <method>提出 Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network（TCINet）。其核心包括两个组件：1）Task-Specific Feature Adapters（TSFA），在 skip connection 处生成面向特定分支的特征；2）Tissue-Conditioned Task-Aware Cross-Branch Interaction（TC-TACBI），利用预测得到的组织概率调控 nucleus-presence（NP）、horizontal-vertical（HV）和 nucleus-type（NT）三个分支之间的三条非对称交互路径，使网络能够根据组织环境自适应调整信息交换方式。</method>
 <result>在 PanNuke 数据集的标准三折交叉验证下，TCINet 的多类全景质量分数（mPQ）和二值全景质量分数（bPQ）分别为 0.519 和 0.692。在 MoNuSeg 上进行零样本检测测试时，检测 F1 分数达到 0.876。消融实验中，在 PanNuke fold 0 上加入 TC-TACBI 后，mPQ 从 0.505 提升到 0.525，说明该组织条件跨分支交互模块对分割性能有正向贡献。</result>
 <conclusion>TCINet 通过显式建模不同任务对局部边界与全局形态上下文的不同需求，并利用组织概率调控多分支交互，能够同时改善细胞核分割与细胞类型相关的全景分割性能，并展现出一定的零样本泛化能力。代码已公开。</conclusion>
</invoke>



<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Separating touching nuclei requires local boundary detail, while cell type classification requires broader morphological context. These requirements also vary with the surrounding tissue environment. We propose the Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network (TCINet), a single-stage model with Task-Specific Feature Adapters (TSFA) and Tissue-Conditioned Task-Aware Cross-Branch Interaction (TC-TACBI). TSFA produces branch-specific features at skip connections, and TC-TACBI controls three asymmetric paths among nucleus-presence (NP), horizontal-vertical (HV), and nucleus-type (NT) branches using predicted tissue probabilities. On PanNuke under standard three-fold cross-validation, TCINet achieves multiclass and binary panoptic quality scores of 0.519 and 0.692, respectively. Zero-shot tests on MoNuSeg yield a detection F1 score of 0.876. On PanNuke fold 0, adding TC-TACBI increases mPQ from 0.505 to 0.525. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [24] [CounterSketch: Physics-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Sketch-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04244)
*Hien Trinh,Tan-Hiep To,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CounterSketch，一个物理知识驱动的框架，将“如果……会怎样？”类反事实问题转化为对静态草图演变过程的可信动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草图动画或视觉推理方法往往难以把自然语言中的假设性干预与真实物理变化过程对应起来，导致生成的草图演变缺乏物理合理性、因果关系和可验证性。

Method: 该方法通过多阶段流程实现：首先利用VLM对草图场景进行语义与对象层面的场景理解；其次基于因果事件链推理模拟“如果……”假设下的场景变化；随后通过约束满足和轨迹建模生成连续、物理可信的对象运动轨迹；再通过物理、几何和语义三类验证器检查轨迹合理性；最后将验证后的轨迹投影为对象级别的运动边界框，用于引导后续草图视频合成。

Result: 在作者构建的CounterSketchBench（包含80个问题）上的实验表明，基于物理建模生成的轨迹能为草图动画中的对象运动提供更有效的引导，优于直接依赖VLM进行预测的方式。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在反事实草图动画生成任务中，显式引入物理约束、因果推理和可验证轨迹生成能够显著提升运动建模的可靠性，说明物理知识与语言模型结合是推动草图动画走向真实世界合理性的有效路径。

Abstract: Counterfactual sketch animation explores how a static sketch evolves under a hypothetical intervention. We present CounterSketch, a physics-grounded framework that transforms natural-language ``What if...?'' queries into physically plausible sketch animations. The framework integrates VLM-guided scene grounding, causal event-chain reasoning, constraint-based trajectory generation, and verification. It models physical transitions to update the scene graph, computes continuous object trajectories, and validates them against physical, geometric, and semantic constraints. The verified trajectories are then projected into object-level motion bounding boxes to guide downstream sketch-video synthesis. Experiments on CounterSketchBench (80 questions) demonstrate that physics-grounded trajectories provide stronger motion guidance than direct VLM predictions.

</details>


### [25] [P-SER: Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation for Class-Incremental HRRP Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04254)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P-SER框架，通过物理一致性约束的可扩展散射表征，解决HRRP类别增量识别中的视角敏感、无旧样本知识遗忘和动态模型增长开销大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨距离像（HRRP）虽紧凑表征雷达目标，但具有强烈视角敏感性；观测角变化会移动散射峰位置和幅度，且角反射器组可能产生类船响应，使无历史样本的类别增量识别困难。同时，现有动态扩展方法带来较大参数和内存开销。

Method: 提出物理一致性约束的可扩展散射表征框架P-SER。首先利用和通道与方位差通道复HRRP及观测几何重建主导散射点，获取空间结构、目标尺度和能量分布；随后据此生成类级散射原型和样本级显著性先验，为特征学习和旧类知识保持提供物理约束。另构建基本散射体字典，用于刻画局部强散射机理，并在推理阶段校准不确定的船—反射器识别。最后，在共享HRRP主干网络中增量加入轻量残差适配器，以较小模型增长学习新类。

Result: 在仿真和实测HRRP数据集上，P-SER在不保留旧类样本的条件下取得较有竞争力的识别性能，同时保持物理一致性、抗干扰能力、旧知识保持能力和模型可扩展性。

Conclusion: P-SER通过将HRRP散射物理信息引入表示学习和增量扩展机制，缓解视角敏感、目标混淆和灾难性遗忘问题，为无样本例子的雷达目标类别增量识别提供有效方案。

Abstract: High-resolution range profiles (HRRPs) provide compact radar target representations but exhibit strong aspect sensitivity, complicating class-incremental recognition when historical data are unavailable. Changes in viewing aspect shift scattering-peak locations and amplitudes, while reflector groups may produce responses resembling ships. Existing dynamic expansion methods can also incur substantial parameter and memory overhead. To address these challenges, we propose P-SER, a Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation framework for exemplar-free class-incremental HRRP recognition. P-SER reconstructs dominant scattering points from complex-valued sum- and azimuth-difference-channel HRRPs and observation geometry. The reconstructed spatial structure, target extent, and energy distribution are used to derive class-level scattering prototypes and sample-level saliency priors, providing physically grounded constraints for feature learning and old-class knowledge retention. A primitive scatterer dictionary further characterizes local strong-scattering mechanisms and calibrates uncertain ship--reflector predictions at inference. Meanwhile, lightweight residual adapters are incrementally introduced into a shared HRRP backbone to learn new classes with limited model growth. Experiments on simulated and measured HRRP datasets show that P-SER delivers competitive recognition performance without retaining old-class exemplars, while maintaining physical consistency, interference robustness, knowledge retention, and model scalability.

</details>


### [26] [Sketch-Omni: Instance-Level Sketch-Based Visual Prompting for Perception MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04262)
*Dongyu Wang,Dar-Yen Chen,Zhiming Hong,Arkaprabha Basu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch-Omni是首个面向草图的可提示感知多模态大模型，既能在草图上完成检测、指点、指代消解与可供性定位，也能以草图作为查询在照片中定位特定实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示感知模型均面向真实照片设计，难以直接处理无颜色、无纹理的草图；同时缺乏“草图—照片实例”配对数据，导致无法实现以草图定位照片中特定对象这一新查询范式。

Method: 通过边缘检测将带标注照片自动转化为保留标签的草图训练数据，并构造草图到照片的实例对应关系；进一步采用照片引导的关系对齐，使模型在草图输入下复现照片输入中短语与图像块、图像块之间的关系，而无需任何人工绘制训练标签。

Result: 在草图提示定位任务上达到91.4 F1，显著高于最强通用多模态大模型的81.8；当照片中存在多个同类目标时优势进一步扩大，并在六个任务的40个评测指标中有39项取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: Sketch-Omni证明了草图可作为真实视觉感知中的有效输入与查询媒介，拓展了可提示感知模型的应用边界；同时发布了代码、模型以及包含2754条草图—照片链接的人工标注基准。

Abstract: Promptable perception, from GroundingDINO to Rex-Omni, puts one model behind one prompt interface and returns coordinates for any localisation task. Every model in that line was built for photographs; none has been given a sketch. We present Sketch-Omni, to our knowledge the first perception MLLM built to work on sketches and from them. On a sketch, the sketch is the image, and text prompts detect, point, resolve object and spatial referring expressions, and ground affordances in it. From a sketch, the sketch is the prompt, and a drawing of one object localises that object in a photograph, even among several of the same category, a query that neither a category name nor a photo exemplar can pose. Two obstacles stand in the way. A photo-trained model's attention is tuned to colour and texture, which a sketch does not have, and no dataset pairs a drawing with the one instance it depicts among others of its kind. We build the training data instead of collecting it. An edge detector turns every labelled photograph into a sketch-like drawing that keeps the photograph's labels, and two constructions produce sketch-to-photo instance pairs from existing data. On top of this, photo-guided relation alignment passes each photograph and its edge sketch through the same model and trains the sketch pass to reproduce how the photograph pass relates phrases to patches and patches to each other. Not one training label is drawn by hand. Sketch-Omni reaches 91.4 F1 on sketch-prompted localisation against 81.8 for the strongest general-purpose MLLM, with a lead that doubles when the photograph holds several instances of the queried category, and it is best in 39 of 40 evaluation cells across six tasks. Code, model, and a hand-annotated benchmark of 2754 sketch-to-photo links will be released.

</details>


### [27] [Weight-Space Programming for Improving Image-Restoration Diffusion Models: Technical Conjectures and a Recursive Improvement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04268)
*Zhuoran Zheng,Doubao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种 conjectural 框架，探讨大语言模型能否通过 weight-space programming 直接编辑扩散式图像复原模型的少量权重，以实现无梯度、无配对数据、无微调的质量提升，并设想 diagnose-edit-evaluate-re-diagnose 的最小递归自我改进循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受 LLM 在程序修复中仅修改极少量参数即可解决结构化 bug 的启发，试图将 weight-space programming 从“debugging”推进到“improvement”，尤其在精确回归类的图像复原任务中验证其可行性，包括去模糊、超分辨率、去噪和补全。

Method: 文章提出三个技术猜想：C1，LLM 能在约 200 次前向传播预算内定位导致质量下降的层；C2，对已识别的小规模权重方向进行编辑，例如缩放高频滤波器范数或校正输出偏置通道，可提升 PSNR 和感知质量；C3，通过递归循环持续诊断、编辑、评估和再诊断，可逐轮累积增益，形成面向视觉任务的 minimal recursive self-improvement 框架。

Result: 摘要未提供实验结果，而是给出分析、技术猜想、所需 AI 能力评估、像素级精确回归的挑战讨论，以及一个 PyTorch 风格的部署流程和 benchmark 设计。结论主要停留在 conjectural 层面，旨在开辟新的子研究方向。

Conclusion: 图像复原扩散 UNet 因底层信号处理层较集中、高层语义层较分布，可能成为测试 LLM 直接编辑权重的折中场景。若能验证，将表明目标式权重编辑可在不训练的情况下改善特定复原缺陷，并为视觉模型的递归自我改进提供初步范式。

Abstract: Can an LLM directly edit the weights of a diffusion-based image-restoration model to \emph{improve} restoration quality---without gradients, paired data, or fine-tuning? Recent experiments show frontier LLMs can fix structural bugs by modifying as little as \(0.016\%\) of parameters, but cannot instill new capabilities. We ask whether weight-space programming (WSP) can move from \emph{debugging} to \emph{improvement} in the precise-regression setting of image restoration (deblurring, super-resolution, denoising, inpainting). Restoration diffusion UNets present a middle ground: low-level signal-processing layers are concentrated enough for targeted weight-direction edits, while high-level semantic layers remain distributed and resistant to sparse modification. We propose three technical conjectures: (C1) an LLM can localize the layer behind a quality gap (over-smoothing, color shift, hallucinated texture) within a 200 forward-pass budget; (C2) editing a small set of identified weight directions (e.g., scaling high-frequency filter norms, correcting output-bias channels) can measurably improve PSNR and perceptual quality; (C3) a recursive loop---diagnose \(\rightarrow\) edit \(\rightarrow\) evaluate \(\rightarrow\) re-diagnose---can compound gains across rounds, forming a minimal recursive self-improvement (RSI) loop for vision. We analyze the required AI capability (arguing targeted edits need not superintelligence, while general capability generation does) and detail the challenges of precise pixel-level regression. We provide a PyTorch-style deployment pipeline and outline a benchmark. This manuscript is conjectural, intended to open a new subtopic for image restoration.

</details>


### [28] [CLOCs-R: Radar-Assisted Camera-LiDAR Fusion for Long-Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04300)
*Richard Beuck,John Nguyen,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CLOCs-R，将雷达证据扩展到相机-LiDAR候选级融合中，用于长距离3D目标检测。在aiMotive数据上评估发现：CLOCs-R在30–50 m有一定AP提升，但在>50 m几乎无显著收益，无法恢复远距离漏检。残差评分变体可修正远距离性能退化。总体结论是，雷达虽可补充远距离观测，但现有特征与候选生成限制使远距离增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中远距离3D检测面临LiDAR点云稀疏和相机证据不足的问题，而雷达能远距离持续返回。现有CLOCs主要融合相机与LiDAR候选，难以充分利用雷达对远距离目标的辅助信息，因此需要将雷达证据引入候选级重评分以提升远距离检测。

Method: 提出CLOCs-R：首先对雷达多扫次数据进行累积和ego-motion补偿，再通过距离自适应门控将雷达回波关联到NMS前的3D候选。随后提取紧凑雷达特征，包括候选支持度、多普勒速度与散布、距离残差和偏移，并将其附加到相机-LiDAR候选对之后，由轻量融合网络对候选进行重评分。作者先在KITTI上验证融合实现可复现CLOCs，再在aiMotive白天晴好天气子集上，使用冻结的SECOND和Cascade R-CNN进行分距离段评测，范围延伸至200 m。

Result: 相机-LiDAR CLOCs相比SECOND在0–50 m有提升，但超过50 m后性能退化；事后引入的残差评分变体通过修正SECOND分数而非替换分数，恢复了大部分远距离损失。加入雷达证据后，CLOCs-R在30–50 m使BEV AP从0.857提升到0.870；但在>50 m，center-distance AP仅提升约+0.003，处于随机种子波动范围内，最终与仅LiDAR的SECOND基本持平。

Conclusion: CLOCs-R表明雷达证据可在中近距离改善相机-LiDAR候选重评分，但对远距离检测提升有限。原因包括雷达特征对真假候选区分能力弱、远距离正样本稀少、相机证据不足，以及LiDAR候选生成阶段已经漏检时重评分无法恢复。因此该工作揭示了单纯在候选级融合中增加雷达，不足以根本解决长距离3D检测问题。

Abstract: Long-range 3D object detection remains difficult for autonomous vehicles because LiDAR returns become sparse and camera evidence weakens with distance, while automotive radar keeps returning measurements far beyond both. We present CLOCs-R, an extension of the CLOCs candidate-level fusion framework that adds radar evidence to camera-LiDAR rescoring. Radar returns are accumulated over several sweeps, compensated for ego-motion, and associated with each pre-NMS 3D candidate through a range-dependent gate. Compact features summarizing support, Doppler speed and spread, range residual, and offset are then appended to every camera-LiDAR pair before a lightweight fusion network rescores the candidates. We first verify that our fusion implementation reproduces CLOCs on KITTI. We then evaluate on the daytime clear-weather subset of the aiMotive dataset, reporting distance-binned metrics out to 200 m with frozen SECOND and Cascade R-CNN detectors. Camera-LiDAR CLOCs improves on SECOND up to 50 m but degrades beyond it. A residual scoring variant, introduced post hoc, corrects SECOND's score instead of replacing it and recovers most of this loss. Radar consistently improves BEV AP at 30-50 m (0.857 to 0.870), but beyond 50 m its gain is small (+0.003 center-distance AP), within seed variation, and CLOCs-R with residual scoring is statistically tied with LiDAR-only SECOND. Our analysis attributes the limited long-range benefit to weak separation of true and false detections by radar features, scarce far-range training positives, poor camera evidence at distance, and missing LiDAR proposals that rescoring cannot recover. We report all as-designed and post hoc results side by side.

</details>


### [29] [FlowTranslator: Training-Free In-Image Machine Translation with Flow-Based Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04303)
*Zhixian Li,Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Yunyan Xiao,Yanglin Huang,Pinxuan Chen,Jiyu Zhuang,Cong Xu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowTranslator，一种无需训练的图内机器翻译框架，通过OCR、LLM和文本生成扩散模型，结合轨迹控制实现高质量翻译文本注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图内机器翻译方法依赖大规模配对训练图像；同时先进文生图模型已具备较实用的多语言文本生成能力，可迁移用于翻译文本的自然嵌入。

Method: FlowTranslator首先使用OCR提取图像文本，并由LLM生成翻译提示，再注入基于扩散的文生图模型；同时引入Region Coupling Release和Coupling Expansion两种轨迹控制，以优化译文生成的准确性与自然性。

Result: 在EasyOCR read-back协议下，FlowTranslator的平均PRIM BLEU为20.6、COMET为57.2，明显优于基于训练的基线UniTranslator；在更真实的ScenePair整图英文翻译为中文任务上达到40.1%精确匹配准确率，并超过部分仅处理文本渲染的现有编辑系统。

Conclusion: 实验表明，基于流/扩散编辑的FlowTranslator可作为图内机器翻译任务的强且完全无需训练的基线，证明了将多语言文生图能力迁移到翻译文本嵌入的可行性。

Abstract: In-image machine translation (IIMT) replaces text in an image with its translation while preserving the surrounding visual context. Existing task-specific models achieve convincing performance but require massive paired training images before and after translation. As advanced text-to-image (T2I) models have reached a practically usable level of multilingual text generation, we argue that this capability can be transferred to IIMT and present FlowTranslator. It is a training-free IIMT framework that first employs an OCR model and an LLM to generate the translation prompt, which is injected into the T2I-based diffusion model. To optimize the accurate and natural generation of translated text, we introduce two trajectory controls: Region Coupling Release (RCR) partially decorrelates target-branch noise inside the text region early in the trajectory, while Coupling Expansion (CE) amplifies the emerging edit displacement mid-trajectory. Under the EasyOCR read-back protocol, FlowTranslator achieves average PRIM BLEU and COMET scores of 20.6 and 57.2, respectively, substantially outperforming the strong training-based baseline UniTranslator. On the more challenging and realistic whole-image ScenePair photographs translated into Chinese, it reaches 40.1\% exact-match accuracy and surpasses existing editing systems, some of which only address the text-rendering stage rather than the complete IIMT task. These results establish flow-based editing as a strong, fully training-free baseline for IIMT.

</details>


### [30] [Weather Adaptive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04305)
*Nicholas Taylor,Alexander Picon,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究关注恶劣天气下相机目标检测器的置信度阈值决策问题，提出一个可校准的“误报-漏检权衡旋钮”λ，用于在固定检测器上选择最优判定规则，并计划通过CRC恢复分天气条件下的漏检保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨、雾、雪、沙等恶劣天气会降低目标检测器召回率，而部署时使用的置信度阈值直接决定漏检数量与误报数量；不同天气条件可能需要不同阈值。SeqCRC等保形风险控制方法可在各天气分片中校准全局阈值以保证漏检预算，但其误报率不可控，因此需要更系统地调节漏检与误报之间的权衡。

Method: 首先复现SeqCRC，将其第一步作为基线：在DAWN数据集的各天气条件中分别使用保形风险控制校准置信度阈值。随后提出一个校准的权衡旋钮λ，表示为避免漏检一个目标所接受的误报数量。基于λ选择期望成本最优的决策规则：可使用单一分数阈值，也可使用逐检测框规则——利用检测器自身的逐框输出，通过梯度提升树估计检测框为真实目标的校准概率，并在该概率不低于1/(1+λ)时保留检测框。同时考察F-score偏好如何映射到该旋钮，并计划对λ本身施加CRC，以在每种天气条件下恢复漏检保证。λ仅在BDD100K训练集上拟合，并只在BDD100K验证集和DAWN上进行评估。

Result: YOLOv8x配合SeqCRC可在DAWN各天气分片中将留出集漏检比例控制在20%预算内，而单一全局阈值在雾天会失效；但其误报率成为被动结果，在DAWN和BDD100K不同条件下从每图1.6个上升到117个。研究进一步提出λ旋钮以调节漏检-误报权衡，并指出F-score偏好可映射到同一旋钮。论文强调该权衡机制在BDD100K train上拟合、仅在BDD100K val和DAWN上评估。

Conclusion: 固定检测器在恶劣天气下的阈值决策不仅是校准漏检风险，还应提供可控的误报-漏检权衡。SeqCRC能保证各天气条件下的漏检预算，但误报不可选；提出的λ旋钮通过成本敏感判定规则统一了全局阈值与逐框概率决策，并为后续用CRC控制λ提供了方向，以兼顾天气自适应保证与实际部署中的误报成本。

Abstract: Camera object detectors lose recall in rain, fog, snow and sand, and the confidence threshold at which a detector is deployed decides how many objects it misses and how many false alarms it raises. We study how a frozen detector should make this decision in adverse weather. We first reproduce SeqCRC, a conformal object detector whose first step calibrates the confidence threshold by conformal risk control (CRC), and use that step, calibrated separately in each weather condition, as our baseline. With YOLOv8x it keeps the held-out miss fraction within a 20% budget in every DAWN weather bin, where a single global threshold fails in fog, but the false alarms it needs are an output rather than a choice: from 1.6 to 117 per image across DAWN and BDD100K conditions. We then propose a calibrated trade-off dial. One setting, $\lambda$, the number of false alarms accepted to avoid one missed object, selects the expected-cost-optimal decision rule: either one score threshold, or a per-detection rule that keeps a box when its calibrated probability of being a real object, estimated by gradient-boosted trees from the detector's own per-box outputs, is at least $1/(1+\lambda)$. F-score preferences map onto the same dial, and CRC applied to $\lambda$ itself is planned to restore a per-condition miss guarantee. The dial is fitted once on BDD100K train and graded only on BDD100K val and DAWN.

</details>


### [31] [SteelHuman: Predicting Operator Estimates of Steel-Scrap Composition from Sparse RGB Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04317)
*Jovan Kamchev,Zoran Ivanovski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种更简单的钢废料堆成分估计方法：利用稀疏手持视角图像、冻结的 DINOv2 特征并跨视图聚合，在 600 个 out-of-fold 废料堆上相对人工估算达到 13.4% TV 距离；同时用同一冻结特征在 DOES 公开数据集上取得 93.97% 分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢废料成分估计用于产量计划和供应商补偿，但多吨废料堆的成分测量成本高，常依赖人工经验；已有成分级方法需要结构化采集和多阶段预处理，因此作者希望探索无需复杂采集流程、仅用稀疏手持视角即可估计成分的方法。

Method: 对稀疏手持采集的废料堆图像提取冻结 DINOv2 tokens，并在多视图之间均匀聚合以估计成分。作者进一步分析误差来源，发现主要剩余误差集中在按厚度划分的四类重熔废料 HMS 的内部分配上；于是采用依赖图像的分配方式替代训练集分布先验。为了进行外部评估，作者使用面向分类任务的公开 DOES 数据集，训练分类器并检验相同特征与成分模型是否能对齐相关类别。

Result: 在 600 个 out-of-fold 废料堆上，冻结 DINOv2 tokens 的跨视图均匀聚合达到 13.4% TV distance。误差主要来自四类 HMS 的内部成分分配；图像依赖分配将 within-HMS TV 从 25.1% 降至 11.2%。在公开 DOES 数据集上，相同冻结特征分类器达到 93.97% accuracy，超过文中比较的已发表结果；成分模型无需专门适配也能与相关 DOES 类别对齐。

Conclusion: 稀疏手持视角结合冻结视觉大模型特征可显著简化钢废料成分估计流程，并表现出良好的跨任务一致性。但成分预测仍缺乏公开数据集和统一基准，当前外部评估主要依赖分类数据集，且模型误差仍集中于厚度相近的 HMS 类别分配。

Abstract: Steel scrap composition estimation is an important part of yield planning and fair supplier compensation. Measuring the composition of a multi-ton pile is an inherently laborious process, so the assessment is often left to trained human operators. Recent works tackle composition-level tasks, but require structured acquisition and multi-stage preprocessing. We study a simpler approach to steel scrap load assessment by aggregating features from sparse, handheld views of the pile. On 600 out-of-fold piles, frozen DINOv2 tokens aggregated uniformly across views reach 13.4% total variation (TV) distance against operator estimates. Most remaining error lies in allocation among four thickness-defined heavy melting scrap (HMS) classes, yet image-dependent allocation reduces within-HMS TV from 25.1% under a training-set split prior to 11.2%. Due to the lack of public datasets and benchmarks for predicting composition, external evaluation relies on the public DOES dataset intended for classification. A classifier using the same frozen features reaches 93.97% accuracy, exceeding the published results we compare against. The composition model also aligns with related DOES categories without adaptation.

</details>


### [32] [EndoS3:Endoscopic Semantic Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04358)
*Mona Sheikh Zeinoddin,Benjamin Wright,Zafer Tandogdu,Greg Shaw,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoS3，一种基于高斯泼溅的语义SLAM框架，并结合CRCD++数据集，用于内窥镜手术场景中的相机位姿跟踪、可变形重建和语义建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D手术场景理解虽已有进展，但语义SLAM仍面临两大瓶颈：其一，需要同时完成相机位姿跟踪、高保真可变形重建和语义建图；其二，缺少能覆盖全部评估任务、且接近真实手术环境的标注数据集。

Method: EndoS3利用实例掩码引导三项任务：通过实例级运动分解模块补偿相机运动并分析残差光流，区分静态与动态实例；相机位姿基于静态高斯跟踪，动态实例由实例感知的形变场控制节点和形变网络建模；同时设计语义节点分配模块，根据实例尺寸和非刚性程度重新分配固定节点预算。作者还提出CRCD++离体胆囊切除术数据集，提供RGB-D图像、相机位姿和实例标注。

Result: 在CRCD++和SemSup数据集上，EndoS3在相机跟踪、重建质量和语义建图三项任务上均优于现有通用及手术语义SLAM方法。

Conclusion: 语义感知的运动与形变建模对手术语义SLAM至关重要。EndoS3与CRCD++分别为内窥镜场景的几何理解和语义理解提供了新方法与新基准。

Abstract: Purpose: Despite advances in 3D surgical scene understanding, semantic simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM) remains under-explored for two key reasons. First, it requires solving three challenging tasks in parallel: camera pose tracking, high-fidelity deformable reconstruction, and semantic mapping, making progress particularly demanding. Second, the scarcity of datasets providing the necessary ground truth to evaluate all three tasks comprehensively under conditions that closely reflect real surgical procedures environments. Method: We propose EndoS3, a Gaussian Splatting-based semantic SLAM framework that uses instance masks to guide all three tasks. An instance-level motion decomposition module separates static and dynamic instances by evaluating residual flow after compensating for camera motion, accounting for flow noise and comparing motion across neighbouring instances. The camera is tracked against the static Gaussians, while an instance-aware warp field gives each dynamic instance its own control nodes and deformation network. A semantic node allocation module redistributes a fixed node budget across instances according to their size and non-rigidity. We also introduce CRCD++, an ex-vivo Cholecystectomy dataset with RGB-D frames, camera pose and instance annotations for full semantic SLAM evaluation. Results: EndoS3 surpasses all state-of-the-art general-purpose and surgical semantic SLAM methods in tracking, reconstruction and semantic mapping on the CRCD++ and SemSup datasets. Conclusion: Semantic-aware motion and deformation modelling is vital to surgical semantic SLAM. EndoS3 and CRCD++ offer a method and benchmark for improved geometric and semantic understanding in endoscopy.

</details>


### [33] [IGSW: Multi-Weather Urban Scene Reconstruction with Intrinsic-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04374)
*Zhongquan Wang,Long Cao,Peijia Xie,Senlei Ma,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IGSW是一个面向4D场景高保真天气编辑的两阶段框架：第一阶段将高斯场景外观解耦为天气共享的本征量与天气特定成分，并借助扩散编辑图和扩散逆渲染本征图提供特征级软监督；第二阶段通过结构保持门控抑制不可靠监督，并在每个高斯上引入天气传输变量与目标天气分支，以在保持结构与时序连续性的同时实现显著天气外观变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真天气编辑对自动驾驶端到端训练和闭环仿真具有重要价值。虽然将生成式图像编辑与动态高斯表示结合可支持多天气场景重建，但编辑后的图像常出现结构畸变，若直接拟合其外观会把教师图像中的误差传播到4D场景中，破坏原始几何结构和时间连续性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架IGSW。Stage I：对背景高斯外观进行内在解耦，分为天气共享的反照率与法线，以及天气相关的辐照度和残差；利用扩散式图像编辑模型生成多天气教师图像，并用扩散式逆渲染器估计本征图，为结构与光照变化提供特征级软监督。Stage II：设置结构保持门控，结合语义冲突与跨天气本征差异识别并抑制不可靠教师区域；同时为每个高斯建模天气传输变量，并引入目标天气分支，以强化所需天气下的外观、光照和大气效果。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上，相比现有方法，IGSW取得了最高的内容相似度和语义一致性，以及最低的时间误差，同时保留了明显的天气相关光照和大气变化。

Conclusion: IGSW通过将高斯场景分解为天气共享本征属性与天气特定成分，并在优化过程中抑制生成图像中的结构畸变，有效解决了生成式天气编辑向4D场景传播误差的问题，为自动驾驶中的多天气场景重建和高保真天气编辑提供了更稳定、更结构一致的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity weather editing of 4D scenes can support end-to-end training and closed-loop simulation for autonomous driving. Combining generative image editing with dynamic Gaussian representations enables multi-weather scene reconstruction, but weather-edited images often contain structural distortions. Directly fitting their appearance may propagate errors from teacher images into the reconstructed 4D scene, compromising the original scene structure and temporal continuity. To address these challenges, we propose IGSW, a two-stage framework that combines intrinsic-decoupled 4D Gaussian scene modeling with structure-preserving weather refinement. In Stage I, background Gaussian appearance is decomposed into weather-shared albedo and normals and weather-specific irradiance and residuals. Weather teacher images generated by a diffusion-based image editing model and intrinsic maps estimated by a diffusion-based inverse renderer provide feature-level soft supervision for stable structure and illumination changes. In Stage II, a structure-preservation gate combines semantic conflicts with cross-weather intrinsic discrepancies to suppress unreliable teacher regions. Per-Gaussian weather transport variables and target-weather branches then strengthen the desired weather appearance over the intrinsic-decoupled scene. Experiments on nuScenes and Waymo show that IGSW achieves the highest content similarity and semantic consistency and the lowest temporal error among the compared methods, while retaining pronounced weather-dependent illumination and atmosphere. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [Understanding Backdoor Attacks in Scene Graph Generation via Global Context Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04379)
*Xiaoguang Chang,Tuo Chen,Chengze Jiang,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究场景图生成（SGG）训练期后门攻击风险，发现全局上下文通路是后门效应的关键载体；替换被污染的表征可显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
