Motivation: 场景图生成能输出结构化的物体关系表示，但其训练时后门攻击尚未被充分研究。相比图像分类，SGG存在“物体—关系对”层面的攻击面，可针对特定类别的关系进行选择性破坏；同时，由于SGG依赖局部证据，全局触发器的作用不明确，而新型SGG模型越来越强调全局上下文，可能放大此类风险。

Method: 论文提出有效的SGG后门攻击方法，并建立“三轴评估协议”刻画后门行为；在Visual Genome上，对代表性开放词表SGG模型和闭集SGG模型进行实验。进一步通过机制性分析，检查全局上下文通路对后门效应的贡献，例如在推理阶段将污染的全局上下文表征替换为干净表征，以及在训练阶段干预该通路中的触发信号。

Result: 实验证明所提出的SGG后门攻击对开放词表和闭集模型均有效。机制分析表明，显式全局上下文通路是后门效应的主要承载路径；在推理时替换污染的全局上下文表征，可使OvSGTR系列和RelationFormer的攻击成功率分别下降100%和97%。在OvSGTR系列上进行训练时干预后，抑制该通路中的触发信号会降低攻击成功率，但也会显著损害模型效用。

Conclusion: 显式的全局上下文设计虽然有助于SGG场景建模，但也可能成为训练期后门攻击的重要风险入口。论文揭示了全局上下文通路在SGG后门行为中的关键作用，提示未来SGG模型在引入全局信息时需要兼顾安全性与后门防御能力。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) produces structured scene representations, yet its training-time backdoor risk remains unexplored. Unlike classification, SGG exposes a pair-level attack surface that enables selective corruption of relations involving a designated object category. However, because SGG largely relies on localized evidence, globally placed triggers may face difficulty in manipulating such predictions. Recent SGG architectures increasingly incorporate global context to improve scene modeling, which may in turn facilitate such attacks. In this paper, we systematically study this backdoor risk and investigate the role of the global context pathway in backdoor behavior. Concretely, we first instantiate this attack with an effective method, and propose a three-axis evaluation protocol to characterize backdoor behavior. Experiments on Visual Genome with representative open-vocabulary and closed-set SGG models demonstrate the effectiveness of the attack. Mechanistic analyses reveal that the explicit global context pathway serves as a major carrier of the backdoor effect across the evaluated SGG models. In particular, replacing the poisoned global context representation with its clean counterpart during inference sharply suppresses the attack, yielding 100% and 97% reductions in attack success for the OvSGTR family and RelationFormer, respectively. Moreover, training-time interventions in the OvSGTR family show that suppressing trigger signals in this pathway either reduces attack success or substantially degrades utility. Together, these findings suggest that explicit global context designs may pose a backdoor risk in SGG architectures.

</details>


### [35] [StepX-OPD: Rethinking Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04386)
*Chenru Wang,Xuanyang Zhang,Yucheng Han,Wenbo Nie,Wei Cheng,Xianfang Zeng,Gang YU,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 StepX-OPD，将文生图扩散模型的“能力整合”和“少步采样蒸馏”合并到同一训练阶段，使学生模型在自身短轨迹 rollout 状态上同时接受冻结专家监督并学习少步生成；在 SD3.5-Medium 和 FLUX.1-dev 上，其四步模型在指令遵循、文字渲染、图像质量和人类偏好综合表现中领先，并显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可部署文生图模型既要具备指令遵循、文字渲染、人类偏好对齐等能力，又要支持少步采样以实现低延迟生成。现有方法通常分两阶段：先通过多教师在线策略蒸馏训练全步模型以整合能力，再以该模型为固定教师进行步数蒸馏。但全步模型被压缩成少步模型后通常会性能下降，而且第二阶段必须等第一阶段完成，造成额外算力浪费和次优结果。

Method: StepX-OPD 将能力蒸馏与少步采样学习统一到一个学生模型的训练过程中。学生不再只在完整长轨迹上学习，而是在自己实际 rollout 经过的状态上接收多个冻结专家教师的监督，从而避免“训练轨迹”和“推理轨迹”不一致的问题。作者发现，在 rollout 的 KL 权重分解下，速度场不匹配在接近干净图像的低噪端最严重，因此额外对学生模型的速度预测在低噪部分进行回归。针对能力整合后出现的“未要求文字却把 prompt 词写入图像”的问题，论文还提出一种降低该现象的变体。

Result: 在 SD3.5-Medium 和 FLUX.1-dev 两个文生图基座模型上，StepX-OPD 在四步采样方法中取得最高的归一化平均分数，覆盖指令遵循、文字渲染、图像质量和人类偏好等指标，并超过需要四十步采样的 Flow-OPD 模型。在 SD3.5-Medium 上，相比先做 DiffusionOPD 再做步蒸馏的两阶段流程，StepX-OPD 训练消耗约减少 37% GPU-hours。

Conclusion: 能力整合和少步采样不必分成两个阶段进行；通过让学生模型在自身 rollout 状态上接受多教师监督，并针对低噪端速度不匹配进行额外约束，可以同时提升少步文生图模型的综合能力和生成质量，并显著降低训练开销。该结果说明统一的多教师在线策略蒸馏与步数压缩是部署级扩散模型更有效的训练范式。

Abstract: A deployable text-to-image diffusion model must hold several capabilities at once, such as instruction following, text rendering and human preference, and must sample in a few steps. Capabilities and few-step sampling are built in separate stages: a full-step model first consolidates capabilities through multi-teacher on-policy distillation, then serves as a fixed teacher for step distillation. Every full-step model we evaluate scores lower once compressed, and the second stage cannot begin until the first finishes. We introduce StepX-OPD, which merges the two stages: one student acquires every capability while learning to sample in a few steps, so frozen specialists supervise it on the states its own rollout visits rather than on a longer trajectory it never runs. Because a decomposition of the rollout's KL weights velocity mismatch most heavily near the clean end, we also regress the student's velocity on the low-noise part of the rollout. Separately, we find that capability integration makes models write prompt words into images on prompts that never asked for text, and we give a variant that lowers it. We evaluate on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev across instruction following, text rendering, image quality and human preference. On both architectures, StepX-OPD attains the highest normalized average score among the four-step methods compared and exceeds the forty-step Flow-OPD model, and on SD3.5-Medium it trains with about 37% fewer GPU-hours than sequential DiffusionOPD followed by step distillation.

</details>


### [36] [Spin Spherical Fourier Neural Operators: Efficient Equivariant Learning for Vector and Spin Fields on the Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04389)
*Kevin Mulder,Matthew A. Price,Eirini Leftaki,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将球面傅里叶神经算子（SFNO）推广到任意自旋场，提出自旋等变的球面算子架构 S²FNO，通过旋转群上的调和表示与采样定理实现精确旋转等变，并在相关球面场建模任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理场定义在球面上并具有自旋性质，例如风场、宇宙微波背景偏振和弱引力透镜剪切场；这些量在局部参考系旋转时会发生相位变化，因此普通标量神经网络或标量算子难以天然保持几何对称性。为了更准确地建模球面上的物理过程，需要能够处理任意自旋场且具有旋转等变性的神经网络算子。

Method: 作者将自旋场提升到旋转群上的 Mackey 函数，以建立严格的旋转等变框架；在此基础上构造 lifted spherical convolutions，并利用球面及旋转群上的谐波表示与采样定理，实现精确且高效的计算。该框架将 SFNO 扩展到任意自旋场，最终构建出自旋球面傅里叶神经算子，即 S²FNO。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的 S²FNO 层能够在机器精度上满足旋转等变性，并在现有基准测试中达到最优性能。此外，作者引入新的基准，结果显示考虑自旋特性的模型相比仅处理标量场的模型表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过把自旋场表示为旋转群上的 Mackey 函数并利用球面谐波与采样定理，本文实现了可扩展到任意自旋场的旋转等变球面神经算子。S²FNO 不仅具有严格几何对称性，还提升了球面物理场建模的性能，表明自旋感知架构在等变科学机器学习中的价值。

Abstract: Many fields of physical interest on the sphere, such as wind, the polarisation of the cosmic microwave background and the shear of weak gravitational lensing, are spin fields, whose values change phase when the local frame rotates. We extend spherical Fourier neural operators (SFNOs) to fields of any spin, through lifted spherical convolutions that are exactly rotationally equivariant. Lifting spin fields to Mackey functions on the rotation group provides the conceptual framework, while computation is performed exactly and efficiently through their harmonic representation and by appealing to sampling theorems on the sphere and rotation group. We construct spin SFNOs (S$^2$FNOs) from these building blocks. We demonstrate empirically that rotational equivariance holds to machine precision for these layers. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks, and introduce new benchmarks on which spin-aware models outperform their scalar counterparts.

</details>


### [37] [Auditing Targetless Camera-LiDAR Extrinsic Correction before Recursive Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04397)
*Bo Ma,Weiqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究 Camera-LiDAR 融合中的“无标靶外参修正”是否应在通过分层审计后才被下游使用。基于 Argoverse 2 的合成外参漂移实验表明：单帧 Canny 对齐在轻微漂移下有害；多帧对齐虽改善严重漂移的旋转误差，但平移误差恶化，且联合误差量级改善显著为负；置信度/风险门控未形成可部署的非零覆盖工作点。最终保守策略几乎不接受任何修正，等价于原始参数，而“始终修正”反而增大轨迹误差。结论是该方法支持将校正、置信排序和递归污染分开审计，但未证明实际可用的标定门控或正面 SLAM/里程计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Camera-LiDAR 融合在投影、关联和递归状态估计中反复依赖外参。若用无标靶方法自动修正外参，错误校正可能以递归方式污染下游状态。因此需要回答：校正本身是否真正改善 6-DoF 外参？其置信度能否被可靠排序？在下游递归估计中是否会带来额外风险？而非简单地“检测到漂移就修正”。

Method: 作者提出 log-disjoint 协议，在 Argoverse 2 上注入合成外参漂移，并分阶段评估：1）单帧与多帧无标靶对齐质量；2）旋转、平移和联合误差量级改善；3）校正/置信排序/递归风险三类审计；4）用 solver-disagreement 的 AUROC 和 risk-coverage 检验是否能形成有效的修正准入门控。跨数据集与里程计实验作为有界诊断。最终在 15 条日志的系统级角色中比较 Raw、保守锁定策略和 Always-correct。

Result: 27 条测试日志显示：单帧 Canny 对齐在轻微漂移层级是有害的。多帧对齐在严重漂移层级将旋转误差改善 2.52°，但平移误差恶化 0.177 m；作者声明的联合误差量级改善仍为负，-0.158，95% CI [-0.223, -0.098]，因此不能视为 6-DoF 校正成功。零注入检查会使估计远离真实外参；solver-disagreement AUROC 未产生经过验证的、具有非零覆盖率的风险-覆盖工作点。在 15 条日志的最终系统评估中，锁定的保守策略不接受任何候选修正，效果等同于 Raw，而 Always-correct 会增加轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献在于提出并实证支持“校正质量、置信排序、递归风险”的可审计分离框架。它表明：无标靶 Camera-LiDAR 外参修正不能因为检测到漂移或局部旋转改善就自动注入下游；必须验证其联合 6-DoF 改善、风险覆盖门控和递归污染边界。当前证据不支持可部署的标定门控，也未证明里程计、SLAM、地图一致性或闭环安全方面的正向收益。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR fusion repeatedly uses an extrinsic transform in projection, association, and recursive state estimation. We study whether a targetless correction should be admitted to a downstream consumer only after separate audits of correction quality, confidence ranking, and recursive risk. Our log-disjoint protocol evaluates these stages under synthetic extrinsic drift on Argoverse 2 and treats cross-dataset and odometry experiments as bounded diagnostics. On 27 test-split logs, single-frame Canny alignment is harmful in the mild tier. Multi-frame alignment improves severe-tier rotation by 2.52 degrees but worsens translation by 0.177 m; its declared joint error-magnitude improvement is also negative (-0.158, 95% CI [-0.223, -0.098]), so the rotation gain is not a six-degree-of-freedom correction success. A zero-injection check moves away from the true extrinsic, and solver-disagreement AUROC on selected splits does not yield a validated nonzero-coverage risk-coverage operating point. In a 15-log final system role, the locked conservative policy admits no candidate and equals Raw, whereas Always-correct increases trajectory error. These results support an auditable separation of correction, confidence, and recursive contamination; they do not establish a deployable calibration gate, positive odometry or SLAM improvement, map consistency, or closed-loop safety.

</details>


### [38] [SphereFlow: Missing Modality Imputation via Geometric Transport on Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04414)
*Huihan Wang,Juncheng Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Wenlong Hou,Guoqi Yu,Chao Xu,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SphereFlow，通过将不同模态映射到共享超球面潜空间，把缺失模态填补转化为短距离几何传输，并利用几何感知的流匹配训练显著提升生成质量与下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失模态填补方法通常假设各模态位于独立潜流形上，迫使生成模型学习跨越多个模态空间的长距离、无结构映射，导致生成困难、路径偏离数据流形且解码器缺乏有效监督。

Method: 作者利用冻结的自监督编码器将所有模态投影到共享超球面潜空间，使跨模态填补变为球面上邻近点之间的短程传输；针对流匹配与球面几何的冲突，提出两项改进：一是用观测模态潜变量替代标准高斯噪声作为源分布，使速度场仅学习跨模态位移；二是采用时间加权损失训练解码器，使其覆盖流轨迹偏离球面附近的近球面区域。理论上，该“以数据为源”的表述将最坏情况下的离流形偏差从随潜变量维度增长降为由模态间相似度控制的小而维度无关的界。

Result: 在BraTS多模态MRI填补和CT-MRI转换任务上，SphereFlow在所有设置中取得最先进的生成保真度；使用其填补数据后，下游脑肿瘤分割性能接近全模态oracle，并缩小约百分之八十的性能差距。

Conclusion: 将跨模态填补重构为共享超球面空间中的短程几何传输，并针对流匹配引入源分布与解码器监督的几何感知修正，可有效提升缺失模态生成的稳定性和质量，同时带来显著的下游任务增益。

Abstract: Missing modality imputation aims to synthesize unobserved modalities from observed ones. Existing methods treat each modality as living on its own latent manifold, forcing the generator to learn a long, unstructured jump between them. We present SphereFlow, which takes a fundamentally different view: by projecting all modalities into a shared hyperspherical latent space via a frozen self-supervised encoder, cross-modal imputation reduces to short-range geometric transport between nearby points on the sphere. Realizing this idea with flow matching, however, exposes two geometric conflicts: the standard Gaussian noise source is catastrophically mismatched with the unit-norm data manifold, and the linear interpolation path departs the sphere surface into a region where the decoder has no training signal. We resolve both with simple, geometry-aware modifications: replacing the noise source with the observed-modality latent so that the velocity field learns only the cross-modal displacement, and training the decoder with a time-weighted loss that extends its domain to the near-surface region traversed by the flow. We prove that this data-as-source formulation reduces the worst-case off-manifold deviation from a quantity that grows with the latent dimension to a small, dimension-independent bound governed by inter-modality similarity. Experiments on BraTS multi-modal MRI imputation and CT-MRI translation demonstrate state-of-the-art generation fidelity across all settings, while downstream brain tumor segmentation with our imputed data closes roughly eighty percent of the gap to the full-modality oracle. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [39] [Efficient and Faithful High-Resolution Image Editing with Sparse Cross-Resolution Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04416)
*Maosen Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Pengtao Chen,Peng Ye,Gang Yu,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLASH提出一种面向高分辨率图像编辑的稀疏跨分辨率去噪架构，在同一个Transformer中联合使用冻结的1K语义结构分支与目标分辨率细节分支，以较低计算成本实现2K/4K的高保真编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令驱动图像编辑器主要在1K分辨率运行，而实际应用要求忠实的2K/4K输出。直接高分辨率密集注意会显著增加目标参考注意成本；先在1K编辑再外部增强则会削弱细节恢复与编辑轨迹之间的耦合。

Method: FLASH基于稀疏跨分辨率去噪：冻结1K分支提供语义与结构锚点；目标分辨率分支在同一Transformer中重建局部细节。采用非对称拓扑：保留完整1K注意，用空间对齐的低分辨率噪声目标状态指导高分辨率查询，并将高分辨率目标参考交互限制在局部Halo邻域内。通过分辨率特定扩散状态映射和空间YaRN适配预训练编辑器到更大网格，还提供可选的免训练渐进式推理路径，以1K前缀替代早期高分辨率去噪并在同一编辑器中完成细化。

Result: 在VINS-4KEval上，直接模型在2K和4K下的结构保真度优于比较方法。固定批量大小为1的4K前向测试中，相比密集目标参考注意，FLASH总前向执行快4.2倍，注意计算快25.3倍。渐进式推理可将4K端到端延迟降低30.5%，仅带来轻微质量损失。

Conclusion: FLASH为高分辨率指令图像编辑提供了兼顾语义忠实、结构一致和局部细节的高分辨率原生编辑方案，并通过稀疏跨分辨率注意与渐进推理显著降低4K编辑的计算开销。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors still primarily operate at 1K, whereas practical editing increasingly requires faithful 2K/4K outputs. Dense high-resolution adaptation preserves the editing trajectory but incurs expensive target-source attention; editing at 1K followed by external refinement is cheaper but can separate detail restoration from the editing trajectory. We introduce FLASH, a native high-resolution editor based on sparse cross-resolution denoising. A frozen 1K branch provides a semantic and structural anchor, while a target-resolution branch reconstructs local detail within the same Transformer. An asymmetric topology retains full 1K attention, guides high-resolution queries with spatially aligned low-resolution noisy-target states, and confines high-resolution target-reference interaction to local Halo neighborhoods. Resolution-specific diffusion-state mapping and spatial YaRN adapt the pretrained editor to larger grids. An optional, training-free progressive path replaces early high-resolution denoising with a 1K prefix and completes refinement in the same editor. On VINS-4KEval, the direct model achieves the strongest structural fidelity among the compared methods at both 2K and 4K. A separate 4K forward benchmark with R = 1 records 4.2x faster total forward execution and 25.3x faster attention than dense target-reference attention. Progressive inference reduces end-to-end 4K latency by 30.5%, with a modest quality cost.

</details>


### [40] [VidUEU-Agent: A Data-Curation Agent for Multimodal Understanding, Editing, and Unified Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04418)
*Tengju Ru,Weitong Lian,Zecong Tang,Lingyi Meng,Haoran Li,Zhejun Cui,Yichen Zhu,Hangshuo Cao,Qi Kang,Yechi Liu,Kaixuan Wang,Yu-Jie Yuan,Chunwei Wang,Yu Zhang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VidUEU-Agent，一个从原始视频自动合成高质量指令数据的代理式流程，用于训练多模态理解、编辑与统一任务模型，并基于该流程构建 VidUEU-100K 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型发展受限于高质量指令数据稀缺和人工标注成本高。原始视频虽然数量庞大且信息丰富，但难以直接转化为精确、可训练的指令数据，因此需要自动化数据合成方法。

Method: 作者设计四阶段自动化流水线：先进行全局感知并过滤低质量镜头，再挖掘关键帧和多模态信号，随后构建元数据并路由生成指令样本。该代理框架还能根据下游训练效果自动迭代数据分布，从而优化数据质量、任务平衡和训练效果。最终构建包含 10 万条训练样本、覆盖多个领域和任务的 VidUEU-100K 数据集。

Result: 在多个代表性基准上，使用 VidUEU-100K 微调基线模型能够带来稳定且显著的性能提升，验证了 VidUEU-Agent 数据合成框架和所构建数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: VidUEU-Agent 通过将原始视频转化为多模态指令数据，为多模态理解、编辑和统一任务模型提供了可扩展、自动化且可自我优化的数据构建方案，有助于缓解高质量标注数据不足的问题。

Abstract: The advancement of multimodal learning is heavily constrained by the scarcity and high annotation cost of high-quality instruction data. While raw videos offer an abundant and dynamic source of visual knowledge, transforming them into precise instruction data remains highly challenging. To bridge this gap, we present VidUEU-Agent, an automated pipeline that synthesizes instruction data from raw videos for multimodal understanding, editing, and unified tasks. VidUEU-Agent follows a four-stage pipeline: it performs global perception to filter low-quality shots, mines keyframes and multimodal signals, constructs metadata and routes the metadata to synthesize instruction samples. More importantly, our agent framework can autonomously iterate the data distribution based on downstream training results, thereby optimizing data quality, balance, and training effectiveness. Scaling this pipeline, we construct VidUEU-100K, a large-scale dataset of 100K training samples spanning multiple domains and tasks, providing a robust foundation for developing comprehensive multimodal capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning baseline models on VidUEU-100K yields significant and consistent performance gains across diverse representative benchmarks, validating the effectiveness of our proposed agent framework and dataset.

</details>


### [41] [GVT3D: Gesture-Vision-Text Token Enrichment for 3D Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04422)
*Vimarsh Shah,Dulanga Weerakoon,Ajinkya Deshpande,Saksham Agarwal,Nilesh Bhatia,Neel Kiran Naik,Parth Shah,Sravan Danda,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Vigneshwaran Subbaraju,Sougata Sen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GVT3D，用于3D具身指代理解：在包含人体指向手势、语言描述和场景点云的3D环境中定位目标物体。其核心是通过统一嵌入空间中的多模态融合和针对指向区域的选择性点云处理，提升准确率并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然的人机交互要求机器人结合语言、手势和3D场景信息完成取放、物体检索等任务。现有3D Embodied Reference Understanding模型虽然能够根据人体指向和语言描述从点云场景中定位目标，但存在语言、手势与视觉表征之间对齐不足的问题，并且处理成本会随点云规模增大而显著上升。因此需要一种既能提升跨模态对齐、又能降低点云计算开销的方法。

Method: 作者提出GVT3D模型。该方法包含两个关键设计：一是具身融合模块，将语言、手势和视觉输入在共享嵌入空间中进行联合建模，从而增强不同模态之间的表征对齐；二是引入中央凹式选择性处理机制，根据估计的指向方向，仅对粗粒度指向区域附近的点云进行精细处理，减少无关点云区域带来的计算负担，提升整体推理效率。

Result: 相较于先前3D具身指代理解模型，GVT3D在准确率上提升约8%。同时，中央凹式点云处理策略能够在保持相近精度的前提下，使模型执行速度提升约2.95倍，体现出该方法在精度与效率之间的更好平衡。

Conclusion: GVT3D通过共享嵌入空间中的多模态联合建模改善语言、手势和3D视觉信息的一致性，并通过指向区域选择性计算降低大规模点云处理成本。结果表明，该方法能够更准确、高效地支持机器人从人体指向和语言描述中定位目标物体，为实际场景中的自然、可扩展人机交互提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Natural human-robot interaction requires robots to interpret language and gestures within a 3D environment to support tasks such as pick-and-place and object retrieval. 3D Embodied Reference Understanding (3D-ERU) supports this by identifying the 3D location of a target object from a scene (point-cloud) containing a human pointing gesture and an accompanying language description. However, current 3D-ERU models lack alignment of cross-modal representations and incur high processing costs that increase with the point-cloud's size. To address this, we propose GVT3D, which employs an Embodied Fusion module that jointly models language, gesture, and vision inputs within a shared embedding space, promoting cross-modal alignment. We additionally introduce a foveation approach that selectively processes the point cloud within a coarse region around the estimated pointing direction to reduce computations. GVT3D achieves ~8% higher accuracy and foveation enables 2.95x faster execution while maintaining comparable accuracy, relative to prior 3D-ERU models.

</details>


### [42] [SWAP-INR: Implicit Neural Representation with Switchable Attention Paths for Time-varying Multivariate Scientific Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04423)
*Hyunsoo Son,Jeonghyun Noh,Chaoli Wang,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SWAP-INR，一种面向多变量、时变科学模拟数据的高保真隐式神经表示压缩方法，通过可切换注意力路径和自适应SIREN模块提升压缩质量并控制内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量科学模拟数据规模巨大，在高性能计算环境中带来严重存储瓶颈；现有隐式神经表示方法难以显式建模变量间相互作用，也难以适应不同物理变量多样化的频谱特征。

Method: 提出SWAP-INR架构，包含两个核心组件：其一为可切换注意力路径模块，利用跨变量注意力层显式建模变量交互，并通过确定性的、由损失驱动的标准将每个变量路由至注意力路径或非注意力路径，同时剪枝非活跃层以实现结构压缩；其二为可适应性增强SIREN模块，用通道级可学习调制替代固定频率缩放，以低额外开销适应异质物理特征。训练后结合16位量化进一步压缩表示。

Result: 在小堆模化反应堆数据集上取得最先进的高保真压缩性能，在公开物理模拟数据集上保持竞争力，同时维持受约束的内存占用。

Conclusion: SWAP-INR通过显式建模跨变量交互和自适应频谱表示，有效缓解了科学模拟数据压缩中内存占用与重建质量之间的矛盾，为大规模多变量时变模拟数据提供了实用的隐式神经压缩方案。

Abstract: Visualizing multivariate scientific simulation data is essential for understanding complex physical phenomena, yet the massive scale of such data imposes a severe storage bottleneck in high-performance computing environments. Implicit neural representations (INRs) have emerged as a promising continuous alternative to traditional lossy compressors, but existing methods struggle to explicitly capture cross-variable interactions and to adapt to the diverse spectral characteristics of physical variables. To address these limitations, we propose SWAP-INR, an architecture for high-fidelity compression of multivariate, time-varying simulation data. SWAP-INR introduces two key components: (1) The switchable attention path (SWAP) module explicitly models cross-variable interactions through a cross-variable attention layer, then routes each variable to an attention or non-attention path by a deterministic, loss-driven criterion, and prunes the inactive layers for structural compression. (2) The adaptability amplification SIREN (A2-SIREN) replaces rigid frequency scaling with channel-wise learnable modulation, adapting to heterogeneous physical features with negligible overhead. Combined with post-training 16-bit quantization, SWAP-INR achieves state-of-the-art performance on small modular reactor (SMR) datasets and remains competitive on public physics simulation datasets, while maintaining a constrained memory footprint. Our code is this https URL.

</details>


### [43] [A Systematic Point Cloud Edge Detection Framework for Automatic Aircraft Skin Milling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04435)
*Zijie Wu,Yaonan Wang,He Xie,Mingtao Feng,Haotian Wu,Chao Ding,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向航空制造中飞机蒙皮自动铣边任务的边缘检测框架，区别于传统仅提取几何边缘的方法，通过空间切线连续性构造边缘概率，并结合分层分支搜索从原始点云中提取适合铣削的目标边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空制造中，飞机蒙皮边缘检测是后续铣削加工的重要步骤。现有边缘检测方法多面向传统边缘提取任务，通常只关注几何边缘的识别，而忽略了铣削加工对边缘连续性、完整性、可跟踪性和工艺可用性的系统性要求，因此难以直接满足自动铣边需求。

Method: 本文提出一个面向自动铣边的飞机蒙皮边缘检测框架。首先，设计基于空间切线连续性（spatial tangent continuity, STC）的边缘概率检测器，用于为边缘提取提供可靠的参考依据。其次，提出分层分支搜索（hierarchical branch searching, HBS）方法，从原始点云中逐级剥离出目标铣削边缘。该方法包含三个渐进阶段：分支主干生成、分支扩展和分支修剪，以逐步筛选并保留符合铣削需求的连续边缘结构。

Result: 在合成模型和飞机蒙皮工件上的实验表明，所提方法优于其他基线方法，能够检测出更准确、更符合铣削任务需求的边缘。结果验证了该方法在自动铣边应用中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究将边缘检测从一般几何边缘提取问题拓展到面向飞机蒙皮铣削工艺的系统性边缘获取问题。通过STC提供边缘参考并结合HBS进行分层筛选，所提方法能够稳定获得适合自动铣削的目标边缘，为航空制造中的蒙皮自动加工提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The edge detection technique is an essential step for aircraft skin milling in aviation manufacturing. Most of the current detection methods focus on traditionally defined edge extraction tasks but disregard the crucial systematic requirement of edge milling. In this paper, we proposed a novel edge detection framework for automatic edge milling of aircraft skins. First, an edge probability detector is proposed by the spatial tangent continuity (STC) to provide the essential reference. Second, we propose a hierarchical branch searching (HBS) method to hierarchically strip the desired milling edges from the raw point cloud, which consists of three graded progressive steps: branch backbone generation, branch extension, and branch pruning. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed method on both synthetic models and aircraft skin workpieces. The proposed method outperforms the other baselines and shows accurate edges for the edge milling task.

</details>


### [44] [Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification for Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04438)
*Riasad Alvi,Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Md. Ahashan Habib,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出熵门控动态纠偏方法EGDR，利用生成过程中每步 logits 的预测熵作为局部、因果可得的控制信号，动态调节潜在引导的干预阈值，从而在单次前向推理下降低大型视觉语言模型的物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型常将图像中不存在的物体“命名”出来，造成幻觉。已有潜在引导方法可在一次前向推理中减少此类错误，但使用固定阈值对所有解码步骤一视同仁，忽视了生成过程中熵的时间差异：自由生成时熵波动明显，而短答案状态往往处于窄而自信的区域。因此需要按 token 级不确定性动态控制干预时机。

Method: 作者提出 Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification（EGDR），不额外增加模型前向次数。方法基于已有 static steering gate，仅将固定干预阈值替换为由前一步 logits 计算得到的熵驱动的动态阈值：当预测熵高时降低阈值、加强干预；当熵低时保持校准后的操作点，避免过度干预。该控制信号是 token-local 且 causally available，适合自回归生成。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上，static gate 将 CHAIR CS/CI 从 53.00/15.30 降到 32.00/14.30；EGDR 进一步降到 29.00/13.60。在匹配 58% 干预率时，static control 为 30.50/14.00，说明收益并非来自简单增加干预次数。EGDR 在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上同样有效，且生成预算越大增益越明显；POPE 分数和通用多模态能力基本保持不变。额外延迟约 2%，无需额外前向计算。

Conclusion: EGDR 表明，生成过程中的预测熵可作为有效的时间感知控制信号，使潜在引导干预从固定阈值变为动态、逐步、因果可用的门控机制。该方法以极低额外成本降低 LVLM 物体幻觉，同时保持其他多模态能力，具有实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) routinely name objects that are absent from the image. Latent steering can reduce this failure in one model forward pass, but a fixed gate treats early and late decoding steps by the same criterion. We observe that predictive entropy varies sharply within free-running generation, while states that commit to short answers occupy a narrow, confident band. We exploit this temporal mismatch by using uncertainty as a causally available, token-local control signal for an existing steering gate. We propose Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification (EGDR), which lowers the intervention threshold when entropy derived from the preceding step's already available logits is high and retains the calibrated operating point when it is low. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, the underlying static gate lowers CHAIR CS/CI from 53.00/15.30 to 32.00/14.30; replacing only its constant threshold with EGDR further lowers them to 29.00/13.60. At a matched 58% intervention rate, the corresponding static control obtains 30.50/14.00. The gain over the static gate holds on LLaVA-NeXT-7B and grows with the generation budget, while POPE scores and general multimodal capability remain effectively unchanged. EGDR requires no additional model forward pass and adds roughly 2% measured latency. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [45] [DynOSeg: Dynamic Object Segmentation via Motion-Geometry Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04452)
*Xu Han,Yiduo Wang,Yiran Wang,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出 DynOSeg：一种面向运动物体分割的两阶段融合方法，先融合几何残差图与深度编码形成运动的几何表示，再引入语义特征进行细化，并构建包括 CARLA-MOS 的多样化训练数据集，实验表明其优于现有 SOTA 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动物体分割方法主要依赖纯视觉运动线索，如光流或点轨迹，但受相机运动、物体运动以及阴影、反射等外观变化影响，存在固有歧义；简单融合深度和语义信息也难以充分利用其互补性。

Method: 提出 DynOSeg，采用两阶段融合结构。第一阶段将几何感知的残差图与深度编码结合，构建用于刻画相机运动和动态物体的几何运动表示。第二阶段引入语义特征，对物体理解进行细化，以提升运动物体分割的准确性。此外，作者构建了多样化训练语料，其中包含面向 MOS 训练的新合成城市场景数据集 CARLA-MOS。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，DynOSeg 能够持续超越已发表的先进运动物体分割方法。论文还公开了代码和预训练模型。

Conclusion: DynOSeg 证明了将几何运动表示与语义特征进行结构化两阶段融合，比单纯依赖视觉运动线索或朴素融合额外信息更有效，可提升动态场景中的运动物体分割性能。

Abstract: Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) detects and separates dynamic objects from the static background in a video, which plays a fundamental role in dynamic-scene understanding. Most MOS methods rely on pure visual motion cues alone, such as optical flow or point tracks, which is inherently ambiguous due to the compounded camera and object motions as well as motion-induced appearance changes, e.g. shadows and reflections. Naive fusion of additional information such as depth and semantics, however, cannot leverage their complementarity to the fullest extent. We therefore propose DynOSeg, a novel MOS approach that takes on a two-stage fusion structure. DynOSeg first combines a geometry-aware residual map and depth encoding into a geometric representation of motion, delineating camera motions and object dynamics. We then further incorporate semantic features to refine object understanding. We have additionally curated a diverse training corpus including CARLA-MOS, a new synthetic urban-driving dataset for MOS training. Our experiments show that DynOSeg consistently outperforms published state-of-the-art MOS methods across a diverse range of datasets. Our code and pretrained models can be found at this https URL

</details>


### [46] [Is Forgetting Unified? Exploring Concept Unlearning in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04467)
*Zhongkai Xue,Junkai Chen,Mingcong Lei,Rumo Zhang,Yixiao Liu,Zhimin Sun,Shu Wu,Qiang Liu,Lihua Zhang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究统一多模态模型中的跨路径概念遗忘：不仅阻止模型生成目标概念图像，也阻止其在视觉理解中识别该概念，并通过 UUBench 和 UniForget 评估与实现联合遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单路径遗忘方法通常只针对生成侧或理解侧，无法充分验证或实现统一多模态模型在图像生成和视觉理解两条路径上同时遗忘目标概念，因此需要研究跨路径概念遗忘。

Method: 作者提出诊断性基准 UUBench，覆盖 30 个目标概念和 60 个语义相关邻居概念；随后提出 UniForget 方法，以同时抑制目标概念的生成与识别，并在代表性统一多模态模型上评估联合遗忘与能力保持。

Result: 实验表明，单路径生成编辑方法虽能较强抑制目标图像生成，但视觉理解侧遗忘有限；理解侧基线甚至难以在自身路径实现充分遗忘，直接组合也效果有限。UniForget 在 BAGEL-7B-MoT 上实现 80.0%、在 Janus-Pro-7B 上实现 92.7% 的联合遗忘率，并优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: 跨路径概念遗忘需要同时约束生成和理解能力；UUBench 提供了有效评估工具，UniForget 能提升联合遗忘效果，但遗忘与通用能力保持之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate image generation and visual understanding, but are existing single-pathway unlearning methods sufficient to suppress target concepts across both? In this work, we study cross-pathway concept unlearning. Our goal is to prevent a model from both generating images of a target concept and identifying it in input images, while preserving other capabilities. To this end, we introduce UUBench, a diagnostic benchmark covering 30 target concepts and 60 semantically related neighboring concepts. Experiments on representative UMMs show that the evaluated single-pathway methods achieve limited joint forgetting: generation-side editors strongly suppress target generation but yield limited forgetting in visual understanding, while understanding-side baselines struggle to achieve substantial forgetting even in their own pathway. Their direct composition also yields limited joint forgetting. We therefore propose UniForget, achieving joint forgetting rates of 80.0% on BAGEL-7B-MoT and 92.7% on Janus-Pro-7B, outperforming representative baselines. Further analysis reveals trade-offs between joint forgetting and capability preservation.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond Token Representations: Explicit Visual Object Grounding for Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04472)
*Jun Huang,Junyang Zhang,Zijie Yue,Bingkun Wang,Yong Luo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对视频推理分割（VRS）中现有分割 token 语义单一、空间模糊的问题，提出显式视觉对象 grounding 框架，通过多模态大模型生成并精炼边界框和点集等空间提示，引入多样化查询及其一致性对齐，并选取可靠关键帧辅助掩码解码与传播，在八个数据集上显著超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理分割需要根据带有推理语义的查询定位并分割视频目标。现有方法先生成少量 segmentation token 再送入掩码解码器，但这些 token 在语义表达和空间定位能力上不足，难以充分刻画目标，导致掩码预测受限。

Method: 作者提出显式视觉对象 grounding 框架。首先设计空间感知提示生成与精炼机制，利用多模态大语言模型生成目标对应的边界框和点集，并通过多步对话进行优化；随后引入查询多样化与对齐模块，从不同视角描述同一目标并生成辅助查询，通过跨查询空间提示一致性缓解查询表达不足造成的训练偏差；最后基于提示选择若干可靠关键帧，用于支撑掩码解码和跨帧传播。

Result: 在八个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比现有 SOTA 视频推理分割方法取得了显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式空间提示、多样化查询对齐和关键帧选择能够有效提升视频推理分割中的目标定位与掩码预测能力，证明了显式视觉对象 grounding 对 VRS 的有效性。

Abstract: Video Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to infer and segment target objects specified by reasoning queries in videos. Existing methods typically first infer the target and generate a few segmentation tokens for it, which are then fed into a mask decoder for mask prediction. However, such segmentation tokens tend to be both semantically monotonous and spatially ambiguous, making them largely insufficient to represent the target to guide mask prediction. In this paper, we propose an explicit visual object grounding framework for VRS. Specifically, we first propose a spatial-aware prompt generation and refinement scheme, in which we query a multimodal large language model to generate explicit spatial prompts (i.e., bounding box and point set) for the target and then refine them through multi-step dialogue. Furthermore, we introduce a query diversification and alignment module to generate auxiliary queries that describe the same target from different perspectives. We then enforce cross-query consistency between their predicted spatial prompts to alleviate the training bias caused by query limitations. Finally, we select several reliable frames using a prompt-based keyframe selection strategy to support mask decoding and propagation. Extensive experiments on eight datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [48] [KeyWise: Efficient Video-Only Piano Transcription with a Lightweight Per-Key Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04476)
*Anastasios Chatzigiannis,Natalia Panagou,Maria Koziri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 KeyWise，一种轻量级逐键钢琴视频转录模型：将键盘划分为 88 个等尺寸 ROI，并用共享的小型循环网络独立跟踪每个琴键的时间变化。该方法在保持高起始音准确度的同时显著减少参数并加速，在跨数据集上也能取得可比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于音频的钢琴转录容易受到延音踏板、噪声或缺失音轨影响；视频转录可通过俯视键盘直接观察按键状态，但已有方法通常用全局特征描述整个键盘，需要额外学习每个键的位置，效率较低。

Method: 利用已知键盘几何结构，将校正后的键盘等分为 88 个 ROI，每个琴键对应一个区域；使用一个小型循环网络在所有键之间共享，逐键建模按键与释放的时间动态。

Result: 在 PianoVAM 上，KeyWise 达到 95.0% 起始音 F1@50ms（V2N 为 94.7%）、97.8% F1@100ms（V2N 为 97.0%），仅用 1.3M 参数而非 28.2M，并在同一 GPU 上快 4.7 倍；在包含键释放或力度正确性评估时也更准确。在 PianoYT 上，经 PianoVAM 预训练后达到 69.4% 起始音 F1@100ms，接近最佳已发表结果，但新录制条件下仍需微调。

Conclusion: 先验键盘结构加上逐键共享循环网络能够显著提升视频钢琴转录的效率和准确性；但面对新的采集场景，仍需要一定程度的微调或域适应。

Abstract: Audio-based piano transcription is hampered by the sustain pedal and by noisy or missing soundtracks, problems that video-only transcription avoids, since a top-view video shows directly when each key is pressed and released. Most visual transcription networks, however, describe the whole keyboard with one global feature vector and must learn where each key is. In this paper, we propose KeyWise, a lightweight per-key architecture that exploits the known geometry of the keyboard instead of learning it. The rectified keyboard is divided into 88 equal tiles that serve as regions of interest (ROIs), and one small recurrent network, shared by all keys, follows each key over time. On PianoVAM, KeyWise reaches the onset accuracy of the state-of-the-art V2N (95.0% vs. 94.7% onset F1 at 50 ms and 97.8% vs. 97.0% at 100 ms) with 1.3 M instead of 28.2 M parameters, and it runs 4.7 times faster on the same GPU. At 100 ms, it is also significantly more accurate when the key releases (97.0% vs. 95.5% F1) or the velocities (89.4% vs. 84.5% F1) must be correct as well. On PianoYT, KeyWise pre-trained on PianoVAM reaches 69.4% onset F1 at 100 ms, comparable to the best published result, although it needs fine-tuning for new recording conditions.

</details>


### [49] [Bridging CLIP with DINO: Cross-Modal Information Maximization for Online Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04489)
*Taehoon Kim,Wonjun Hwang,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对CLIP等视觉-语言模型在测试时图像退化下出现的“模态偏置”和熵最小化导致的自我强化错误问题，提出一种三层次在线测试时自适应方法CDIM，通过冻结自监督主干与CLIP互补、带门控的信息最大化优化以及在线logit调整来修正预测分布，提升鲁棒分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP能够利用任意类别名进行零样本分类，但在测试时图像损坏下，视觉编码器会将失真特征高置信地投影到错误的文本锚点上，形成“模态偏置”。若继续采用标准熵最小化作为测试时自适应目标，模型会进一步强化这些高置信错误预测，造成确认偏误循环，并导致预测标签分布坍缩，从而削弱适应效果。因此，需要在表示、优化和预测三个层面打破这一循环，同时抑制文本锚点带来的偏置并维持预测多样性。

Method: 提出在线测试时自适应方法CDIM，从三个层面设计组件：
1. 表示层面：将冻结的自监督视觉主干（如DINO v2）与CLIP结合，使用交叉协方差logit集成。具体地，将CLIP文本原型投影到无文本监督特征的自主干特征空间中，使自监督分支直接基于原生图像特征进行分类，从而降低CLIP文本锚点偏差的影响。
2. 优化层面：采用带门控的测试时信息最大化目标，结合逐样本预测锐化与batch级预测多样性约束；同时通过自适应门控机制将不可靠样本排除在梯度更新之外，避免错误高置信预测污染适应过程。
3. 预测层面：引入在线logit调整，修正残余的类别分布偏斜，缓解长时间测试时自适应中的预测坍缩问题。

Result: CDIM在ImageNet-C、CIFAR10-C和CIFAR100-C等损坏测试集上优于对比的CLIP在线测试时自适应基线，并在持续型TTA和混合域TTA场景中保持有竞争力。大量分析与消融实验表明，表示层面的CLIP-自监督集成、优化层面的信息最大化与门控机制、预测层面的logit调整等组件均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心结论是：CLIP在测试时损坏下的关键瓶颈不仅来自特征失真，还来自文本锚点诱导的模态偏置，以及熵最小化带来的高置信错误强化。CDIM通过利用无文本监督的自监督视觉表示、门控信息最大化和在线分布校正，打破了确认偏误循环，实现了更稳定、更鲁棒的在线测试时自适应。该方向表明，将CLIP与自监督视觉主干互补结合，是提升视觉-语言模型在分布偏移和图像退化条件下可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP enable zero-shot classification from arbitrary class names, but under test-time corruptions, their visual encoder projects distorted features onto incorrect textual anchors with high confidence: a phenomenon we term modality bias. Standard entropy minimization reinforces these overconfident errors, which creates a confirmation-bias loop and collapses the predicted label distribution. We propose an online test-time adaptation (TTA) method that breaks this loop at three levels. At the representation level, a frozen self-supervised backbone (e.g., DINO v2) is combined with CLIP through a cross-covariance logit ensemble. CLIP text prototypes are projected into the feature space of this text-free backbone, so the self-supervised branch classifies native image features and is less affected by the text bias of CLIP. At the optimization level, a gated test-time information-maximization objective pairs per-sample sharpening with batch-level diversity, while an adaptive gate excludes unreliable samples from the gradient. At the prediction level, an online logit adjustment corrects the residual class-distribution skew. CDIM outperforms the compared CLIP-based online TTA baselines on ImageNet-C, CIFAR10-C, and CIFAR100-C, and remains competitive in continual and mixed-domain TTA. Extensive analyses and ablation studies verify the contribution of each of its components. Our code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [50] [Polarimetric Scattering-Structure Contrastive Learning for Few-Shot HRRP Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04490)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出极化散射结构对比学习（PSS-CL），利用可解释的物理散射信息增强高分辨距离像（HRRP）的少样本识别，在实测六类数据的跨方位角少样本任务中显著提升分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRRP 识别受目标散射特性随方位角变化强烈以及新类别样本极少的影响，常规少样本方法难以稳定提取与物理结构一致、跨视角鲁棒的表示。因此需要一种能将雷达极化散射机制、距离位置与能量贡献等结构化先验引入表征学习的方法。

Method: 提出 PSS-CL：首先将 HRRP 中的主要局部响应映射为到典型极化散射机制、归一化距离位置和能量贡献的软亲和关系，构建物理可解释的训练视角；随后采用对称跨视角对比目标，将该散射结构知识迁移到 HRRP 表征中。推理阶段去除物理分支，仅依靠 episodic prototype learning 对新类别进行分类。实验在实测六类数据上设置 Random-aspect 与 Cross-aspect 两种少样本场景。

Result: 在 Cross-aspect 1-shot、3-shot、5-shot 识别任务中，PSS-CL 的平均类别准确率（MCA）分别达到 81.73%、86.92% 和 90.98%，相比最佳基线分别提升 5.62%、3.09% 和 3.56%。

Conclusion: 将极化散射结构作为对比学习的辅助物理视角，可有效缓解 HRRP 少样本识别中的方位角敏感和新类支持样本稀疏问题，提升跨视角泛化能力，为雷达目标识别中的少样本学习提供了结构化先验利用的新途径。

Abstract: Few-shot high-resolution range profile (HRRP) recognition is challenged by strong aspect-dependent variation and sparse novel-class support. We propose polarimetric scattering-structure contrastive learning (PSS-CL), which supplies an episodic HRRP encoder with a structured physical training view. Dominant local responses are represented by their soft affinities to canonical polarimetric mechanisms, normalized range locations, and energy contributions. A symmetric cross-view objective transfers this scattering structure to the learned HRRP representation, while episodic prototype learning supports novel-class inference without the physical branch. We conduct Random-aspect and Cross-aspect experiments on a six-class measured dataset. In Cross-aspect 1-, 3-, and 5-shot recognition, PSS-CL achieves 81.73%, 86.92%, and 90.98% mean class accuracy (MCA), respectively, outperforming the best competing results by 5.62%, 3.09%, and 3.56%.

</details>


### [51] [SPA-Q: Structure-Preserving Adaptive Post-Training Quantization for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04491)
*Jaemin Choi,Jincheol Yang,Nahyun Lim,Yun-Seong Jeong,Matti Zinke,Hyunwoo Yu,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SPA-Q，一种面向单目深度估计（MDE）的结构保持自适应训练后量化（PTQ）框架，重点解决 Depth Anything 类 Transformer 模型在低比特量化中因注意力误差传播和层间特征分布偏移导致的性能下降问题；在 4-bit 量化下，该方法在 NYUv2 和 KITTI 上平均将 AbsRel 降低 25.9%、将 δ1 提升 17.3%，优于已有 PTQ 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Depth Anything 等基于 Transformer 的单目深度估计基础模型泛化能力强，但计算与内存开销大，难以高效部署。PTQ 是实用压缩方案，但低比特 PTQ 在 MDE 模型上仍困难：一方面，仅独立最小化 Query 和 Key 投影量化误差不能保持二者交互产生的注意力分布；另一方面，量化误差会跨层累积，造成中间特征分布偏移，从而影响深度预测精度。

Method: 提出 SPA-Q，包含两个核心模块。第一是注意力保持校准（APC），用于确定 Query 和 Key 的量化参数，其目标不是单独逼近原始权重输出，而是匹配全精度模型中的完整注意力分布，从而保持注意力结构。第二是通道级分布对齐（CWDA），通过学习逐通道的仿射变换来补偿量化引起的中间特征分布偏移；校准完成后，这些学习参数可吸收进权重中，因此不会在推理阶段引入额外计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，在 Depth Anything 等 MDE 模型上，SPA-Q 在 4-bit 量化场景下优于现有 PTQ 方法。在 NYUv2 和 KITTI 数据集上，平均 AbsRel 降低 25.9%，δ1 指标提升 17.3%，说明该方法能较好地在低比特压缩下保持深度估计精度。

Conclusion: 论文证明，针对基于 Transformer 的单目深度估计基础模型，低比特 PTQ 的关键不只是降低线性层或投影层的直接量化误差，更要保持注意力交互结构并抑制层间分布漂移。SPA-Q 通过注意力保持校准和通道级分布对齐实现结构敏感的自适应量化，为 Depth Anything 等 MDE 模型的高效低比特部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) has advanced rapidly with the emergence of foundation models such as Depth Anything. Their transformer-based architectures provide strong generalization across diverse scenes and domains, but also incur high computational and memory cost, making efficient deployment challenging. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) provides an efficient and practical solution for model compression, yet low-bit PTQ remains challenging for MDE models. When applying PTQ to Depth Anything, we identify two key challenges: (i) independently minimizing the quantization error of the query and key projections fails to preserve the attention maps induced by their interaction, and (ii) quantization errors accumulate across layers, resulting in intermediate feature distribution shifts. To address these issues, we propose SPA-Q, a Structure-Preserving Adaptive PTQ framework for MDE, consisting of two main components. First, Attention-Preserving Calibration (APC) determines query and key quantization parameters by matching the full-precision attention distribution. Second, Channel-Wise Distribution Alignment (CWDA) learns channel-wise affine transformations to mitigate quantization-induced distribution shifts, and the learned parameters are absorbed into the weights after training. Experimental results show that SPA-Q consistently outperforms existing PTQ methods under 4-bit quantization, achieving an average 25.9% reduction in AbsRel and a 17.3% improvement in $\delta_1$ across NYUv2 and KITTI datasets.

</details>


### [52] [3DGS-VBench+ and 3DGSV-Assessor: A Multi-Dimensional Subjective Benchmark and a Reference-Flexible Objective Model for 3D Gaussian Splatting Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04498)
*Yuke Xing,William Gordon,Jiarui Wang,Yujie Zhang,Guangtao Zhai,Yiling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出面向3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）视频的多维质量评估基准3DGS-VBench+和参考灵活的评估模型3DGSV-Assessor，可在缺乏对齐视频真值、仅有稀疏参考视图的条件下，对整体质量、几何质量和颜色质量进行联合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现实时新视角合成，但其存储开销大，需要压缩；训练与压缩过程会引入与表示方式相关的失真。许多生成式压缩方法对几何和颜色属性采用不同参数处理，可能导致不同维度上的失真不一致，因此需要多维质量评估。同时，真实场景中很难获得与连续合成相机轨迹严格对齐的真值视频，只能依赖稀疏采集视角，使现有视频质量评估方法难以直接应用。

Method: 作者首先构建大规模主观视频质量评估基准3DGS-VBench+，包含3,036个3DGS重建及其渲染视频，覆盖11个真实场景和22个合成场景，由6种代表性压缩算法在不同强度下生成，并提供9,108个MOS标注，分别评估整体、几何和颜色质量。随后提出3DGSV-Assessor，一种参考灵活的大规模多模态评分框架，可在一次生成过程中同时输出三个维度质量分数，并通过带门控机制融合基于排序的参考线索，从而支持有参考受限（RR）和无参考（NR）评估。

Result: 实验表明，3DGSV-Assessor在3DGS-VBench+上与人类感知具有最先进的相关性，并且在其他新视角合成基准上展现出较强的零样本泛化能力。数据集和代码计划公开。

Conclusion: 该工作为3DGS视频质量评估提供了多维、主观标注充分的大规模基准，并提出能够兼容稀疏参考视图的联合评分模型，有助于更准确地评价3DGS重建在几何、颜色和整体感知上的质量退化，推动3DGS压缩与渲染质量的可靠评估。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis (NVS), but its heavy storage makes compression essential, and both training and compression introduce representation-specific distortions. Since generative compression in many methods treats geometric and color attributes via different parameters, it can induce dimension-specific distortions, motivating multi-dimensional assessment. Moreover, real-world captures generally lack an aligned ground-truth video for a synthesized, continuous camera trajectory, leaving only sparse captured views as references. To enable multi-dimensional quality assessment of 3DGS videos with scarce aligned references, we first present 3DGS-VBench+, a large-scale subjective video quality assessment (VQA) benchmark comprising 3{,}036 3DGS reconstructions, each paired with a rendered video, across 11 real-world and 22 synthetic scenes, generated by 6 representative compression algorithms at systematically designed levels and annotated with 9{,}108 MOSs covering overall, geometry, and color quality. Based on 3DGS-VBench+, we propose 3DGSV-Assessor, a reference-flexible, large multimodal framework that scores all three dimensions in one generation pass and fuses a ranking-based reference cue into the frozen regressor through a bounded gate, supporting both reduced-reference (RR) and no-reference (NR) assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art correlation with human perception on 3DGS-VBench+ and strong zero-shot generalization to other NVS benchmarks. The dataset and code will be released at this https URL.

</details>


### [53] [BtsPruner: Role-Aware Visual Token Pruning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04537)
*Zihao Qi,Zhibo Zhang,Miaoxin Zhang,Jincheng Cai,Zengqiang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需训练、角色感知的视觉 token 剪枝框架 BtsPruner，通过保留大视觉语言模型中不同角色的视觉 token，在极低 token 预算下显著降低计算开销并尽量保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在推理时通常处理数百甚至数千个视觉 token，带来较高计算和显存开销。现有训练无关剪枝方法多依赖语言模型注意力、视觉注意力或特征相似度估计 token 重要性，但通常对所有视觉 token 使用统一标准，忽略了它们在视觉编码过程中可能承担不同功能角色。作者观察到，视觉 token 的角色会随编码层数演变，逐渐从局部细节承载者转变为全局被引用的代表 token，因此需要根据不同角色进行差异化保留。

Method: BtsPruner 是一个训练无关的视觉 token 剪枝框架。它首先根据注意力集中度的演化识别 token 角色转换点，然后从较后的编码阶段发现被全局引用的代表 token，再递归地从较早阶段检索局部细节 token，以恢复互补的细粒度证据。在固定 token 预算下，该方法同时保留全局代表性和局部细节两类互补角色。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、Qwen-VL、InternVL 和 CogVLM 等多个模型上，BtsPruner 在激进 token 削减下表现出更优的性能保持能力。特别是在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上，仅保留 576 个视觉 token 中的 32 个，即约 5.6%，仍可保留密集模型 91.6% 的性能。在高分辨率 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 设置中，最高可实现约 5.95 倍 CUDA 推理延迟加速。

Conclusion: 视觉 token 在 LVLM 编码过程中具有动态演变的功能角色，仅用统一重要性标准进行剪枝可能破坏互补信息。BtsPruner 通过角色感知和互补性保留机制，在大幅减少视觉 token 数量、提高推理效率的同时，能够较好保持多模态任务性能，说明面向 token 角色的剪枝是高效部署大视觉语言模型的有效途径。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often process hundreds or thousands of visual tokens, resulting in substantial computational and memory overhead during inference. Existing training-free pruning methods typically estimate token importance using language-model attention, visual attention, or feature similarity. However, these methods treat visual tokens with uniform criteria and overlook that tokens may serve different roles during visual encoding. In this work, we investigate the evolution of visual token roles in LVLMs and observe that tokens progressively transition from local detail carriers to globally referenced representatives as encoding proceeds. Based on this observation, we propose BtsPruner, a training-free role-aware visual token pruning framework that preserves complementary token roles under a fixed budget. Specifically, BtsPruner first identifies the token-role transition point based on the evolution of attention concentration. It then discovers globally referenced representatives from later encoding stages and recursively retrieves local detail tokens from earlier stages to recover complementary fine-grained evidence. Extensive experiments across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, Qwen-VL, InternVL, and CogVLM demonstrate that BtsPruner achieves superior performance retention under aggressive token reduction. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, BtsPruner preserves 91.6% of the dense model's performance using only 32 of 576 visual tokens (5.6%). In the high-resolution LLaVA-NeXT-7B setting, it achieves up to a 5.95x CUDA-latency speedup.

</details>


### [54] [Predicting Void Points in Self-Occluding Forest Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04548)
*Benjamin Bachmayr,Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对合成孔径成像（SAI）森林冠层重建中遮挡区域被误判为植被、导致生物量高估的问题，提出通过生成包含“空体/负类”的训练标签来改进深度学习模型；在模拟场景实验中，修改标签后的 U-Net 类网络在冠层占用分割上优于 COLMAP 和 DepthAnything3 等传统重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAI 通过多视角偏移图像的 warp 与平均来推断森林冠层，但遮挡只能被抑制而不能完全消除，导致未被充分观测的区域呈现为“有物体”，进而造成系统性生物量高估。Deep Forest 项目的训练数据依赖实测反射点，缺少空体样本，使网络无法学习明确的空间占用边界，因此难以区分重建伪影与真实植被。该研究的核心动机不是改进成像或重建流程，而是从标签端解决“空体不可见”的问题。

Method: 作者构建了一套基于仿真的训练与评估流程。首先使用 Gazebo 程序化生成含 80–200 棵树、三个树种的森林立木场景；随后对每个场景进行两类渲染：9×9 俯视图像用于生成焦栈，以及 550 层深度裁剪 slab 用于从场景几何导出占用标签，并显式将空体作为负类。图像端使用预训练 Pix2Pix 模型将 RGB 渲染图转换为合成近红外波段，再用 Light Field Rendering 生成 550 个平面、三通道对齐的焦栈。最后在留出场景上比较多种网络与传统重建方法的冠层占用分割性能。

Result: 在留出场景的 IoU 评估中，普通 2D U-Net 达到 0.6290，高于体积式 3D U-Net 的 0.5707。传统重建方法表现明显较差：COLMAP 为 0.3446，DepthAnything3 为 0.2959，且均在各自最优膨胀阈值后仍落后。最高结果来自体积架构的 RGB 输入变体，其 IoU 达到 0.6514，且无需膨胀处理或逐场景校准。这些结果表明，引入基于场景几何的空体标签和合适输入表示有助于模型学习冠层内部占用关系。

Conclusion: 该研究证明：对于 SAI 森林冠层重建，遮挡造成的主要瓶颈不仅是成像问题，也是训练数据没有表示空体空间的问题。通过从仿真场景几何生成显式占用标签，模型可更好地区分真实植被与重建伪影。实验结果还显示传统重建方法只能恢复可见表面，难以推断闭合冠层内部占用，而基于学习的占用分割更具潜力。但该结论主要建立在模拟数据和留出场景验证上，后续仍需真实无人机数据和跨场景泛化评估来支持向实际应用推广。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Imaging (SAI) enables drones to observe forest canopies by warping and averaging multiple displaced views at a common depth. However, occlusion is suppressed rather than eliminated, causing poorly observed regions to appear occupied and leading to systematic biomass overestimation. The Deep Forest project cannot distinguish reconstruction artifacts from real vegetation because its training data relies on measured reflectance points, excluding empty space and preventing the network from learning explicit occupancy boundaries. This thesis addresses the problem by modifying training labels rather than the imaging process. Each simulated scene is rendered twice: as a 9x9 grid of top-down captures for focal-stack generation and as 550 depth-clipped slabs derived from scene geometry to provide occupancy labels, explicitly representing void space as a negative class. A Gazebo pipeline generates procedural forest stands containing 80-200 trees across three species. A pretrained Pix2Pix model translates rendered RGB images into a synthetic near-infrared band, and Light Field Rendering produces aligned focal stacks of 550 planes across three channels. Four architectures were evaluated on a held-out scene. The plain 2D U-Net achieved an IoU of 0.6290, outperforming the volumetric 3D U-Net (0.5707). Established reconstruction methods performed substantially worse: COLMAP achieved 0.3446 and DepthAnything3 0.2959, both after their respective optimal dilation sweeps. An RGB-input variant of the volumetric architecture achieved the highest IoU of 0.6514 without dilation or per-scene calibration. These results highlight the limitations of conventional reconstruction methods, which recover visible surfaces but struggle to infer occupancy within closed canopies.

</details>


### [55] [SEEK-VAU: Towards Evidence-Faithful Video Anomaly Understanding via Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04554)
*Minghe Wang,Weijun Zeng,Dapeng Luo,Xinyue Wang,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEEK-VAU，将视频异常理解（VAU）从基于预定义规则的前馈式被动推理，重构为在有限搜索预算下的主动“搜索—验证”智能体交互协议，并引入证据忠实反事实验证（EFCV）和SEEK-Bench，以提升异常事件链证据的完整性与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解系统通常依赖预定义规则采集的有限证据，例如固定采样帧或由帧聚类得到的事件，并在证据缺失或不完整时仍直接进行推理。一旦推理开始，模型无法主动补充缺失证据，因此容易受到预定义规则不完善的影响，导致异常判断不可靠。

Method: SEEK-VAU将异常事件建模为包含“前兆—触发—后果”的事件链，策略主动跨这些阶段搜集证据，并在最终判断前验证证据是否充分。为了避免搜索成本失控，方法引入有限预算约束证据搜索过程。为让“去哪里找证据”和“何时停止搜索”可学习，提出证据忠实反事实验证（EFCV），奖励在反事实验证下仍具有支持性、简洁性和必要性的证据。同时构建SEEK-Bench，包含视频级episode、时间区间标注、语义问答和事件链阶段标签。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果，但指出SEEK-VAU与SEEK-Bench共同建立了面向“证据忠实”和“主动可验证”的视频异常理解的强基础，表明该方法在证据搜集、验证和基准构建方面具有支撑性作用。

Conclusion: 本文将视频异常理解从被动依赖预定义证据的推理范式，推进为主动搜证与验证事件链完整性的智能体范式，使证据不足问题能够被显式处理和优化，为后续构建更可靠、可解释和可验证的视频异常理解系统提供了方向与基准。

Abstract: Most video anomaly understanding (VAU) systems still infer anomaly judgments from evidence collected in a feed-forward way under predefined rules, typically sampled frames, or events clustered from such frames. This protocol fails when the collected evidence is incomplete under imperfect predefined rules, because once reasoning begins the model can no longer search for the missing evidence. We address this limitation with SEEK-VAU, which reframes VAU as an agentic search-and-verify interaction protocol under a bounded search budget: instead of passively reasoning over pre-collected evidence, the policy actively gathers evidence across the three stages of an anomaly event chain (precursor, trigger, and aftermath) and verifies its sufficiency before finalization, turning event-chain completeness into an explicit design rather than an outcome of predefined rules. A bounded budget caps the cost of this search, but does not by itself teach the policy where to look or when to stop. To make this behavior learnable, we introduce evidence-faithful counterfactual verification (EFCV), which rewards selected evidence that remains supportive, compact, and necessary under counterfactual verification. We further introduce SEEK-Bench, featuring video-level episodes with temporal interval annotations, semantic QA and event-chain stage labels. Together, SEEK-VAU and SEEK-Bench establish a strong foundation for evidence-faithful and actively verifiable VAU.

</details>


### [56] [Multi-Path Mamba Diffusion for 3D Point Cloud Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04583)
*Li Liang,Naveed Akhtar,Jordan Vice,Anwaar Ulhaq,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将状态空间建模与扩散生成模型结合的神经网络框架，用于提升三维点云语义场景补全任务中的几何结构与语义信息估计能力，并在 SemanticKITTI 基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维语义场景补全对自动驾驶等下游系统至关重要，但现实场景中常存在遮挡、传感器视角限制和数据稀疏等问题，导致几何结构和语义信息不完整。现有方法需要更好地建模长序列三维点云关系，并提升补全内容的生成质量与表征能力。

Method: 作者提出 MPath Mamba 扩散网络，核心设计包括 skip-triple Mamba 结构、维度分解残差、多方向膨胀以及多路径数据处理，用于高效提取长序列特征。该方法在变分自编码器的条件潜空间中运行，并引入状态空间机制增强三维点云表征与重建能力，从而结合扩散生成模型完成三维语义场景补全。

Result: 在 SemanticKITTI 基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在三维点云语义场景补全任务中取得了高度竞争力的性能，说明所提 Mamba 结构、潜空间扩散生成和状态空间增强机制具有有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将状态空间模型与扩散生成方法相结合，能够有效提升三维点云语义场景补全的性能。其价值在于同时改善长序列三维数据建模、潜空间生成质量以及几何与语义联合补全能力，为自动驾驶感知系统提供更完整、更可靠的环境理解方法。

Abstract: 3D semantic scene completion plays a critical role in enabling various downstream tasks within autonomous systems by estimating both missing geometric structures and semantic information in scene data. In this work, we introduce a unique neural framework that integrates state space modeling with diffusion-based generative approaches to enhance 3D point cloud semantic scene completion. We propose \textit{MPath Mamba} diffusion network, leveraging a skip-triple Mamba structure, dimensional decomposition residuals, and multi-directional dilations within multi-path data to efficiently process long-sequence data for feature extraction. The proposed method operates in the conditioned latent space of a variational autoencoder, enhanced by a state-space mechanism to improve 3D point cloud representation and reconstruction. Extensive experiments conducted on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that our approach achieves highly competitive performance for 3D point cloud semantic scene completion.

</details>


### [57] [SparseGraph-I2P: Consistency-Guided Hypothesis Selection for Efficient Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04591)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseGraph-I2P通过稀疏图引导的粗到细图像到点云匹配与深度兼容约束，提高内点比例、注册召回率并降低重复细匹配开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细I2P注册容易丢弃正确但模糊的图像patch与点云patch配对；若保留更多候选，又会在重叠patch上反复进行精细匹配，导致效率下降和错误传播。需要一种在候选选择、匹配计算和位姿求解内点质量之间平衡的方法。

Method: 首先构建Coarse Hypothesis Graph，将外观相似性与深度导出的一阶/二阶兼容关系结合，在固定预算内选择高质量patch对。随后进行Sparse Fine Matching：对每个查询像素仅解码一次，并利用祖先patch已与该点云区域配对所覆盖的像素—点对比较进行跳过。若使用传感器深度，则基于SC2谱支持对对应点进行深度感知排序，并单次EPnP求解；若使用单目深度，则先从对应几何中校准深度或逆深度，再进行过滤。

Result: 在RGB-D Scenes V2和7-Scenes上，使用传感器深度时取得最高注册召回率和最低延迟。使用预测单目深度时，在RGB-D Scenes V2上仍保持最高召回率，但需额外校准开销。

Conclusion: 通过深度兼容的粗假设图、稀疏精细匹配和深度感知对应点排序，SparseGraph-I2P能有效提升I2P注册中的内点比例、召回率与计算效率，并适用于传感器深度和单目预测深度场景。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine image-to-point-cloud (I2P) registration risks discarding correct but ambiguous patch pairs, while retaining more candidates repeats fine matching over overlapping patches. SparseGraph-I2P aims to improve registration recall by increasing the inlier ratio at the pose solver, using sensor or monocular depth. A Coarse Hypothesis Graph combines appearance with depth-derived first- and second-order compatibility to select a fixed budget of patch pairs. Sparse Fine Matching decodes each queried pixel once and skips pixel--point comparisons covered by an ancestor patch paired with the same point-cloud region. With sensor depth, depth-aware filtering ranks correspondences by SC2 spectral support for a single EPnP solve. With monocular depth, depth or inverse depth is calibrated from correspondence geometry before filtering. SparseGraph-I2P achieves the highest registration recall and lowest latency among the compared methods on RGB-D Scenes V2 and 7-Scenes with sensor depth. With predicted depth, it retains the highest recall on RGB-D Scenes V2 at an additional calibration cost. Code will be released.

</details>


### [58] [Gradient-Guided Orientation Learning for Weakly Supervised Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04616)
*Junyong Jang,Juheon Park,Minyoung Jeon,Jaehyup Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向弱监督旋转目标检测的梯度引导方向学习策略：从物体区域的图像梯度中提取主导方向线索并生成伪角度标签作为辅助监督，同时引入角度感知评估指标以更真实地衡量方向预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督旋转目标检测依赖水平框或点等廉价标注，但这些标注不包含显式方向信息。现有方法通常通过几何一致性或等变约束间接推断角度，在光照、颜色、阴影和复杂背景等真实外观变化下容易出现歧义和不稳定。此外，传统 AP 指标可能因空间 IoU 较高而掩盖角度预测误差。

Method: 该方法观察输入图像本身包含可利用的方向先验，提取物体区域中的主导梯度方向，将其转换为伪角度标签，用于辅助监督检测器学习方向感知表示。该策略无需旋转框标注，也无需额外可学习参数。同时，作者提出将角度差异纳入标准 AP 评估流程，构建面向方向预测质量的评估指标。

Result: 在弱监督旋转目标检测基准上的实验表明，所提伪角度监督能够提升整体检测性能和方向估计质量，并且新提出的评估指标比仅使用常规 AP 更能反映模型的角度预测能力。

Conclusion: 图像梯度中的方向先验可为弱监督旋转目标检测提供简单有效的显式方向监督信号；同时，旋转目标检测评价应进一步关注角度误差，而不能仅依赖传统 AP。

Abstract: Weakly supervised oriented object detection (WS-OOD) aims to reduce the annotation cost of rotated bounding boxes by leveraging cheaper annotations such as horizontal boxes or points. However, these weak annotations provide no explicit orientation information, forcing existing methods to infer object angles through indirect geometric consistency or equivariant constraints. Such indirect supervision often becomes ambiguous and unstable under real-world appearance variations, including illumination changes, color shifts, shadows, and background clutter. In this paper, we present a simple yet effective gradient-guided orientation learning strategy for WS-OOD. Motivated by the observation that the input image itself contains useful orientation priors, our method extracts dominant gradient-based orientation cues from object regions and converts them into pseudo-angle labels for auxiliary supervision, enabling the detector to learn orientation-aware representations without requiring rotated bounding box annotations or extra learnable parameters. Furthermore, since AP can mask substantial angular errors when spatial IoU remains high, we introduce an orientation-aware evaluation metric that incorporates angular discrepancies into the standard AP protocol. Extensive experiments on weakly supervised oriented detection benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed pseudo-angle supervision improves both detection performance and orientation estimation quality, while the proposed metric provides a more faithful assessment of angle prediction than conventional AP alone.

</details>


### [59] [MambaHand: Local Identity and Behavioral Sequence Learning with Mamba for Dynamic Hand Gesture Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04626)
*Zenan Lin,Junduan Huang,Xilai Wang,Wenxiong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MambaHand Net，一种融合 CNN 与 Mamba 的双流动态手势认证框架，用于联合建模细粒度局部身份特征和长程行为依赖，并在 SCUT-DHGA 上取得 SOTA 平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态手势认证方法主要依赖卷积网络或手工运动表示，难以同时捕捉手势执行过程中的细粒度局部身份差异和长程行为依赖。

Method: MambaHand Net 包含局部身份流和行为序列流：局部身份流用 CNN 提取逐帧局部身份线索；行为序列流保留中间视觉特征中的空间 token，并构造一阶和二阶时间差分序列以建模多阶行为变化；同时引入双向 Mamba 对不同空间位置的语义运动进行建模，以高效捕捉长距离手势执行习惯。最后通过注意力引导的空间 token 聚合和双流融合策略生成具有判别性的身份表示。

Result: 在 SCUT-DHGA 基准数据集上取得 SOTA 平均性能，验证了 CNN 与 Mamba 结合对动态手势认证任务的有效性。

Conclusion: 将 CNN 的局部特征建模能力与 Mamba 的长程序列建模能力相结合，是动态手势认证的一种有效新方案。

Abstract: Dynamic hand gesture authentication (DHGA) is an emerging biometric technique that contains both physiological and behavioral identity characteristics. However, existing methods primarily rely on convolutional networks or manually designed motion representations, making it difficult to jointly capture fine-grained local identity differences and long-range behavioral dependencies during hand gesture execution. To address these challenges, we propose a two-stream identity representation learning framework, MambaHand Net, to combine CNNs with Mamba for DHGA. The proposed framework consists of a Local Identity Stream (LIS) and a Behavioral Sequential Stream (BSS). The LIS extracts frame-wise local identity cues with a convolutional network, while the BSS preserves spatial tokens from intermediate visual features and constructs first-order and second-order temporal difference sequences to model multi-order behavioral variations. Moreover, the bidirectional Mamba is incorporated into the BSS to model semantic motion across different spatial locations, enabling efficient long-range modeling of hand gesture execution habits. Finally, attention-guided spatial token aggregation and a two-stream fusion strategy are used to integrate local identity cues and multi-order behavioral patterns into a discriminative identity representation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) average performance on the SCUT-DHGA benchmark dataset, demonstrating that combining CNNs with Mamba is an effective new solution for DHGA.

</details>


### [60] [ReXMo: End-to-End Video Character Replacement with Expression and Motion Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04633)
*Hualiang Wei,Wenhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReXMo，一个端到端视频角色替换框架，通过配对三元组监督、上下文扩散 Transformer 和表情增强适配器，支持单人、多人和选择性角色替换，同时提升身份保真、运动一致性和表情保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频角色替换方法通常依赖掩码生成和姿态引导，在复杂场景中难以细粒度保留原角色的面部表情与动作动态；同时缺少一种统一框架来覆盖单人、多人和选择性替换等多种任务设定。

Method: ReXMo 采用端到端训练范式，并引入配对三元组监督：由合成驱动视频与原始视频构成具有相同表演、不同身份的配对数据，从而实现可扩展训练。模型以 In-Context Diffusion Transformer 为核心，联合条件于驱动视频、参考身份和文本提示，以学习身份转换；同时设计 Expression Enhancement Adapter，用于增强多人场景中的面部表情保持。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReXMo 在身份保真度、运动一致性、表情保持和视觉质量方面优于现有方法，并能够支持单人、多人以及选择性角色替换场景。作者还宣布将公开数据集和代码。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一上下文建模和合成配对监督缓解了视频角色替换中表情与动作保持不足的问题，提升了身份编辑的真实感与细粒度一致性，为视频角色替换提供了可扩展的训练思路和开源资源基础。

Abstract: Video character replacement aims to replace actors while preserving facial expressions, motions, and scene dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on masked generation with pose guidance, limiting fine-grained expression and motion preservation in complex scenes. We propose ReXMo, an end-to-end framework that supports single-, multi-, and selective character replacement scenarios. ReXMo introduces paired triplet supervision, where synthesized driving videos and original videos share identical performances but different identities, enabling scalable training through synthetic data generation. An In-Context Diffusion Transformer learns identity transformation by jointly conditioning on driving videos, reference identities, and text prompts, while an Expression Enhancement Adapter improves facial expression preservation in multi-person scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReXMo outperforms existing methods in identity fidelity, motion consistency, expression preservation, and visual quality. Our dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [61] [Therapy-FIS: A Dataset for Assessing Facilitative Interpersonal Skills in Psychotherapeutic Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04645)
*Qijian Zheng,Wanqing Lin,Yuanfang Wang,Yijun Ge,Jordan Bate,Katie Aafjes-van Doorn,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Therapy-FIS：首个面向促进性人际技能（FIS）自动化评估的公开多模态数据集，并建立两个下游基准任务与 DyadicFIS 基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FIS 评估高度依赖人工观察者评分，耗时、昂贵且难以规模化；同时心理治疗场景中缺乏公开的专业数据，限制了自动化评估的发展。

Method: 作者收集并标注标准化治疗师对模拟患者刺激视频的反应，构建包含面部、声学、文本三类信号的数据集，提供 8 项 FIS 子评分和总分。随后设计两个下游任务：治疗师表现评分和“治疗师—模拟患者”联合表现评分，并提出 DyadicFIS 基线，采用门控融合和路由读出。

Result: 在两项任务中，DyadicFIS 取得了最佳的宏平均 CCC 分数，分别为 0.2807 和 0.3299。

Conclusion: Therapy-FIS 为心理健康治疗中人际技能的自动化、数据驱动评估提供了可复现的数据资源和基准，有助于推动相关研究从人工评分转向可规模化自动评估。

Abstract: Facilitative Interpersonal Skills (FIS) is a performance-based assessment method that provides a structured framework for evaluating the interpersonal competencies of mental health professionals. However, existing FIS assessment relies heavily on human observer ratings, making the evaluation process labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. Progress toward automated FIS assessment has also been constrained by the lack of publicly available professional data in psychotherapy settings. To address this limitation, we introduce Therapy-FIS, a dataset for automated FIS assessment in standardized psychotherapy tasks. To our knowledge, Therapy-FIS is the first publicly available dataset in this domain. It contains facial, acoustic, and textual signals derived from standardized therapist responses to standardized mock patient stimulus clips, based on 890 therapist video recordings and 8 patient videos, and is rated with eight FIS subscale ratings and an overall FIS score. Built on Therapy-FIS, we further establish a benchmark with two downstream tasks: Therapist Performance Rating and Joint Therapist--mock-Patient Performance Rating. We also introduce a competitive baseline, DyadicFIS, with gated fusion and routed readout under therapist-only and dyadic settings. DyadicFIS obtains the best macro CCC(Concordance Correlation Coefficient) scores on both tasks (0.2807 and 0.3299). Therapy-FIS offers a reproducible resource for data-driven automated assessment of therapeutic interpersonal skills in mental health.

</details>


### [62] [BhopalMLS: A LiDAR Dataset for Semantic Scene Understanding in Heterogeneous Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04654)
*Pyare Lal Chauhan,Vaibhav Kumar,Bharat Lohani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BhopalMLS——一个来自印度博帕尔高密度、非规则城市环境的移动激光扫描（MLS）大规模数据集，包含约10公里道路走廊、超过12亿点和19,518个标注实例，覆盖62个细粒度静态类别，并进一步用于12类和21类分割基准；实验表明基于该数据集微调的基础模型性能更优，且训练模型向多个已有数据集迁移时呈现稳定的“从BhopalMLS转出更优”的非对称可迁移性，作者进一步将其归因于BhopalMLS中更丰富的子类覆盖，而非单纯数据规模或道路几何形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D MLS/点云数据集大多采集自规划良好的城市，场景结构相对均质，类别子类多样性不足，难以真实反映发展中城市常见的非规划街道、混合交通、非正式建筑和复杂静态环境等特征。这使得面向3D感知的语义分割与实例分割模型在跨城市、跨场景迁移时面临分布偏移与类别覆盖不足的问题。因此，作者希望构建一个更复杂、更细粒度、更具现实世界挑战性的城市MLS数据集，用于评估模型在真实非规划环境下的感知能力，并研究数据集多样性对跨域可迁移性的影响。

Method: 论文的主要方法包括以下几个方面：\n1. 数据采集与构建：在印度博帕尔一个密集、异构的非规则城市环境中采集移动激光扫描（MLS）数据，覆盖约10公里道路走廊，得到超过12亿点的大规模点云数据集。\n2. 细粒度标注与基准设计：对静态对象进行62个细粒度类别标注，共19,518个实例，并进一步组织为12类和21类两类分割基准；同时提供6个可选运动类子类以支持动态对象分析。\n3. 下游任务评测：在语义分割和实例分割任务上，对比从零训练的监督基线模型与微调基础模型，重点评估Utonia(L)和Concerto(L)等模型在该数据集上的表现。\n4. 跨数据集迁移实验：将BhopalMLS与Ke-MLS、Toronto-3D、Paris-Lille-3D、SemanticKITTI四个已有数据集进行双向训练—测试迁移，比较模型在不同数据集组合上的mIoU表现，验证是否存在稳定的非对称可迁移性。\n5. 归因分析：通过固定标注预算并进行因子控制的重训练实验，比较完整类别与“每类仅保留单一子类型”的数据设置，以判断跨域优势究竟来自数据规模、走廊几何还是类别内部子类型覆盖度。

Result: 实验结果显示：\n1. 在BhopalMLS上的12类基准中，微调基础模型显著优于从零训练的监督基线。Utonia(L)在语义分割上达到71.28% mIoU，高于PTv3的68.04%；在实例分割上达到44.07% mAP50。\n2. 跨数据集迁移实验表明存在一致的非对称可迁移性：以BhopalMLS为训练域、在四个已有数据集上测试时，性能普遍优于反向迁移。这种优势对Utonia(L)和Concerto(L)均成立，平均提升约21.5 mIoU，最大可达36 mIoU。\n3. 这种优势甚至在目标数据集密度更高的情况下依然存在，说明模型收益并非仅由点云密度决定。\n4. 因子控制实验进一步表明，子类覆盖度是关键原因：当每个类别被压缩为单一子类型时，Utonia(L)和Concerto(L)的性能分别下降13.0和13.3 mIoU，说明BhopalMLS的价值主要来自其丰富的细粒度子类型分布，而非单纯的数据量或道路走廊结构。

Conclusion: BhopalMLS填补了现有3D MLS数据集在非规划、高复杂度发展中国家的城市环境表示方面的不足，为语义分割、实例分割以及动态对象分析提供了新的细粒度基准。其核心发现是：更丰富的类别内部子类型覆盖能够显著提升模型跨域可迁移性，使得在BhopalMLS上训练的模型更容易迁移到其他城市数据集。因此，该数据集可作为面向真实世界复杂城市3D感知的重要测试平台，并为后续构建更具鲁棒性和泛化能力的激光点云模型提供数据与方法上的启发。

Abstract: We introduce BhopalMLS, a large-scale Mobile Laser Scanning (MLS) dataset from a dense, heterogeneous urban environment in Bhopal, India, with mixed traffic, informal structures, and narrow unplanned streets. It spans approximately 10 km of road corridors with over 1.2 billion points and 19,518 annotated instances across 62 fine-grained static categories, grouped into 12-class and 21-class benchmark taxonomies; 6 optional moving-class subclasses support dynamic-object analysis. Existing 3D datasets, collected in well-planned cities, are structurally more uniform than such unplanned settings, and lack their subtype diversity. On semantic and instance segmentation at both granularities, fine-tuned foundation models surpass supervised baselines: Utonia(L) reaches 71.28% mIoU (vs. PTv3's 68.04% from scratch) and 44.07% mAP50 on the 12-class benchmark. Cross-region experiments against four established datasets (Ke-MLS, Toronto-3D, Paris-Lille-3D, SemanticKITTI) reveal a consistent asymmetric transferability: models trained on BhopalMLS transfer better than the reverse on every pair, for both Utonia(L) and Concerto(L) (mean 21.5, up to 36 mIoU points), holding even when the target is denser. Factor-controlled retraining at a fixed label budget, repeated on both models, attributes this to the coverage of BhopalMLS's subtype inventory rather than to dataset size or corridor geometry: collapsing each class to a single subtype costs 13.0 and 13.3 mIoU points for Utonia(L) and Concerto(L). BhopalMLS therefore offers a testbed for 3D perception beyond well-planned cities. Data and an interactive viewer are at this https URL.

</details>


### [63] [Phase-DGS: Phase-guided Dynamic Gaussian Splatting from Unsynchronized Multi-view Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04666)
*Hosung Jeon,Jun Young Jeong,Sangwoon Kwak,Joonsoo Kim,Sangmin Kim,Jaesik Park,Won-Sik Cheong,Hyon-Gon Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Phase-DGS，用“语义相位”替代传统时间戳，表示运动周期中每一帧的本征状态，使不同相机、不同时间拍摄的同一运动状态自动对齐，从而实现无需完美同步的动态高斯泼溅场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空场景重建中的动态高斯泼溅（DGS）通常依赖多相机之间的精确时间同步，但现实中很难实现。工业级同步设备昂贵且部署复杂，而音频对齐、几何匹配、位姿跟踪等数据驱动方法又受环境或场景假设限制，难以通用。

Method: Phase-DGS 不再以物理时间戳作为帧间对应关系，而是估计每帧在底层运动周期中的语义相位。只要多个相机拍摄的帧对应同一运动状态，就赋予相同相位，无论其拍摄相机或时间如何。该相位可作为跨相机的时空对应先验，并与现有 DGS 主干（如 RealTime-4DGS、FreeTimeGS）无缝集成。作者在时间偏移、丢帧和相机冻结等条件下进行验证。

Result: 在多相机不同步条件下，Phase-DGS 相比基线方法最高带来 +8.5 dB 的 PSNR 提升，并能兼容现有动态高斯泼溅框架。

Conclusion: 以运动语义相位代替时间戳，可为无完美同步的动态场景重建提供更实用、鲁棒的时空对应机制，降低对专用同步硬件或强场景假设的依赖。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal scene reconstruction with Dynamic Gaussian Splatting (DGS) fundamentally depends on perfect temporal synchronization across all cameras, an assumption rarely achievable in practice. Industry-standard synchronization requires expensive specialized hardware and complex setup, while data-driven alternatives including audio-based alignment, geometry matching, and pose tracking remain limited by restrictive environmental or scene-specific assumptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Phase-DGS, a framework that replaces conventional timestamps with a semantic phase representing each frame's inherent state within the underlying motion cycle. Frames capturing the same motion state share the same phase regardless of which camera records them or when, naturally establishing spatiotemporal correspondence across unsynchronized cameras. We validate Phase-DGS under temporal offsets, frame drops, and camera freezes, achieving up to +8.5 dB PSNR improvement over baselines while enabling seamless integration with existing DGS backbones including RealTime-4DGS and FreeTimeGS.

</details>


### [64] [DenseTwins: Dense Modality Decoupling for Multimodal Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04710)
*Carlo Sgaravatti,Daniele Kopyshevskiy,Davide Secco,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DenseTwins，一个多模态自监督学习框架，通过把密集特征空间显式分解为共享成分和模态特异成分，学习保留空间结构的密集表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督多模态方法常通过特征对齐忽略模态特有信息，或只学习全局表示而破坏空间结构，难以充分利用多模态数据中的互补线索。

Method: DenseTwins 将密集跨模态对应与冗余减少目标结合，同时优化：（1）模态内增强视图目标；（2）跨模态目标，即对齐共享特征并解耦/去相关模态特异特征。

Result: 在多个多模态基准和传感配置上达到优于或竞争于现有自监督多模态基线的性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模共享语义与模态特异信息的密集解耦表示，有助于多模态自监督学习在保留空间结构的同时提升下游性能。

Abstract: Multimodal perception is central to many computer vision applications, including robotics and autonomous driving, where different sensing modalities provide complementary cues. Self-supervised learning offers a scalable solution for exploiting large amounts of unlabeled multimodal data. However, existing methods either neglect modality-specific information by enforcing feature-space alignment between modalities or learn only global representations without preserving spatial structure. We propose DenseTwins, a multimodal self-supervised framework that learns dense representations by explicitly decoupling the dense feature space into shared and modality-specific components. The proposed approach combines dense cross-modal correspondences with redundancy-reduction objectives, enabling the model to capture both shared semantics and modality-specific cues within a structured dense embedding space. DenseTwins jointly optimizes an intra-modal objective across augmented views and a cross-modal objective that aligns shared features while decorrelating modality-specific information. We evaluate DenseTwins across multiple multimodal benchmarks and sensing setups, achieving state-of-the-art or competitive performance across multimodal self-supervised baselines. Code available at: this https URL.

</details>


### [65] [Cycle WAM: Coupling Action and Video through Cycle Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04711)
*Qinglin Yang,Jiepeng Wang,Yuxing Huang,Haibin Huang,Kai Xu,Chi Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Cycle WAM，一种面向世界动作模型的视频-动作统一训练框架，通过双向转换和可微循环重建目标，使机器人动作与视频变化保持因果一致，从而更充分地利用预训练视频先验，并在前向与逆动力学任务上取得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界动作模型通常利用预训练视频先验，从潜表征解码动作，或从生成视频中推断动作。其核心假设是动作与场景变化之间存在因果关系：动作导致视觉状态改变，视频观测又反过来提供推断动作的证据。然而，现有训练方式并未显式保证“动作”与其“预测视觉后果”之间的一致性，这可能削弱预训练视频先验的作用，限制模型在动作预测和动作推断中的能力。

Method: 论文将视频与动作视为由因果关系连接的双模态配对数据，并设计统一训练框架学习二者的对应关系。Cycle WAM 主要包括两部分：第一，在视频-动作协同训练中引入双向转换，即同时建模动作到视频的前向动态和视频到动作的逆向动态，以强化跨模态对应；第二，提出可微的循环重建目标，将两个映射进一步耦合，使预测结果能够闭环重建原始输入，从而提升动作与视觉变化的一致性。最终模型同时支持前向动态预测和逆动力学动作推断。

Result: 实验表明，Cycle WAM 在 LIBERO-Pro 上达到同类方法中的最先进性能，并在 DROID 上显著改进 DreamZero。与 UWM 的比较进一步刻画了其逆动力学性能，同时显示其在可视化前向动态预测中具有更清晰的视觉细节。

Conclusion: Cycle WAM 表明，在视频世界动作模型中显式建模动作与视觉后果之间的双向因果关系，并利用循环一致性约束，可以更有效地利用预训练视频先验。该方法不仅提升动作预测能力，也改善动作推断和视觉动态预测质量，为机器人世界模型训练提供了一种更具一致性和可解释性的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in video modeling have driven substantial progress in world action models (WAMs). Existing approaches exploit pretrained video priors by decoding actions from latent representations or inferring them from generated videos. The underlying link is causal: robot actions produce changes in the scene, and video observations of these changes provide evidence for inferring the actions that produced them. However, existing training formulations do not by themselves ensure that actions and their predicted visual consequences are consistent, potentially limiting the use of pretrained capabilities. We treat video and action as paired modalities linked by this causal relationship and learn their correspondence through a unified training framework designed to better exploit pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM first augments video-action co-training with bidirectional conversion to reinforce cross-modal correspondence and recover forward and inverse dynamics. It then introduces a differentiable cycle-reconstruction objective to further couple the two mappings. The resulting model supports both forward and inverse dynamics, with the potential to improve performance through more effective use of pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable methods on LIBERO-Pro and substantially improves DreamZero on DROID. Comparisons with UWM characterize its inverse-dynamics performance and show sharper visual detail in the displayed forward-dynamics predictions.

</details>


### [66] [VISAGE: Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration via Dual-Stream Diffusion for Arbitrary 3D Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04712)
*Jeremias Krauß,Pragati Jaiswal,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VISAGE，一种基于顶点的内在表面外观生成框架，能够从稀疏多视角 RGB 图像直接在任意网格拓扑上生成物理一致的 3D 人头反照率和粗糙度，无需固定 UV 参数化，并显著降低零样本泛化下的 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏图像恢复身份保真、物理正确的 3D 人头表面外观仍然困难。现有方法多依赖 UV 流水线，容易产生接缝伪影；或在图像空间操作，难以保证表面一致性。因此需要一种直接基于网格拓扑、具备结构一致性和细节恢复能力、且不依赖统一 UV 参数化的表面外观生成方法。

Method: VISAGE 是一个表面条件扩散框架，直接在顶点上合成反照率和粗糙度。其方法包括：1）全局频谱处理结合局部测地线 KNN 细化，以兼顾结构一致性和细节恢复；2）多视角 RGB 通过可见性感知的交叉注意力进行条件约束，提高遮挡鲁棒性；3）区域专用模块分别处理眼睛、嘴部、皮肤和头发等语义复杂区域；4）切空间输出投影用于恢复虹膜等精细结构；5）引入可规模化程序化数据增强管线，为原始几何扫描补充物理合理的反照率、粗糙度和法线贴图用于训练。最终预测属性可直接作为单一 RGBA 顶点颜色通道渲染。

Result: 相对于最强基线，VISAGE 在 NPHM 上将 FID 降低 42.7%，在零样本 FaceVerse 和 i3DMM 上分别降低 22.5% 和 4.4%。核心网络仅有 7.2M 参数，表明该方法在较小模型规模下也能取得较好的生成质量和泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISAGE 通过绕开 UV 参数化、直接在顶点网格上进行物理表面属性生成，并结合全局频谱、局部测地线细化、多视角条件建模、区域专用模块和程序化数据增强，实现了身份保真、遮挡鲁棒且细节丰富的 3D 人头表面外观合成。其零样本跨数据集性能提升说明该方法具有较好的泛化潜力和实用价值。

Abstract: Recovering identity-faithful, physically based surface appearance for 3D human heads from sparse images remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on UV-based pipelines, introducing seam artefacts, or operate in image space, limiting surface consistency. We present VISAGE, Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration, a surface-based conditional diffusion framework that synthesises per-vertex albedo and roughness directly on arbitrary mesh topologies, without requiring consistent UV parameterisation at training time. VISAGE combines global spectral processing with local geodesic K-Nearest-Neighbour refinement, enforcing structural coherence while restoring detail beyond the truncated spectral basis. Multi-view RGB images condition the network via visibility-aware cross-attention, enabling occlusion-robust synthesis across the full head surface. Region-specific modules specialise in semantically demanding and distinct areas (eyes, mouth, skin and hair), while a tangent-space output projection recovers fine iris detail. We further introduce a scalable procedural augmentation pipeline that enriches raw geometry scans with physically plausible albedo, roughness and normal maps for training. The predicted attributes render directly as a single RGBA vertex-colour channel. VISAGE reduces FID by 42.7% on NPHM and 22.5% and 4.4% on zero-shot FaceVerse and i3DMM, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline, with a 7.2M-parameter core network.

</details>


### [67] [SPOD: Recovering Suppressed Temporal Evidence in Video-LLMs via Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04728)
*Yangguang Lin,Quan Fang,Xiaoyan Liu,Zefan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SPOD：一种免训练、单次前向推理的隐藏状态正交分解方法，用于缓解Video-LLM在时序敏感任务中的时间幻觉，核心是发掘并增强模型内部已编码但未被充分利用的时序证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLM在场景转换理解、事件排序等时序推理任务中仍易产生时间幻觉；已有方法往往需要昂贵重训练，或在测试时引入低效多轮推理与外部监督。作者观察到时序证据其实已存在于Video-LLM的隐藏表示中，只是标准推理过程未能充分利用。

Method: 提出Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition（SPOD），包含两个模块：（1）Frame Distinctiveness Analysis（FDA），利用中间层隐藏状态几何特征识别显著帧；（2）Temporal Delta Subspace（TDS），基于加权帧间隐藏状态差异构造相互正交的时序子空间与先验子空间。最终将隐藏状态分解为时序、先验和残差成分，并独立缩放，以增强动态时序信息、抑制静态外观偏置、保留互补信息。整个过程无需训练，且保持单次推理。

Result: 在VidHalluc、EventHallusion和VideoMMMU等基准上，SPOD在多种Video-LLM架构和时序推理任务中稳定优于强推理时基线，同时接近原始模型的推理效率。

Conclusion: 提升Video-LLM的时序推理能力不一定依赖额外监督或重训练，也可以通过在推理阶段恢复模型内部已存在的时序证据实现。SPOD为高效缓解视频模型时间幻觉提供了一种免训练的新思路。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have achieved strong performance on many video understanding tasks, yet remain vulnerable to temporal hallucination in time-sensitive reasoning scenarios such as scene transition understanding and event ordering. Existing solutions either require costly retraining or introduce inefficient multi-pass inference and external supervision at test time. In this work, we show that temporal evidence is already encoded in Video-LLM hidden representations but is not fully exploited during standard inference. Motivated by this observation, we propose Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition (SPOD), a training-free framework that recovers suppressed temporal evidence through analytical hidden-state manipulation in a single forward pass.
