Following semantic classification, the same reconstruction pipeline processes both mobile and aerial LiDAR data without modality-specific code or parameter tuning. The method is evaluated through degradation experiments, benchmarking, parameter sensitivity analysis, and large-scale validation. On the aerial dataset, it achieves 96.87% precision, 96.66% recall, a 96.76% F$_1$-score, and a geometric RMSE of 0.127 m. On the mobile dataset, where conductor-level reference annotations were unavailable, it achieves a point-to-model RMSE of 0.045 m and a reconstruction coverage of 95.27%. Source code, documentation, and evaluation data are publicly available at this https URL.

</details>


### [99] [TAQ-MOTR: Tracking-Aware Detection Query Formation for End-to-End Multiple Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05205)
*Hanyang Liu,Hong Zhang,Ding Yuan,Yang Han,Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAQ-MOTR，一种面向Tracking-by-Propagation（TBP）多目标跟踪的检测查询生成框架，通过轨迹状态感知的混合查询生成器和辅助检测分支，提升未跟踪目标发现与遮挡下轨迹补全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在端到端多目标跟踪中，TBP方法通过传播track query联合完成检测与身份关联，但检测查询与关联过程紧密耦合。现有检测查询构造方式多沿用单帧检测范式，在解码器前生成，缺乏对已有轨迹状态的感知，导致当前未跟踪目标可能漏检，同时被遮挡等困难轨迹也缺少来自当前帧的互补证据，进而引发关联失败或重复轨迹。

Method: 提出TAQ-MOTR跟踪感知检测查询生成框架。核心包括：1）解码器前的混合查询生成器Hybrid Query Generator（HQG），融合当前帧图像特征、track memory和learnable memory，使检测查询能够基于轨迹状态进行条件化构造，从而适配“发现未跟踪目标”和“补全已有轨迹”两类需求；2）引入仅用于训练的辅助检测分支，其一对一标签分配与主跟踪分支解耦，为共享HQG和解码器提供额外检测监督，增强模型对当前帧中未跟踪目标的识别能力。

Result: 在DanceTrack、SportsMOT和BFT数据集上取得性能提升。在DanceTrack测试集上达到72.0 HOTA、63.1 AssA和76.6 IDF1，并公开代码。

Conclusion: TAQ-MOTR通过将轨迹状态信息注入检测查询生成过程，缓解了TBP中检测查询与track query耦合造成的漏检和困难轨迹证据不足问题，提升了端到端多目标跟踪在复杂运动、遮挡和身份关联场景下的性能。

Abstract: Tracking-by-propagation (TBP) methods perform end-to-end multi-object tracking by propagating track queries across frames, jointly handling detection and identity association without heuristic post-processing while naturally modeling temporal information. However, as detection and association are tightly coupled, detection queries in TBP are expected to discover currently untracked objects and provide complementary current-frame evidence for challenging tracks whose queries and identities are retained. Existing methods largely inherit detection-query formation from single-frame detection and construct such queries before the decoder with little awareness of trajectory states. Consequently, currently untracked objects may be missed, while challenging tracked objects with reduced visibility under occlusion may lack complementary evidence, causing association failures or duplicate responses. To address these issues, we propose TAQ-MOTR, a tracking-aware detection query formation framework that employs a pre-decoder Hybrid Query Generator (HQG) to fuse current-frame image features, track memory, and learnable memory. By conditioning detection queries on trajectory states, TAQ-MOTR adapts them to \rev{the discovery of currently untracked objects and tracked-object complementation We further introduce a training-only auxiliary detection branch whose one-to-one label assignment is decoupled from that of the main tracking branch, providing additional detection supervision to strengthen the shared HQG and decoder for the discovery of currently untracked objects. TAQ-MOTR improves performance on DanceTrack, SportsMOT, and BFT, achieving 72.0 HOTA, 63.1 AssA, and 76.6 IDF1 on the DanceTrack test set. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [100] [Diffusion Plug-in Priors for Self-Supervised Ultrasound Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05206)
*Chu Chen,Mingduo He,Yining Li,Yinhao Wu,Wei Tang,Yiwen Luo,Yixuan Chen,Graham Treece,Pasquale Cascarano,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraDiff是一种零样本超声视频联合超分辨与去噪方法，通过冻结预训练扩散模型先验、优化潜变量种子，并在瑞利散射模型下构造稳定数据保真项与TV/时序先验，实现无需配对数据或重训的高精度超声视频修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视频受硬件空间分辨率和信号相关散斑噪声限制，且缺乏低/高分辨率配对训练数据，因此需要无需成对样本和网络再训练的联合超分辨与去斑斑修复方法。

Method: 提出UltraDiff：以冻结预训练扩散模型为插件先验，对每帧通过范围约束的最大后验优化采样器潜变量种子；从瑞利尺度族模型推导散斑感知数据保真项，分析相对残差与自适应加权特性；引入下界稳定且在正观测的对数强度上严格凸的保真形式，结合全变分正则化，并用前帧优化种子热启动下一帧，实现时序连续修复。

Result: 在EchoNet-LVH及模拟散斑/加性噪声条件下，UltraDiff用自然图像先验较最强基线提升PSNR 1.24 dB，超声相关先验进一步提升；在真实临床心脏超声视频中，相比DUP，零样本射血分数预测平均绝对误差由7.31降至6.37 EF点，表明修复有助于下游测量。

Conclusion: 将散斑感知保真项与冻结扩散先验结合，可在无配对数据、无再训练条件下实现超声视频超分辨与去斑斑联合修复，并改善下游EF估计，具有即插即用、不依赖特定扩散检查点的实际意义。

Abstract: \textit{Objective:} Ultrasound videos are limited by hardware-constrained spatial resolution and signal-dependent speckle, while paired low- and high-resolution acquisitions for supervised restoration are rarely available. We aim to jointly super-resolve and despeckle ultrasound videos without paired training data or network retraining. \textit{Methods:} We propose UltraDiff, which uses a frozen pre-trained diffusion model (DM) as a plug-in prior and restores each frame by optimizing the latent seed of the DM sampler under a range-constrained maximum a posteriori formulation. We derive a speckle-aware data-fidelity term from a Rayleigh scale-family model and characterize its relative-residual and variance-adaptive weighting properties. We further introduce a stabilized fidelity that is bounded below and strictly convex in log intensity for positive observations in its unclamped form. A total-variation regularizer complements the DM prior, and a temporal seed update warm-starts each frame from the optimized seed of its predecessor. \textit{Results:} On EchoNet-LVH videos with simulated speckle and additive noise, UltraDiff with a natural-image prior improves PSNR by 1.24\,dB over the strongest baseline, and ultrasound-related priors improve it further. On real clinical echocardiography videos, UltraDiff reduces the mean absolute error of zero-shot ejection fraction (EF) prediction from 7.31 to 6.37 EF points relative to DUP, the strongest baseline. \textit{Conclusion:} Combining a speckle-aware fidelity with frozen diffusion priors enables ground-truth-free ultrasound video restoration that benefits downstream measurement. \textit{Significance:} UltraDiff needs neither paired data nor retraining and is not tied to a specific diffusion checkpoint. Code associated with this work will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [101] [Route Anything: A Study of Model and Policy Routing for General-Purpose AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05275)
*Pengfei Zhou,Yixing Ma,Hexin Wang,Weidong Tang,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Route Anything框架，研究通用AI系统中的模型与策略路由问题，通过为不同任务动态选择最合适的候选模型、工具或策略，在性能与计算成本之间取得平衡，并构建覆盖语言、视觉、语音、代码、智能体和机器人操作的超大规模路由评测矩阵，证明路由能显著提升系统能力与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通往通用人工智能（AGI）可能需要跨多种领域和模态进行理解、生成、推理与行动。依赖单一模型掌握所有能力难度大且成本高，因此更有前景的路径是协调由专业模型、工具和策略组成的异构生态。核心问题在于：AI系统如何根据具体输入动态选择并协调最能胜任的候选者，同时平衡任务质量与计算开销。

Method: 作者提出“Route Anything”统一框架，从理论层面刻画质量—成本权衡下的最优路由，并分析路由器可获取的信息如何决定其可达性能。实验上，构建一个跨模态、跨任务的“轨迹矩阵”，覆盖25个基准，包括语言理解、图像/视频/语音生成、编程与工作智能体、机器人操作等，包含452个候选配置产生的374401次评分运行。在此基础上训练和测试多种路由模型，比较其相对于固定最佳候选、参考模型和逐输入oracle路由的性能与效率。

Result: 结果显示，路由具有明显的能力协同潜力：逐输入oracle路由相比最佳固定候选平均提升5.4分；学习型路由器在16个基准上相比最佳固定候选提升0.3分，在视频生成上提升2.1分，在机器人操作任务上提升1.1分，同时策略时间减少24%。与参考模型相比，路由平均将效率提升8.82倍。

Conclusion: 模型与策略路由可以作为构建通用AI系统的重要机制，通过动态组合异构候选者实现更高性能与更高效率。Route Anything为跨模态、跨任务路由提供了统一理论框架、大规模评测基准和实验证据，表明学习路由能够在不依赖单一全能模型的情况下推进面向AGI的能力编排。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) requires a method that can understand, generate, reason, and act across diverse domains and modalities. Rather than relying on a single model to master every capability, a promising path toward AGI now is to orchestrate a heterogeneous ecosystem of specialized models, tools, and policies (candidates) into a unified AI system. At the heart of this vision lies a fundamental question: how can an AI system dynamically select and coordinate the most capable candidate for each task while balancing performance and computational cost? In this work, we present Route Anything, a unified framework for studying model and policy routing across general-purpose AI systems. We develop a unified theoretical foundation that characterizes optimal routing under quality--cost trade-offs and establishes how the information available to a router determines its attainable performance. To systematically investigate routing across diverse capabilities, we first construct an omni-modal trajectory matrix spanning 25 benchmarks across language understanding, image, video, and speech generation, coding and workplace agents, and robotic manipulation, comprising 374,401 scored runs from 452 candidate configurations. Building on this framework, we train and test different routers to investigate the potential of omni-modal model routing, the capabilities of learned routers, and the conditions under which routing improves efficiency. Our results reveal substantial opportunities for capability orchestration: per-input oracle routing outperforms the best fixed candidate by 5.4 points, and learned routers outperform it by 0.3 points across 16 benchmarks, by 2.1 points on video generation, and by 1.1 points on robot manipulation while using 24% less policy time. More importantly, routing raises efficiency by 8.82x on average compared with the reference model.

</details>


### [102] [Möbius: A Unified Motion-Language Model Bridging Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05279)
*Jiazhi Shu,Chengfeng Zhao,Jinyi Wang,Yubo Zhao,Zhiyang Dou,Ronghui Li,Yu Lu,Xinwei Jiang,Zijiao Zeng,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Möbius，一个真正的统一人体运动模型，在单一框架内实现运动理解、生成与编辑；其核心是将扩散过程与多模态表征空间融合，直接在连续帧级原始运动上运行，并在HumanML3D上达到统一运动模型的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动统一多模态模型（UMM）仅实现部分统一：运动理解通常由语言模型完成，而运动生成交给基于离散化或预训练motion VAE潜变量空间的独立扩散模块，导致理解与生成之间表征割裂，难以形成真正端到端统一框架。

Method: 提出Möbius：将扩散过程嵌入与运动理解共享的多模态表示空间；clean motion、noisy motion和text共同参与共享注意力，并通过role-specific parameters分别处理理解流与生成流；模型直接在连续帧级原始运动上操作，无需离散tokenization，也无需预训练motion VAE，从而统一支持运动描述生成、文本到运动生成、时间补全和指令引导编辑。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，Möbius在统一运动模型中取得最优性能；扩展到1.7B参数和1,700小时运动数据后，仍表现出有效性与可扩展性，证明该框架可支持多种运动任务并在单一模型内联合处理理解与生成。

Conclusion: Möbius通过共享多模态表示空间和连续运动扩散实现真正统一的人体运动建模，避免了离散token化和独立VAE模块的复杂架构，为运动理解、生成、补全和编辑提供了简洁且可扩展的统一框架。

Abstract: We aim to build a truly unified motion model that can understand, generate, and edit human motion within a single framework. Existing motion UMMs remain only partially unified: motion understanding is handled by a language model, while generation is often delegated to a separate diffusion module operating over learned motion latents. We introduce Möbius, which brings the entire diffusion process into the same multimodal representation space used for motion understanding. Clean motion, noisy motion, and text jointly participate in shared attention, while role-specific parameters process understanding and generation streams. Importantly, Möbius operates directly on continuous frame-level raw motion, requiring neither discrete tokenization nor a pretrained motion VAE. This simple formulation naturally supports motion captioning, text-to-motion generation, temporal completion, and instruction-guided editing within a single model. On HumanML3D, Möbius achieves state-of-the-art performance among unified motion models. Scaling to 1.7B parameters and 1,700 hours of motion data further demonstrates that our framework is both effective and scalable.

</details>


### [103] [YANAMI: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Multiturn Stateful Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05280)
*Shengyu Wang,Tengyue Nong,Junming Liu,Haosong Peng,Siyu Li,Ruiqi Lei,Fushuo Huo,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Ding Wang,Nan Cheng,Haozhao Wang,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出YANAMI：首个面向多模态状态化交互的大规模对话数据集与基准，包含400K多轮多模态对话和400条评测对话，重点考察模型在多轮交互中理解、维护、更新并视觉化实现不断演变的对话状态的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型的数据集和基准仍以单轮任务为主，即便部分多轮数据集中各轮也往往可独立求解，缺少对跨轮信息跟踪、状态更新和视觉实现一致的监督与评测，难以反映模型在多模态状态化交互中的真实能力。

Method: 论文构建YANAMI，包括YANAMI-400K和YANAMI-Bench。YANAMI-400K包含400K多轮多模态对话、超过2M图像和2.6M对话轮次，覆盖多图融合、图像编辑、世界建模和视觉推理四类场景，要求模型持续跟踪、保存并修正跨轮演变的多模态对话状态。YANAMI-Bench包含400个多轮对话，联合评测对话状态理解和视觉实现能力，以刻画模型在状态理解与输出实现之间的差距。

Result: 实验表明，在YANAMI-400K上微调BAGEL模型后，其图像编辑、图像生成和多模态状态化交互性能分别提升10.3%、2.0%和13.23%，同时保留原有通用视觉理解能力。YANAMI-Bench评测进一步显示，现有统一多模态模型在维护、更新和视觉化实现演变中的多模态对话状态方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: YANAMI为统一多模态模型的多轮状态化交互提供了新的大规模训练与评测资源，揭示了当前模型在跨轮状态追踪和视觉忠实实现方面的关键短板，并证明针对性数据能够显著改善模型的多模态状态交互能力。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) integrate visual understanding, generation, and editing within a single unified framework. However, existing unified multimodal datasets and benchmarks remain dominated by single-turn tasks. Even in recent multi-turn datasets, many turns can be solved independently without tracking information across turns. Consequently, they provide limited supervision and evaluation for multimodal stateful interaction, which requires models to understand, maintain, update, and faithfully realize evolving dialogue states across turns. To address this gap, we introduce YANAMI, the first large-scale multimodal dialogue dataset and benchmark for stateful interaction, comprising YANAMI-400K and YANAMI-Bench. YANAMI-400K contains 400K multi-turn multimodal dialogues, with more than 2M images and 2.6M dialogue turns. The dialogues span four representative scenarios: Multi-Image Fusion, Image Editing, World Modeling, and Visual Reasoning, and require models to continuously track, preserve, and revise evolving multimodal dialogue states across turns. YANAMI-Bench comprises 400 multi-turn dialogues for systematically evaluating multimodal stateful interaction. It jointly evaluates Dialogue State Understanding and Visual Realization, characterizing the gap between models' ability to understand evolving dialogue states and their ability to faithfully realize them in visual outputs. Experiments show that fine-tuning BAGEL on YANAMI-400K improves image editing, generation, and multimodal stateful interaction by 10.3%, 2.0%, and 13.23%, respectively, while preserving its general visual understanding capabilities. Evaluations on YANAMI-Bench further reveal that existing UMMs still struggle to maintain, update, and visually realize evolving multimodal dialogue states.

</details>


### [104] [Low-Density 3D Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05291)
*Ahmed Baha Ben Jmaa,Faten Chaieb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LD-PointNet++，用于低密度3D点云分类；并系统评测了现有先进方法在不同点云密度下的性能，表明该方法在低密度场景更优且在ModelNet上不同密度下准确率稳定超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中3D点云密度常因传感器限制和环境变化而下降，导致已有密集点云分类方法性能受损，因此需要专门提升低密度点云分类鲁棒性的研究。

Method: 首先构建跨密度条件的基准评测，系统比较现有先进点云分类方法；随后提出针对低密度点云优化分类性能的网络架构LD-PointNet++，并在ModelNet数据集上验证。

Result: 实验显示现有方法在低密度点云分类中出现显著性能下降；LD-PointNet++在低密度场景优于对比方法，并在不同密度水平下保持一致的准确率且超过90%。

Conclusion: 低密度点云分类是实际部署中的重要挑战，LD-PointNet++能够更稳健地应对密度变化，适用于AR/VR、机器人和自动驾驶等现实应用。

Abstract: 3D point cloud classification, a task in computer vision, has recently gained substantial interest due to its extensive applications across various domains such as augmented and virtual reality, robotics, and autonomous driving. However, classifying 3D point clouds becomes challenging in real-world scenarios where the density levels vary. These variations are due to limitations inherent to current sensing technologies and variable environmental conditions, which often prevent the capture of detailed and comprehensive geometric representations. Existing classification methods, which have demonstrated impressive results on dense point clouds, may be affected and could experience reduced performance when dealing with low-density point clouds. Addressing this challenge, this paper introduces LD-PointNet++, an architecture optimized for the classification of low-density point clouds. To pave the way for LD-PointNet++, we conduct the first benchmarking study that systematically evaluates state-of-the-art point cloud classification methods across varying densities, revealing significant performance declines in low-density environments. Experimental results on the ModelNet benchmark dataset demonstrate that LD-PointNet++ not only outperforms these methods in low-density scenarios but also demonstrates consistent accuracy above 90% across different density levels.

</details>


### [105] [Wonder 4: A Mixture-of-Experts Recipe for Image Quality Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05294)
*Yunan Wu,Bin Sun,Partha Acharjee,Xiaoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Wonder 4，一种面向真实图像质量增强的混合专家模型，能够同时处理模糊、噪声、压缩伪影、划痕等多种退化，并自适应地将不同修复操作分配到图像中相应的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实照片往往同时存在多种图像退化，而且每种退化在图像中的强度和空间位置都不同。传统方法通常用单一描述或统一修复策略处理整幅图像，难以区分不同缺陷应在哪个区域修正，导致修复操作之间相互冲突。

Method: Wonder 4 在模型每个块中设置 N=13 个专家，并由训练得到的路由器为每个图像 patch 动态选择 top-k=5 个专家进行处理。该方法不依赖退化分类器、掩码、提示词、检测器或任务标签。针对标准混合专家训练中“各专家负载均衡使用”的问题，作者提出一种仅设置最低使用份额的偏置机制：使用不足的专家会获得更高路由器分数以被重新选中，而超过最低份额的专家不会被强制均衡使用，从而让不同专家更自然地对应不同修复任务。该机制不增加额外损失、额外模块或额外处理步骤。

Result: 在 DIV2K 和 Flickr2K 数据集上，与五种已发表方法和三个开源权重处理流程进行比较。Wonder 4 在 DIV2K ×4、DIV2K ×8 和 Flickr2K ×4 三个测试设置上均取得最优的 LPIPS、DISTS 和 NIQE 指标。

Conclusion: Wonder 4 通过区域级专家路由和 relaxed load-balancing 机制，实现了无需显式退化标签或掩码的多类图像质量联合增强，并在感知质量和无参考质量指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: A real photograph is often blurry, grainy, compressed, scratched, and low-resolution at the same time. Each defect has its own severity and its own place in the image. The corrections for these defects conflict with each other. Exist- ing models describe the whole image with one description, so they cannot say where each correction belongs. To solve this problem, we present Wonder 4, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model for image quality enhancement. It corrects several defects of a real photograph at the same time, each in the region where it occurs. Every block of the model holds a mixture of N = 13 experts. A router, trained with the model, sends every image patch to k = 5 of them. No degradation classifier, mask, prompt, detector, or task label is used. Another issue is that standard MoE training, taken from language models, pushes every expert toward equal use. Equal use applies every correction everywhere, even where it is not needed. We replace this rule with a bias that only sets a minimum share of use. Across all patches, an expert used less than this minimum gets higher router scores until it is selected again. An expert above the minimum is not changed, so no expert is forced to be used as often as the others. The bias needs no extra loss. The method adds no extra module and no extra processing step. We evaluate Wonder 4 on DIV2K and Flickr2K, against five published methods and three open-weight pipelines. It reaches the best LPIPS, DISTS, and NIQE on all three test settings (DIV2K x4, DIV2K x8, and Flickr2K x4).

</details>


### [106] [GSCV++: Lightweight 3D Gaussian Splatting Sequence Compression via Standard Video Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05359)
*Qi Yang,Yingzhan Xu,Kai Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSCV++，一种面向3D高斯泼溅序列压缩的轻量级流水线，通过标准视频编解码器实现低复杂度、硬件友好的压缩，并在联合优化预处理、3D到2D映射和实际视频编码配置后取得显著率失真增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅序列数据量大，工业应用要求压缩过程具有低计算复杂度和硬件兼容性。现有基于视频编解码器的3D高斯泼溅压缩方法在预处理、2D映射和编码配置方面仍有优化空间。

Method: GSCV++联合改进3D高斯泼溅序列压缩的三个阶段：（1）提出能量自适应帧间球谐交流降维方法EA-SHDR，以生成紧凑稳定的时域SH AC特征空间并减少冗余；（2）设计基于先验的Inter-MiniPLAS，即P-MiniPLAS，通过先验加权因子推断和小尺度重映射生成更平滑、更适合视频编码的2D特征图；（3）提出内容感知通用压缩CA-GC，采用非均匀位深划分生成坐标图，并引入编码单元尺寸选择以高效控制压缩复杂度，在不做规范性改动的前提下充分发挥视频编解码器性能。

Result: 实验表明，相比SOTA方法，GSCV++在帧内预测下获得超过20%、在帧间预测下获得超过60%的率失真增益，同时在使用同类型视频编解码器的方法中具有更快的2D图生成、编码和解码速度。消融实验验证了各模块的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GSCV++通过联合优化预处理、3D到2D映射和实际视频编码配置，在不改变视频编解码规范的情况下显著提升了3D高斯泼溅序列压缩效率，并保持了较低复杂度和硬件友好性，为后续研究提供了可行方向。

Abstract: This paper presents GSCV++, a lightweight pipeline for 3D {G}aussian {S}platting (GS) sequences {C}ompression with standard {V}ideo codecs. To meet the requirements of low-complexity and hardware compatibility from the industry, GSCV++ jointly improves preprocessing, 3D-to-2D mapping, and practical video compression configuration of GS compression. First, for preprocessing, energy-adaptive inter-frame spherical harmonic (SH) alternative current (AC) dimensionality reduction (EA-SHDR) is proposed to produces a compact and stable temporal SH AC feature space. It can reduce the data volume of following mapping and practical compression, as well as less complexity while eliminating redundant information. Second, for 3D-to-2D mapping, prior-based Inter-MiniPLAS (P-MiniPLAS) is designed to generate smooth 2D feature maps through prior-based weighting factor inference and small scale remapping. Compared with vanilla Parallel Linear Assignment Sorting (PLAS) and Morton scan, P-MiniPLAS ensures the generated 2D maps are more suitable for the video codec while maintaining a low level of computational complexity. Third, for practical compression, content-aware generalized compression (CA-GC) is developed to use a non-uniform bit-depth split for coordinate map generation, and a coding unit size selection is introduced for efficient compression complexity control, which fully exploits the performance of video codecs without involving normative changes. Experiments demonstrate that GSCV++ reports over 20\% in intra- and 60\% in inter-prediction rate-distortion gains compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. GSCV++ also achieves faster 2D map generation, encoding, and decoding than other SOTA methods using the same type of video codecs. Ablation studies verify the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed modules and provide insights for subsequent research.

</details>


### [107] [Visibility-Aware Reference Generation for Benchmarking Visual 3D Reconstruction in Vegetation-Rich Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05361)
*Zhipeng Zhong,Matthew Coombes,Cunjia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 LiDAR 的参考数据生成流程，用于在植被丰富的户外场景中零样本评估前馈视觉几何模型和传统 SfM 方法的视觉 3D 重建与深度估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被密集的户外环境中缺乏可靠的几何真值，完整扫描成本高，导致视觉 3D 重建和深度估计难以获得稳定、可靠的基准评估。

Method: 利用现有多传感器定位数据集中的相机图像、LiDAR 点云和参考位姿，生成逐视角深度图和可见参考点云；针对累积 LiDAR 扫描中的可见性误差，提出 LargeDepth-Gap Visibility Score（LDGVS），用于逐点可见性过滤，并自动选择 Hidden Point Removal（HPR）的尺度。

Result: 在 TartanAirV2 和 TartanGround 上实验表明，相比适合完整扫描数据的 Poisson 表面重建，HPR 与 LDGVS 联合过滤在相近绝对相对误差下可保留约三倍点密度；将该流程用于 BotanicGarden 数据集后，可系统比较深度估计和 3D 重建方法，发现前馈方法能快速生成稠密深度，但 3D 重建可能出现重复结构，而传统 SfM 几何更一致，但重建稀疏且在高植被场景中更容易失败。

Conclusion: 该 LiDAR 参考生成流程能够为植被丰富户外场景中的视觉几何方法提供可复用、自动化的基准评估工具，并有效揭示前馈模型与传统 SfM 在深度估计和 3D 重建中的不同优劣。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of visual 3D reconstruction and depth estimation in vegetation-rich outdoor environments is limited by the scarcity of geometric ground-truth data and the cost of complete scanner surveys. We present a LiDARbased reference-generation pipeline for evaluating the zero-shot performance of feed-forward visual-geometry models alongside conventional structure-from-motion (SfM) methods. The pipeline reuses camera images, LiDAR scans, and reference poses from existing multi-sensor localization datasets to generate per-view depth maps and visible reference point clouds. To address visibility errors in accumulated LiDAR scans, we introduce the LargeDepth-Gap Visibility Score (LDGVS) for point-wise visibility filtering and automatic scale selection in Hidden Point Removal(HPR). Experiments on TartanAirV2 and TartanGround show that, compared with Poisson surface reconstruction, which is an approach suited to completed scan surveys, the combined HPR and LDGVS filtering retains approximately three times the point density at comparable absolute relative error. We also apply the pipeline to the BotanicGarden dataset to systematically compare depth estimation and 3D reconstruction methods. Feedforward methods produce dense depth predictions efficiently, but their 3D reconstructions can contain duplicated structures. Conventional SfM generally produces more coherent geometry, but its reconstructions remain sparse and more prone to failure in challenging vegetated scenes. The benchmark toolkit and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [108] [DreamX: Video Diffusion Model is a Universal Latent Action Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05377)
*Jisoo Kim,Chaehyun Kim,Junsu Kim,Jiho Park,Seongchan Kim,Eunbeen Hong,Soowon Son,Fu-En Yang,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX 利用预训练视频扩散模型学习紧凑且解耦外观的通用潜在动作表示，实现跨本体、跨环境和跨动作参数化的动作迁移与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型常将动作与视觉变化纠缠，难以在不同身体形态和外观条件下泛化，因此需要一种一致且对具身差异不变的通用动作表示。

Method: DreamX 将预训练视频扩散模型作为潜在动作学习器，从其时空特征中提取紧凑动作码，将每帧潜在视频从约 1200 tokens 压缩到 4 tokens，并借助生成先验分离动作相关动态与外观变化，形成共享动作接口。

Result: 在 LARY-Bench 上取得潜在动作模型中的领先性能；该潜空间支持任意到任意视频迁移、跨具身和跨环境的零样本动作迁移，并能用于低成本视频扩散策略学习。

Conclusion: DreamX 证明了视频扩散模型的时空表征可用于学习紧凑、解耦且可迁移的通用动作表示，为跨具身控制与高效策略学习提供了统一接口。

Abstract: Learning a universal action representation from video requires modeling dynamics that are consistent across different executions of the same action yet invariant to changes in embodiment and appearance. Existing latent action models infer actions from visual transitions, but their representations often entangle the underlying action with irrelevant visual variations, limiting transfer across embodiments. We introduce DreamX, which repurposes a pretrained video diffusion model as a universal latent action learner. Our approach extracts compact latent actions from the video model's rich spatiotemporal features, compressing each latent video frame by $\boldsymbol{300\times}$ ($\boldsymbol{1{,}200 \rightarrow 4}$ tokens). In addition, by leveraging the video model's generative prior, these latent actions capture action-relevant dynamics disentangled from visual appearance. The resulting latent space serves as a shared action interface across embodiments (human and robot), environments, and action parameterizations. On LARY-Bench, DreamX achieves state-of-the-art performance among latent action models. Moreover, actions encoded in one setting remain effective in another: the latent space supports any-to-any video transfer and zero-shot action transfer across diverse embodiments and environments. Furthermore, this shared interface also enables policy learning in a compact latent action space, reducing the computational cost of video-diffusion-based policies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Say the Same, Act Differently: Diagnosing and Steering Text-Action Divergence in Reasoning VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04166)
*Zihao Feng,Qingzhao Zhang,Chunyu Xia,Bo Yu,Z. Morley Mao,Ramesh Govindan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了一种“文本中性动作子空间”（TNAS）现象与攻击方法：在推理型视觉-语言-动作模型中，视觉扰动可以改变机器人或车辆的动作，同时让自然语言推理/理由文本几乎逐字保持不变。因此，只看生成的理由文本无法保证物理动作安全，安全监控必须直接校验动作输出通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型视觉-语言-动作模型通过生成自然语言理由来提高可解释性和安全可监控性。已有攻击通常破坏理由文本本身，容易被人工或文本检查发现。该工作想证明更隐蔽的安全威胁：攻击者可以通过视觉输入扰动改变模型的实际动作，同时让监督者看到的理由文本保持不变或基本不变，从而使“理由安全”与“动作安全”脱钩。

Method: 作者在两类 VLA 模型上研究共享文本/动作接口：用于驾驶的 Alpamayo 和用于操作控制的 InstructVLA。核心是提出 TNAS 诊断，识别对动作最敏感但与文本损失梯度几乎正交的方向。比较该方向扰动与文本梯度方向扰动在相同隐藏状态扰动范数下的效果：前者主要改变动作，后者主要改变文本。进一步将该方向用于引导视觉扰动攻击，包括全图像扰动和局部前视相机图像块扰动，在 154 个带障碍物标注的驾驶场景中评估能否在保持理由文本逐字不变的同时诱发新碰撞，并在 InstructVLA 上评估文本保持率、文本漂移和动作块偏移。

Result: TNAS 诊断显示，局部最动作敏感方向与文本损失梯度方向高度正交，在 Alpamayo 和 InstructVLA 上的范数重叠分别仅为 1.87% 和 5.28%。在相同隐藏状态扰动强度下，沿文本中性残差方向干预使动作偏移达到文本梯度扰动造成的 4.64 到 47.8 倍，而文本梯度扰动造成的文本漂移则是其 21.2 到 277 倍。将视觉扰动沿 TNAS 方向引导后，也能从图像层面复现这种动作与文本解耦。在 154 个 Alpamayo 驾驶场景中，TNAS 引导攻击能在理由文本逐字不变的情况下，使 52.6% 的场景产生新碰撞；若仅扰动局部前视相机图像块，比例为 35.1%，而无引导动作攻击仅为 4.55%。与文本保持基线相比，TNAS 在相近碰撞率下将理由逐字保持率从 83.0% 提升到 89.5%，并将 teacher-forced 文本漂移降低 68.4%。在 InstructVLA 中，TNAS 引导将文本漂移降低 40.5%，在 85.0% 样本上保持精确响应，同时产生 1.73 单位的动作块偏移。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，推理型 VLA 模型中“理由文本安全”不能等价于“物理动作安全”。即使模型生成的理由完全不变，输入视觉扰动也可能导致危险动作。因此，面向 VLA 的安全监控不能只依赖自然语言推理文本，而必须直接验证动作输出、控制指令或执行通道，并考虑利用视觉输入扰动诱导的隐蔽式动作攻击风险。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models pair physical control with natural-language rationales that supervisors can read to monitor embodied safety. Prior attacks on VLA reasoning corrupt the rationale itself and leave visible evidence in the text. We show that visual perturbations can instead alter the physical action while keeping the generated rationale word-for-word identical. At the shared text/action interface of Alpamayo for driving and InstructVLA for manipulation, our text-neutral action-subspace (TNAS) diagnostic shows that the locally most action-sensitive direction is nearly orthogonal to the active text-loss gradients, with 1.87% and 5.28% norm overlap. At equal hidden-state perturbation norms across both models, interventions along this text-neutral residual shift actions 4.64x to 47.8x more than text-gradient perturbations, whereas text-gradient perturbations induce 21.2x to 277x more text drift. Steering visual perturbations along this direction reaches the same decoupling from the image. Across 154 obstacle-annotated driving scenes in Alpamayo, TNAS-guided attacks induce a new collision under a word-for-word identical rationale in 52.6% of scenes under full-image perturbations and 35.1% under a localized forward-camera patch, compared with 4.55% for unguided action attacks. Relative to a text-preserving baseline, TNAS guidance raises exact rationale preservation from 83.0% to 89.5% and cuts teacher-forced text drift by 68.4% at a comparable collision rate. In InstructVLA, TNAS guidance cuts text drift by 40.5% and preserves the exact response on 85.0% of examples alongside a 1.73-unit action-chunk shift. An unchanged rationale therefore cannot certify a safe action, so monitors for reasoning VLAs must verify the predicted action channel directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [CoReMatch: Recurrent Continuous Coordinate Refinement for Real-Time Robotic Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03932)
*Robert J. Wang,Charles Ling,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出CoReMatch，一种在连续坐标空间中循环优化对应关系的特征匹配框架，以突破传统半稠密匹配依赖固定网格和离散置信图的限制，实现更精确、高效且适合嵌入式机器人感知的实时匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位、建图、SLAM和自主导航等机器人感知任务依赖特征匹配。现有半稠密匹配器通常在固定网格上预测置信度，导致对应关系只能落在预设位置，难以精确定位；若要提高精度，需要高分辨率网格，但会带来显著计算开销。因此，需要在不依赖密集离散网格的情况下，实现高精度、高效率且可扩展的连续坐标匹配。

Method: CoReMatch采用循环式坐标精化框架，直接在连续坐标空间中更新对应点位置，而不是预测离散置信图。其核心思想是通过多轮迭代逐步优化匹配坐标。在粗匹配阶段，Deep Aggregated Transformer负责聚合全局上下文信息，并迭代更新对应坐标；随后，一个轻量级回归头对坐标进行局部精修，以进一步提升定位精度。该方法将全局上下文建模与连续坐标回归结合，实现高效精化。

Result: 在室内和室外基准数据集上的实验表明，CoReMatch在精度与效率之间取得了良好平衡。与Efficient LoFTR相比，CoReMatch在匹配精度上更优，同时在嵌入式边缘硬件上的运行速度提高10倍以上，说明其更适合实时机器人感知应用。

Conclusion: 循环坐标精化是一种有效且可扩展的特征匹配范式，能够绕过固定网格和高分辨率置信图带来的计算瓶颈。CoReMatch通过连续空间中的迭代优化和全局上下文聚合，实现了更精确、更高效的实时特征匹配，为嵌入式平台上的机器人视觉感知提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Feature matching underpins many robotic perception tasks, including visual localization, mapping, SLAM, and autonomous navigation. Existing semi-dense matchers predict confidence on fixed grids, limiting correspondences to predefined locations and requiring costly high-resolution grids for precise localization. We present CoReMatch (Coordinate Refinement Match), a recurrent framework that refines correspondences directly in continuous coordinate space rather than predicting discrete confidence maps. CoReMatch progressively improves correspondence estimates by propagating information across iterations. At the coarse stage, a Deep Aggregated Transformer captures global context and iteratively updates correspondence coordinates, while a lightweight regression head further sharpens their localization. Experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks show that CoReMatch strikes a strong balance between accuracy and efficiency, outperforming Efficient LoFTR in accuracy while running more than 10x faster on embedded edge hardware. These results demonstrate that recurrent coordinate refinement offers an efficient and scalable approach to real-time feature matching for robotic perception.

</details>


### [111] [RAFT: Reasoning-Aligned Fine-Tuning Improves Robustness of VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04368)
*Shresth Grover,Andrey Kolobov,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAFT：一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的两阶段后训练方法，先在任务相关推理标注上训练低秩适配器，再冻结适配器并用动作演示微调策略，以在有限演示下同时提升任务成功率和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 VLA 策略在目标任务上用少量演示直接微调虽可提高正常成功率，但容易降低对外观、物体和指令变化的鲁棒性；需要一种能保留通用推理能力、同时适配任务动作的少样本后训练机制。

Method: 提出 reasoning-aligned fine-tuning（RAFT），采用两阶段训练：第一阶段在任务相关推理标注上训练低秩适配器（LoRA），使模型对齐与任务相关的推理表示；第二阶段冻结该适配器，仅基于动作演示对策略进行微调，从而减少动作数据对预训练推理知识的破坏。实验还包括真机 DROID 机器人、双臂 YAM 多阶段任务、模拟抓取放置和开抽屉任务，并通过注意力分布和线性探针分析验证表征变化。

Result: RAFT 在物理 DROID 机器人上实现 75.0% 的分布外任务成功率，显著高于普通微调的 27.9%；在多阶段双臂 YAM 任务上达到 70.0% 的平均成功率，高于最强基线的 36.0%。在模拟任务的视觉、物体和指令偏移设置下，RAFT 也提升了成功率。分析表明，相比普通微调，RAFT 策略更关注标注的任务区域，并在较深层中对物体身份具有更高线性探针准确率；更广泛的推理数据混合还能在不同演示预算下进一步提升下游成功率。

Conclusion: RAFT 通过“先对齐推理、后适配动作”的解耦式后训练，有效缓解了 VLA 策略在少样本任务微调中鲁棒性下降的问题，在真实机器人和模拟环境中均显著提升分布外泛化性能，为低演示预算下的可靠 VLA 策略适配提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Adapting a generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a target task with limited demonstrations can improve nominal task success while reducing robustness to variations in appearance, objects, and instructions. We introduce reasoning-aligned fine-tuning (RAFT), a two-stage post-training procedure: RAFT first trains low-rank adapters on task-relevant reasoning annotations, then freezes them while fine-tuning the policy on action demonstrations. On a physical DROID robot, RAFT achieves $75.0\%$ out-of-distribution success, compared with $27.9\%$ for plain fine-tuning; on a multi-stage bimanual YAM task, it reaches $70.0\%$ mean success, compared with $36.0\%$ for the strongest baseline. RAFT also improves success under visual, object, and instruction shifts in simulated pick-and-place and open-drawer tasks. Compared with plain fine-tuning, RAFT policies place more attention on annotated task regions and yield higher linear-probe accuracy for object identity in later layers. A broader reasoning-data mixture further improves downstream success across demonstration budgets.

</details>


### [112] [Trusting Imagined Futures: Auditing Imagined Rewards for VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04873)
*Zicheng Kong,Zhenbo Xu,Haoyan Zhang,Jinghan Yang,Zhenzhen Li,Yiwei Ru,Liuyu Xiang,WanTing Xu,Min Ren,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TIF（Trusting Imagined Futures），一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型离线后训练的监督样本选择方法；它通过真实执行反馈审核视频世界模型预测出的奖励，避免采纳“想象奖励高但实际执行质量差”的候选轨迹，从而提升行为克隆训练的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型可以在“想象”中评估候选动作并预测其奖励，但仅依赖预测奖励会导致模型偏好高奖励却执行质量差的轨迹。对于 VLA 离线后训练，如果错误地选择这些轨迹作为监督信号，行为克隆会被低质量示范误导。因此，需要一种机制利用真实执行反馈来审核和过滤预测奖励，提高所选监督轨迹的可靠性。

Method: 作者提出 TIF，将候选轨迹筛选与预测奖励审核结合。首先，一个多维奖励模型为候选轨迹预测一个用于排序的效用分数，并预测五个与执行质量相关的维度。其次，审计器利用奖励预测、预测不确定性，以及想象视频与真实执行之间的配对信息，识别模型对奖励的高估模式。在监督选择阶段，TIF 只有在候选轨迹的预测效用相对于已有轨迹的优势超过给定 margin，并且其审计风险低于阈值时，才替换原有监督；否则保留原轨迹。最终，被保留的真实轨迹用于行为克隆（behavior cloning）训练。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件和三个训练种子上，TIF 的平均成功率为 76.25%，相比直接使用已有轨迹训练提升 3.00 个百分点，而轨迹数量和训练预算保持不变。若固定奖励模型和优势 margin，仅增加审计门控，还能进一步提升 1.47 个百分点。实验还显示，该增益在与 advantage ranking 和 logistic regression risk selection 的匹配替换数量比较中仍然成立。在三个真实机器人任务的录制视频中，TIF 将所选轨迹中的 clean completion 从 58.33% 提升到 70.00%（基于 60 个候选池）。

Conclusion: TIF 表明，对视频世界模型生成的想象未来进行真实执行反馈审计，是提升 VLA 离线监督选择质量的有效途径。它不是简单采纳预测奖励最高的候选，而是同时考虑相对优势和执行质量风险，从而减少高估奖励带来的错误监督选择。该方法在仿真基准和真实机器人视频评估中都能提升成功率和轨迹执行质量，说明在同样数据量和训练成本下，通过审核与门控优化监督选择可以带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Video world models enable candidate actions to be evaluated through imagined outcomes, but high predicted rewards can favor trajectories with poor execution quality. We introduce Trusting Imagined Futures (TIF), an offline supervision selection method for vision-language-action (VLA) post-training that audits predicted rewards using execution feedback. A multidimensional reward model predicts a utility score for candidate ranking and five dimensions of execution quality. An auditor uses reward predictions and uncertainty to identify overestimation patterns learned from paired imagined videos and real executions. TIF accepts the candidate with the highest predicted utility when its advantage over the existing trajectory exceeds a margin and its audit risk passes a threshold; otherwise, it retains the existing supervision. The retained real trajectories then supervise behavior cloning. Across four LIBERO suites and three training seeds, TIF achieves a mean success rate of 76.25%, improving over training on the existing trajectories by 3.00 percentage points with the same trajectory count and training budget. Adding audit gating yields 1.47 percentage points with the reward model and advantage margin held fixed. The gains persist against advantage ranking and logistic regression risk selection at matched replacement counts. On recorded videos from three real robot tasks, TIF increases clean completion among selected trajectories from 58.33% to 70.00% across 60 candidate pools.

</details>


### [113] [Do Agentic Policies Need to Look at Every Step? Geometric Abstraction for Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05058)
*Yihang Liu,Yiming Chen,Mingzhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出用 3D 几何抽象的文本/符号接口替代机器人策略中反复调用多模态视觉推理：将 RGB-D 观测编译为带类型、证据感知的符号场景表示，并通过紧凑文本检查点和度量残差供语言模型读取，从而提升多步操作完成度并显著减少视觉查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态语言模型可直接推理机器人场景，但每一步交互都重复解释原始视觉观测成本高、不稳定且冗余。作者希望把常规空间推理从图像转移到文本和符号层面，使场景几何状态成为显式、可复用、可被语言模型直接读取的“认知基底”，只在必要时请求视觉模型复核。

Method: 提出一个以 3D 几何抽象为核心的机器人推理接口：使用经标定的 RGB-D 观测将物体部件局部更新为带类型和证据置信度的符号引用；每次动作后输出紧凑的文本检查点和度量残差，用于反馈误差并重绑定当前场景；上层语言模型基于这些 grounded text/symbols 规划与决策，仅按需调用直接视觉推理。方法覆盖离线策略合成和连续在线执行。

Result: 在离线策略合成和在线连续执行中，基于已定位文本与符号的场景表示进行多步操作推理，比反复直接视觉推理提高了多步操作任务完成率，并大幅减少多模态视觉查询次数，说明显式几何状态能有效支持 agentic robot policies。

Conclusion: 该工作证明显式 3D 几何状态可以作为语言模型机器人策略的高效中间表示：将视觉观测转换为文本化、符号化、可局部更新且包含误差信息的场景抽象，有助于复用任务结构、稳定决策，并降低昂贵多模态推理的调用频率。

Abstract: Recent multimodal language models can reason directly about robot scenes, but it is unclear whether they need to repeatedly reinterpret raw visual observations at every step of an interaction. We propose shifting routine spatial reasoning from images to text and symbols through a 3D geometric abstraction that is refreshed locally from observations and read directly by the language models. Specifically, our interface represents scene geometry via typed, evidence-aware symbolic references to object parts, grounds them from calibrated RGB-D, and returns compact textual checkpoints with metric residuals after each action. Symbolic task structure can thus be reused across decisions while metric values are rebound to the current scene, with direct visual reasoning invoked only on demand. Across offline policy synthesis and continuous online execution, reasoning over grounded text and symbols improves multi-step manipulation completion while substantially reducing repeated multimodal queries, positioning explicit geometric state as an effective substrate for agentic robot policies.

</details>


### [114] [Trajectory Evaluation via Rollout-Free World Model for End-to-end Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05072)
*Junzhe Wang,Jinqing Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RFWorld，一种免 rollout 的端到端轨迹评估世界模型，通过共享场景记忆和时间/空间读出机制，以更低推理成本实现候选轨迹比较，并在 NAVSIM 和 Bench2Drive 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型候选轨迹评估器通常通过预测每条候选轨迹条件下的未来状态（rollout）实现未来感知打分，但 rollout 推理代价高，且递归预测会累积误差。作者认为规划评估不需要重建完整未来场景，只需要提取与未来相关的可比信息。

Method: RFWorld 首先构建共享场景记忆，并通过训练阶段轻量级的 temporal readout 和语义监督，将场景记忆塑造成可被未来状态读取的表示。推理阶段，候选轨迹不再执行递归 rollout，而是通过 sparse spatial readout 直接查询共享记忆，获得用于轨迹打分的未来相关信息，从而减少约“规划时间步数 × 候选轨迹数量”倍的计算量。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 和 Bench2Drive 实验上，RFWorld 学习到更利于规划的世界表示，并达到当前最先进的端到端规划/轨迹评估性能。

Conclusion: 端到端轨迹评估可以不依赖昂贵的显式 rollout，而通过共享场景记忆与稀疏读出机制获取未来相关信息，在降低推理成本和避免预测误差累积的同时提升规划精度。

Abstract: Although existing candidate trajectory evaluators have substantially improved end-to-end planning accuracy, their underlying mechanism remains suboptimal. Model-based evaluators typically make scoring future-aware by explicitly predicting candidate-conditioned future states over the planning horizon, which we term rollout. However, rollout incurs high inference cost and accumulates prediction errors. We argue that planning does not require reconstructing a full future scene for each candidate, but only future-relevant information for reliable comparison. We therefore propose RFWorld, a Rollout-Free World model for end-to-end trajectory evaluation. RFWorld constructs a shared scene memory and shapes it into a future-readable representation via lightweight training-time temporal readout with semantic supervision, keeping training overhead low. At inference time, candidate trajectories directly query the shared memory via sparse spatial readout for scoring, reducing computation by roughly the planning horizon times the number of candidate trajectories, while avoiding recursive rollout errors. Experiments on NAVSIM and Bench2Drive show that RFWorld learns a more planning-aligned world representation and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>
